AI01匹配 / Matching

审阅背景，不属于学生题面 / Reviewer context, not part of the student question

AI、生成式AI、聊天界面与提示词的关系

生成式AI创建新内容；聊天机器人提供自然语言交互界面；提示词是引导系统的输入。需要区分能力、界面和输入所承担的不同角色。

本题保留考察：评估四个具体服务组成部分与分类、内容生成、对话界面和引导性输入这些不同角色之间的对应关系，同时避免混淆界面与生成内容的系统。

Relationships among AI, generative AI, chatbots and prompts

Generative AI creates new content; a chatbot provides a natural-language interface; a prompt is an input guiding the system. Distinguish the roles of capability, interface and instruction.

Retained judgment: Evaluate the correspondence between four concrete service components and the distinct roles of classification, content generation, conversational interface, and guiding input, while avoiding confusion between the interface and the system producing content.

来源 / Source: Video 1.1 ·

某大学帮助服务将请求标记为招生、费用或住宿，拟写回复，并在对话窗口中显示这些回复。一名学生输入：“请礼貌回复，少于80个词，并附上住宿办公室的电子邮件地址。”分类是指将某个项目归入现有类别。请将每个组成部分与其最恰当的角色相匹配。每个选项使用一次。该服务的技术架构未作说明。

备选项

C1在预定义的结果中进行选择；它并不撰写回复。
C2生成回复的措辞，而不只是对请求进行归类。
C3支持自然语言交流；其背后的技术未作说明。
C4通过用户提供的要求引导生成期望的输出。

待匹配内容

R1应用三个请求标签之一
R2撰写一份此前未提供过的回复
R3交换消息的窗口
R4学生关于语气、长度和必需信息的句子

整题完全匹配计分，不计部分分。 每个选项最多使用一次。

A university help service labels requests as admissions, fees, or housing and drafts replies shown in a conversational window. A student types, “Reply politely in under 80 words and include the housing-office email.” Classification means assigning an item to an existing category. Match each component to its best role. Use each option once. The service’s technical architecture is unspecified.

Choices

C1Chooses among predefined outcomes; it does not compose the response.
C2Produces the response wording rather than merely sorting the request.
C3Supports the natural-language exchange; what powers it is unspecified.
C4Directs the desired output through user-supplied requirements.

Rows to match

R1Applying one of the three request labels
R2Writing a reply not previously supplied
R3The window where messages are exchanged
R4The student’s sentence about tone, length, and required information

Whole-item exact match; no partial credit. Use each option at most once.

参考答案与解释 / Reference answer and explanation

R1 → C1 (在预定义的结果中进行选择；它并不撰写回复。); R2 → C2 (生成回复的措辞，而不只是对请求进行归类。); R3 → C3 (支持自然语言交流；其背后的技术未作说明。); R4 → C4 (通过用户提供的要求引导生成期望的输出。)

R1属于分类，因为它分配的是现有类别。R2属于生成，因为它创建了新文本。R3是对话界面；由于架构未作说明，不能将其认定为生成草稿的系统。R4是提示词，因为学生的要求引导了输出。

R1 → C1 (Chooses among predefined outcomes; it does not compose the response.); R2 → C2 (Produces the response wording rather than merely sorting the request.); R3 → C3 (Supports the natural-language exchange; what powers it is unspecified.); R4 → C4 (Directs the desired output through user-supplied requirements.)

R1 is classification because it assigns an existing category. R2 is generation because it creates new text. R3 is the conversational interface; the unspecified architecture means it cannot be identified as the draft-producing system. R4 is the prompt because the student's requirements guide the output.

五阶段设计记录 / Five-stage design record

步骤1：表现目标

识别具体服务的多个组成部分如何分别对应广义人工智能、生成式功能、交互界面和提示词。

给出多个“组成部分—作用”对应关系，其中包括一种可能出现的界面与模型混淆。不得用单一的整体服务方案比较取代这些不同的对应关系。

Stage 1: performance claim and observable evidence

Identify how several components of a concrete service correspond to broad AI, generative functions, an interaction interface and prompts.

Several component-to-role correspondences, including a plausible confusion between interface and model. Do not replace these distinct mappings with a single overall service-plan comparison.

步骤2：情境与开放任务

某大学帮助服务使用软件将每项请求标记为招生、费用或住宿；拟写新的回复；并在对话消息窗口中显示草稿。一名学生输入：“请礼貌回复，少于80个词，并附上住宿办公室的电子邮件地址。”工作人员会在发送前审核每份草稿。

为服务经理创建一份简明的组成部分对应图。对于每个案例要素，识别其在更广泛的AI服务中的角色，并论证这种对应关系。明确区分请求标记能力、内容创建功能、对话界面和用户指令。还要解释消息窗口与生成草稿的系统之间一种可能出现的混淆。

Stage 2: scenario and open task

A university help service uses software to label each request as admissions, fees, or housing; draft a new reply; and display the draft in a conversational message window. A student types, “Reply politely in under 80 words and include the housing-office email.” Staff review every draft before sending.

Create a concise component map for the service manager. For each case element, identify its role in the broader AI service and justify the mapping. Explicitly distinguish the request-labelling capability, content-creating function, conversational interface, and user instruction. Also explain one likely confusion between the message window and the system producing the draft.

GRASPS

目标: 制作一份站得住脚的服务各项AI相关组成部分对应图。

角色: 初级服务分析师

受众: 大学帮助服务经理

情境: 经理将整个服务描述为一个聊天机器人。

GRASPS

goal: Produce a defensible map of the service’s distinct AI-related components.

role: Junior service analyst

audience: University help-service manager

situation: The manager is describing the entire service as a chatbot.

开放任务的产物与成功标准

一份简明的带注释组成部分对应图

  • 将每个已说明的组成部分对应到一个不同的角色。
  • 使用案例事实论证每种对应关系。
  • 区分标记与创建新内容。
  • 在不假定具体技术架构的情况下，纠正将界面与底层系统混为一谈的问题。

Open-task product and success criteria

A concise annotated component map

  • Maps every stated component to a distinct role.
  • Uses the case facts to justify each mapping.
  • Distinguishes labelling from creating new content.
  • Corrects the interface-versus-underlying-system confusion without assuming a specific technical architecture.

步骤5：响应设计 / Stage 5: response design

题型理由

匹配题最能保留核心关系：多个具体组成部分对应课程所定义的不同角色。四行可以涵盖标记、拟写、消息窗口和输入的限制条件，同时不暗示任何特定的技术架构。

未保留的表现

  • 独立制作组成部分对应图。
  • 为每种对应关系分别撰写论证。
  • 解释所有组成部分如何组合成更广泛的服务。
  • 自行选择表格、图示或文字结构。

Format rationale

Matching best preserves the focal relationship: several concrete components correspond to distinct course-defined roles. Four rows can cover labelling, drafting, the message window, and typed constraints without implying a particular technical architecture.

Performance not retained

  • Independent production of a component map.
  • Writing a separate justification for every mapping.
  • Explaining how all components combine into a broader service.
  • Choosing an original table, diagram, or prose structure.
AI02单选 / Single choice

审阅背景，不属于学生题面 / Reviewer context, not part of the student question

TACOS结构化提示词设计

TACOS明确任务、受众、背景、输出要求及信息来源。应判断这些要素如何共同满足用途，而不是只数是否出现五个标签。

本题保留考察：选择最连贯地支持预期迎新用途、同时遵守信息来源边界并承认一项真正的剩余局限的提示词修订版本及其配套理由。

Structured prompt design with TACOS

TACOS specifies Task, Audience, Context, Output and Sources. Evaluate how these requirements work together for the intended use rather than counting labels.

Retained judgment: Select the prompt revision and accompanying rationale that most coherently support the intended orientation use while respecting the source boundary and acknowledging a genuine remaining limitation.

来源 / Source: Video 3.1 ·

改进“写一些关于面向学生的校园服务的内容。”选择最连贯的修订版本及其理由，用于新生迎新说明。它必须涵盖所提供的校园地图文本中的服务地点，以及所提供的通知中的图书馆开放时间，只使用这两份文本，不使用任何其他信息来源。可用的格式应便于查阅；有意义的局限是实际的权衡或边界。

A使用这两份文本和一般大学知识，撰写一份供教职员工使用的详细报告。详尽有助于内容完整，但学生可能会觉得太长。
B仅使用所提供的两份文本，创建一份简明的新生迎新说明，并分别以服务地点和图书馆开放时间为标题。标题便于查阅，但简洁可能会导致细节缺失。
C仅使用图书馆开放时间通知，创建一份面向新生的开放时间核对清单。它便于快速使用，但遗漏了服务地点。
D仅使用所提供的两份文本，为新生撰写一篇关于服务地点和图书馆开放时间的学术论文。证据范围受到限定，但论文查阅起来较慢。

整题完全匹配计分，不计部分分。

Improve “Write something about campus services for students.” Select the most coherent revision and rationale for a new-student orientation note. It must cover service locations from the supplied campus-map text and library times from the supplied notice, using both texts and no other sources. A usable format is readily consultable; a meaningful limitation is a practical trade-off or boundary.

AWrite a detailed staff report using both texts and general university knowledge. Detail supports completeness, but students may find it long.
BUsing both supplied texts only, create a concise new-student orientation note with headings for service locations and library times. Headings aid consultation, but brevity may omit detail.
CUsing only the library-hours notice, create a new-student checklist of opening times. It is quick to use but omits service locations.
DUsing both supplied texts only, write an academic essay for new students about service locations and library times. Evidence is bounded, but the essay is slow to consult.

Whole-item exact match; no partial credit.

参考答案与解释 / Reference answer and explanation

B (仅使用所提供的两份文本，创建一份简明的新生迎新说明，并分别以服务地点和图书馆开放时间为标题。标题便于查阅，但简洁可能会导致细节缺失。)

B整合了预期任务、新生受众、迎新情境、便于查阅的格式以及两项允许使用的信息来源。其理由将标题与易用性联系起来，并指出了真正的简洁性与细节之间的权衡。A改变了受众，并允许使用外部知识。C排除了地图及其中的地点信息。D遵守了受众和信息来源边界，但刻意选择了一种不适合便捷查阅的格式。

B (Using both supplied texts only, create a concise new-student orientation note with headings for service locations and library times. Headings aid consultation, but brevity may omit detail.)

B integrates the intended task, new-student audience, orientation setting, consultable format, and both permitted sources. Its rationale connects headings to usability and identifies a genuine brevity–detail trade-off. A changes the audience and allows outside knowledge. C excludes the map and its location information. D respects the audience and source boundary but deliberately selects a format poorly suited to ready consultation.

五阶段设计记录 / Five-stage design record

步骤1：表现目标

评价一项连贯的提示词修订是否整合了实际任务、受众、情境、输出和来源约束。

综合判断各项要求是否同时得到满足，并指出一个有意义的遗留冲突；不能只是复述或改称TACOS各字母。

Stage 1: performance claim and observable evidence

Evaluate whether a coherent prompt revision integrates the actual task, audience, context, output and source constraints.

An integrated decision about jointly satisfied requirements and a meaningful remaining conflict; not merely reciting or relabelling TACOS letters.

步骤2：情境与开放任务

一名学习技能辅导员需要一个能让AI为新生生成实用迎新说明的提示词。AI只能使用所提供的校园地图文本和图书馆开放时间通知；不允许使用任何其他信息来源。

审阅这份草稿：“写一些关于面向学生的校园服务的内容。”将其修订为一个连贯的提示词，明确说明AI必须做什么、新生受众、迎新情境、可用的输出格式以及两个允许使用的信息来源。然后说明这些要求如何协同作用，并指出仍然存在的一项有意义的冲突或局限。

Stage 2: scenario and open task

A study-skills tutor needs an AI prompt that produces a useful orientation note for new students. The AI may use only the supplied campus-map text and library-hours notice; no other sources are permitted.

Review this draft: “Write something about campus services for students.” Revise it into one coherent prompt specifying what the AI must do, the new-student audience, the orientation context, a usable output format, and the two permitted sources. Then justify how these requirements work together and identify one meaningful conflict or limitation that remains.

GRASPS

目标: 创建并评估用于迎新说明的连贯提示词。

角色: 学习技能辅导员

受众: 大学新生

情境: 学生需要仅基于所提供的两份文本的准确且便于查阅的指导。

GRASPS

goal: Create and evaluate a coherent prompt for an orientation note.

role: Study-skills tutor

audience: New university students

situation: Students need accurate, accessible guidance based only on two supplied texts.

开放任务的产物与成功标准

修订后的提示词及简短说明。

  • 提示词使全部五项要求都具体且能够相互配合使用。
  • 说明解释了这些要求之间的相互作用，而非仅仅列出标签。
  • 指出一项真正的剩余冲突或局限。

Open-task product and success criteria

A revised prompt plus a brief justification.

  • The prompt makes all five requirements concrete and mutually usable.
  • The justification explains their interaction rather than merely naming labels.
  • A genuine remaining conflict or limitation is identified.

source: AI02-course		taught_knowledge
结构化提示词会明确AI应使用哪些信息来源。
A structured prompt specifies which information sources the AI should use.

步骤5：响应设计 / Stage 5: response design

题型理由

单项选择最能保留核心主张的综合性质。每个选项都可以将完整的提示词修订版本与理由配对，使学习者能够评估整体适切性，而不是识别孤立的标签。四个选项符合规定的数量限制，并可容纳合理的替代方案，同时不暗示只有一种措辞或输出格式普遍有效。

未保留的表现

  • 学习者不再独立生成一个连贯的修订版提示词。
  • 该题目提供了候选修订版本及其理由，因此无法显示学习者能否自行构思合适的措辞或格式选择。

Format rationale

Single choice best preserves the integrated nature of the focal claim. Each option can pair a complete prompt revision with a rationale, allowing the learner to evaluate joint fitness rather than identify isolated labels. Four options fit the required limit and permit plausible alternatives without implying that only one wording or output format is universally valid.

Performance not retained

  • Learners no longer independently produce a coherent revised prompt.
  • The item provides candidate revisions and rationales, so it cannot show whether learners can originate suitable wording or format choices.
AI03匹配 / Matching

审阅背景，不属于学生题面 / Reviewer context, not part of the student question

任务需求与AI能力的匹配

生成、分类、预测、检测、推荐和决策支持具有不同作用。先识别任务需要的处理方式，再安排适合的能力和输出。

本题保留考察：将四种不同的服务台处理需求与合适的 AI 能力进行匹配，其中包括基于预定义模式的检测与基于偏离正常活动的异常检测之间的直接区分。

Matching task requirements to AI capabilities

Generation, classification, prediction, detection, recommendation and decision support serve different roles. Match the required processing and result to the task.

Retained judgment: Match four distinct help-desk processing needs to suitable AI capabilities, including a direct distinction between predefined-pattern detection and anomaly detection based on departure from normal activity.

来源 / Source: Video 4.1 ·

某虚构大学的服务台需要为每项相互独立的任务选择合适的 AI 能力。请将每项任务与最符合所需处理和结果的能力进行匹配。每种能力使用一次。预定义模式是已经明确指定的条件；通常行为是该账户已形成的行为模式。

备选项

C1分类
C2预测
C3检测
C4异常检测

待匹配内容

R1使用先前已标注的示例，为每份书面请求分配一个主题标签。
R2使用历史每周总量估算下周的请求量。
R3标记符合预定义可疑登录模式的活动。
R4标记与账户通常行为显著不同的活动。

整题完全匹配计分，不计部分分。 每个选项最多使用一次。

A fictional university help desk needs a suitable AI capability for each independent task. Match each task to the capability that best fits the required processing and result. Use each capability once. A predefined pattern is an already specified condition; usual behavior is the account’s established pattern.

Choices

C1Classification
C2Prediction
C3Detection
C4Anomaly detection

Rows to match

R1Assign each written request one topic label using prior labeled examples.
R2Use historical weekly totals to estimate next week’s request volume.
R3Flag activity matching a predefined suspicious login pattern.
R4Flag activity differing markedly from the account’s usual behavior.

Whole-item exact match; no partial credit. Use each option at most once.

参考答案与解释 / Reference answer and explanation

R1 → C1 (分类); R2 → C2 (预测); R3 → C3 (检测); R4 → C4 (异常检测)

R1 需要分配主题类别，因此分类最合适。R2 需要估算未来的请求量，因此预测最合适。R3 搜索已明确指定的条件，因此检测最合适。R4 识别与已形成的正常行为之间的偏离，因此异常检测最合适。

R1 → C1 (Classification); R2 → C2 (Prediction); R3 → C3 (Detection); R4 → C4 (Anomaly detection)

R1 requires assigning topic categories, so classification fits. R2 requires estimating a future volume, so prediction fits. R3 searches for an already specified condition, so detection fits. R4 identifies a departure from established normal behavior, so anomaly detection fits.

五阶段设计记录 / Five-stage design record

步骤1：表现目标

确定哪种人工智能能力分别适用于多个不同的处理需求。

依据输入和预期结果，说明多个“任务—能力”对应关系，而不是提出一个总体偏好的工作流程。

Stage 1: performance claim and observable evidence

Determine which AI capability addresses each of several distinct processing needs.

Several task-to-capability correspondences justified by inputs and intended results, not a global preferred workflow.

步骤2：情境与开放任务

某大学服务台接收书面请求。工作人员需要 AI 支持，以便按主题标注每份请求、标记异常账户活动、估算下周的请求量、起草常规回复、建议相关帮助文章，并展示证据以帮助工作人员决定是否将案例升级。工作人员对所有行动保留责任。

为服务台经理撰写一份简明的能力方案。针对每项需求，选择一种 AI 能力，并结合输入、所需处理和预期结果说明匹配理由。区分异常检测（发现异常模式）与更广义的检测。说明工作人员将如何验证输出并使用这些输出，同时不将最终责任转交给 AI。在能够根据所述需求给出理由的情况下，不止一种方案是合理的。

Stage 2: scenario and open task

A university help desk receives written requests. Staff need AI support to label each request by topic, flag unusual account activity, estimate next week's request volume, draft routine replies, suggest relevant help articles, and show evidence to help staff decide whether to escalate a case. Staff retain responsibility for all actions.

Write a concise capability plan for the help-desk manager. For each need, select an AI capability and justify the match using the input, required processing, and intended result. Distinguish anomaly detection (finding unusual patterns) from broader detection. Include how staff would validate outputs and use them without transferring final responsibility to AI. More than one plan is legitimate when justified by the stated need.

GRASPS

目标: 将每项不同的服务台需求与合适的 AI 能力进行匹配。

角色: 初级 AI 工作流顾问

受众: 服务台经理

情境: 经理需要针对具体任务的支持，而不是用一种 AI 工具处理整个工作流。

GRASPS

goal: Match each distinct help-desk need to a suitable AI capability.

role: Junior AI workflow adviser

audience: Help-desk manager

situation: The manager needs task-specific support rather than one AI tool for the entire workflow.

开放任务的产物与成功标准

一份简明且有理据的能力方案

  • 分别处理每项所述需求。
  • 将每项选择与可用输入和预期结果联系起来。
  • 在合理的角色中使用生成、分类、预测、检测或异常检测、推荐和决策支持。
  • 说明实际的人工验证和责任。

Open-task product and success criteria

A concise, justified capability plan

  • Addresses every stated need separately.
  • Links each choice to the available input and intended result.
  • Uses generation, classification, prediction, detection or anomaly detection, recommendation, and decision support in defensible roles.
  • States practical human validation and responsibility.

步骤5：响应设计 / Stage 5: response design

题型理由

匹配最直接地保留了焦点对应关系和 O2 的选择要求。四行使主题分类、预测、预定义模式检测和异常检测能够分别得到考查，同时不揭示它们的映射关系。

未保留的表现

  • 独立分解服务台的总体目标
  • 独立给出理由
  • 明确说明输入、处理和结果之间的联系
  • 设计验证方法
  • 构建工作流交接
  • 生成完整的能力方案
  • 对生成、推荐和决策支持进行判断
  • 涵盖回复起草、文章建议和升级支持
  • 应用人工责任方面的考虑

Format rationale

Matching most directly preserves the focal correspondence relation and O2's selection demand. Four rows allow topic classification, prediction, predefined-pattern detection, and anomaly detection to be observed separately without revealing their mappings.

Performance not retained

  • Independent decomposition of the overall help-desk goal
  • Independent production of justifications
  • Explicit explanation linking input, processing, and result
  • Design of validation methods
  • Construction of workflow handoffs
  • Production of a complete capability plan
  • Judgments about generation, recommendation, and decision support
  • Coverage of reply drafting, article suggestion, and escalation support
  • Application of human-responsibility considerations
AI04判断题组 / Judgment matrix

审阅背景，不属于学生题面 / Reviewer context, not part of the student question

AI输出评价：证据、用途与完整性

使用前需检查相关性、准确性、清晰度、完整性、保密性、合规性、偏见及来源；检查范围应与实际用途相对应。

本题保留考察：根据预期的公开用途、所提供的证据和相关的输出审查考量，分别判断四项拟议的审查决定中，每项行动及其理由是否有充分依据。

Evaluating AI outputs against evidence and intended use

Before use, evaluate relevance, accuracy, clarity, completeness, confidentiality, compliance, bias and sources. Select the checks relevant to the actual intended use.

Retained judgment: Judge four proposed review decisions independently for whether each action and rationale is warranted by the public intended use, supplied evidence, and relevant output-review considerations.

来源 / Source: Videos 5.1 and 7.1 ·

某社团将于明天公开发布一个由 AI 起草的食品募捐网页。日期已确认，收集点尚未确认，受助者被称为“the needy（需援助者）”，某项引用的出处无法找到，并且一名志愿者的私人电话号码在未说明已获授权的情况下出现在页面中。请标明每项提议是否有充分依据。权威信息来源是指负责提供活动信息的来源。

p1向权威信息来源核实收集点；仅确认日期仍可能使捐赠者前往错误的地点。
p2将“the needy（需援助者）”替换为中性措辞，因为公开描述可能以带有偏见或不恰当的方式呈现受助者。
p3核实该引用的出处，或删除该引用，因为不应将读者引向一个无法找到的来源。
p4保留私人电话号码，因为公开一个直接联系方式有助于捐赠者迅速联系该社团，因而具有相关性。

整题完全匹配计分，不计部分分。

A society will publicly post an AI-drafted food-drive page tomorrow. The date is confirmed, the collection point is unconfirmed, recipients are called “the needy,” a citation cannot be found, and a volunteer’s private phone number appears without stated authorization. Mark each proposal warranted or not warranted. An authoritative source is one responsible for event information.

p1Verify the collection point with an authoritative source; confirming only the date could still leave donors with an incorrect location.
p2Replace “the needy” with neutral wording because the public description may represent recipients in a biased or inappropriate way.
p3Verify the citation or remove it because readers should not be directed to a source that cannot be found.
p4Keep the private phone number because publicizing a direct contact is relevant to helping donors reach the society quickly.

Whole-item exact match; no partial credit.

参考答案与解释 / Reference answer and explanation

p1: 成立; p2: 成立; p3: 成立; p4: 不成立

P1 有充分依据：地点仍未确认，仅核实日期是不够的。P2 恰当地处理了可能带有偏见的公开措辞。P3 处理了信息来源的可靠性问题。P4 没有充分依据，因为相关性并不能消除保密风险；在未说明已获授权的情况下，应删除该私人号码，或将其替换为经授权的公开联系方式。

p1: Supported; p2: Supported; p3: Supported; p4: Not supported

P1 is warranted: the location remains unconfirmed, and checking the date alone is insufficient. P2 appropriately addresses potentially biased public wording. P3 addresses source reliability. P4 is not warranted because relevance does not resolve the confidentiality risk; without stated authorization, the private number should be removed or replaced with an authorized public contact.

五阶段设计记录 / Five-stage design record

步骤1：表现目标

依据证据、预期用途和相关评价标准，评价多个各自有依据的输出审查决定。

分别判断应核验或修改什么及其原因，其中包括看似合理但并不充分的检查。不得把所有行动合并为一个最佳总体方案。

Stage 1: performance claim and observable evidence

Evaluate several independently warranted output-review decisions using evidence, intended use and relevant evaluation criteria.

Parallel judgments about what to verify or revise and why, including plausible but insufficient checks. Do not bundle all actions into one best overall plan.

步骤2：情境与开放任务

你正在协助一个学生社团发布一个由 AI 起草的校园食品募捐网页。草稿中的活动日期正确，但列出了一个尚未确认的收集点，遗漏了轮椅通行信息，将受助者描述为“the needy（需援助者）”，引用了一个无法找到的来源，并包含一名志愿者的私人电话号码。该网页计划于明天公开使用。

为社团编辑撰写一份简洁的发布建议。针对每个问题，说明应核实、修改、删除还是接受；指出相关的审查考量；并结合预期的公开用途和所述证据说明你的决定。至少包括一项单独采用时并不充分的检查。

Stage 2: scenario and open task

You are helping a student society publish an AI-drafted webpage for a campus food-drive. The draft gives the correct event date but names an unconfirmed collection point, omits wheelchair-access information, describes recipients as “the needy,” cites a source that cannot be found, and includes a volunteer’s private phone number. The page is intended for public use tomorrow.

Write a concise release recommendation to the society editor. For each issue, state whether to verify, revise, remove, or accept it; identify the relevant review consideration; and justify your decision using the intended public use and the stated evidence. Include at least one check that would be insufficient on its own.

GRASPS

目标: 在发布前，对 AI 草稿作出可辩护的、以证据为指导的决定。

角色: 学生传播内容审查员

受众: 社团编辑

情境: 一个公开网页必须在发布前接受审查。

GRASPS

goal: Make defensible, evidence-guided decisions about an AI draft before publication.

role: Student communications reviewer

audience: Society editor

situation: A public webpage must be reviewed before release.

开放任务的产物与成功标准

一份简洁的发布建议，针对每个问题分别作出决定。

  • 分别处理所述的每个问题。
  • 选择与公开用途相关的审查考量。
  • 将每项行动与案例证据联系起来。
  • 说明为何一项看似合理的检查单独采用时并不充分。

Open-task product and success criteria

A concise release recommendation with separate decisions for each issue.

  • Addresses every stated issue separately.
  • Selects review considerations relevant to public use.
  • Links each action to case evidence.
  • Identifies why one plausible check is insufficient alone.

步骤5：响应设计 / Stage 5: response design

题型理由

四命题判断矩阵在遵守作答限制的同时保留了并列判断关系。通过移除轮椅通行问题而不是将其与另一个问题合并，明确缩小了覆盖范围。

未保留的表现

  • 独立撰写给编辑的建议。
  • 生成原创的行动、审查考量和理由。
  • 形成一项有条件的总体发布建议。
  • 审查被遗漏的轮椅通行信息。
  • 通过轮椅通行问题体现完整性审查。
  • 处理原案例中的每个问题。

Format rationale

A four-proposition true/false matrix preserves the parallel-judgment relation while respecting the response limit. Coverage is explicitly narrowed by removing the wheelchair-access issue rather than combining it with another issue.

Performance not retained

  • Independent production of an editor recommendation.
  • Generating original actions, review considerations, and rationales.
  • Forming a conditional overall release recommendation.
  • Reviewing the omitted wheelchair-access information.
  • Demonstrating completeness review through the wheelchair-access issue.
  • Addressing every issue from the original case.
AI05匹配 / Matching

审阅背景，不属于学生题面 / Reviewer context, not part of the student question

人工验证、批准与持续责任

人负责对照真实资料验证结果、批准使用，并在资料或系统变化后维护成果。责任贯穿流程，不只是在末尾形式上确认。

本题保留考察：评估在虚构的 AI 支持型指南工作流的每个阶段，哪种不同的人员验证、批准或持续负责最为合适。

Human validation, approval and continuing responsibility

People validate results against real evidence, approve their use and maintain them as sources or systems change. Ownership and accountability continue across the workflow.

Retained judgment: Evaluate which distinct human validation, approval, or continuing ownership is appropriate for each stage of the fictional AI-supported guide workflow.

来源 / Source: Videos 5.1 and 5.2F ·

某大学团队使用 AI，根据官方记录更新校园无障碍指南。当前校园信息也可供使用。不要求任何特定职位。请将每种情况与最合适的人员责任进行匹配。维护是指使已发布材料保持最新。每个选项使用一次。

备选项

C1在考虑已完成的循证审查后，授权使用。
C2将提取出的陈述与权威且最新的证据进行比较。
C3监测相关变化，并在需要时修订公开材料。
C4评估整个条目是否准确、完整且适合用户。

待匹配内容

R1提取出的坡道位置与近期校园调查结果相冲突。
R2一份草稿有事实依据，但遗漏了对用户而言重要的通行限制。
R3检查已经完成；该条目正在等待是否公开使用的决定。
R4发布后，翻修改变了无障碍入口。

整题完全匹配计分，不计部分分。 每个选项最多使用一次。

A university team uses AI to update a campus-access guide from official records. Current campus information is also available. No particular job title is required. Match each situation to the most appropriate human responsibility. Maintenance means keeping published material current. Use each option once.

Choices

C1Authorize use after considering the completed evidence-based review.
C2Compare the extracted claims with authoritative and current evidence.
C3Monitor relevant changes and revise the public material when needed.
C4Assess whether the whole entry is accurate, complete, and suitable for users.

Rows to match

R1The extracted ramp location conflicts with a recent campus survey.
R2A draft is factually supported but omits access limitations important to users.
R3Checks are complete; the entry awaits a decision about public use.
R4After publication, renovation changes the accessible entrance.

Whole-item exact match; no partial credit. Use each option at most once.

参考答案与解释 / Reference answer and explanation

R1 → C2 (将提取出的陈述与权威且最新的证据进行比较。); R2 → C4 (评估整个条目是否准确、完整且适合用户。); R3 → C1 (在考虑已完成的循证审查后，授权使用。); R4 → C3 (监测相关变化，并在需要时修订公开材料。)

R1 需要循证验证，因为两个信息来源相互冲突。R2 需要对整个条目作出判断，包括其完整性和对用户的适用性。R3 需要在审查后由人员作出授权决定。R4 需要持续负责，因为后续变化可能会使已发布材料过时。

R1 → C2 (Compare the extracted claims with authoritative and current evidence.); R2 → C4 (Assess whether the whole entry is accurate, complete, and suitable for users.); R3 → C1 (Authorize use after considering the completed evidence-based review.); R4 → C3 (Monitor relevant changes and revise the public material when needed.)

R1 requires evidence-based validation because the two sources conflict. R2 requires judgement about the whole entry, including completeness and user suitability. R3 requires a human authorization decision after review. R4 requires continuing ownership because later changes can make published material outdated.

五阶段设计记录 / Five-stage design record

步骤1：表现目标

确定人工智能辅助工作流程不同阶段所需的人工验证或责任。

给出多个“活动—验证或责任”对应关系；保留不同可观察案例情境中的责任差异，而不是用一句笼统的审批口号概括。

Stage 1: performance claim and observable evidence

Determine the human validation or responsibility appropriate to different stages of an AI-supported workflow.

Several activity-to-validation/responsibility correspondences; preserve how responsibility differs across observable case situations rather than one generic approval slogan.

步骤2：情境与开放任务

某大学团队使用 AI 更新校园无障碍指南。工作人员提供官方无障碍记录；AI 检索相关事实并起草条目。一名工作人员将每个条目与这些记录及当前校园信息进行比较。获批准的条目会被发布。建筑物和信息来源记录之后可能会发生变化。

作为工作流协调员，为指南团队撰写一份简要的责任计划。针对每个阶段——信息来源准备、事实检索、起草、发布前审查、批准和发布后维护——说明人员必须验证什么、决定什么或负责什么。找出两个交接点，其中一项输出成为后续环节的输入。建议应由谁采取行动，以及他们应检查哪些证据。解释为什么一次最终批准不足以涵盖此工作流。

Stage 2: scenario and open task

A university team uses AI to update a campus-access guide. Staff supply official accessibility records; AI retrieves relevant facts and drafts entries. A staff member compares each entry with those records and current campus information. Approved entries are published. Buildings and source records may later change.

As the workflow coordinator, write a brief responsibility plan for the guide team. For each stage—source preparation, fact retrieval, drafting, pre-publication review, approval, and post-publication maintenance—state what a person must validate, decide, or own. Identify two handoffs where one output becomes a later input. Recommend who should act and what evidence they should check. Explain why a single final approval would not adequately cover this workflow.

GRASPS

目标: 在 AI 支持型工作流的各环节分配适当的人员验证、批准、负责和维护职责。

角色: 工作流协调员

受众: 校园指南团队

情境: 团队必须发布并维护准确的 AI 辅助指南条目。

GRASPS

goal: Assign appropriate human validation, approval, ownership, and maintenance across an AI-supported workflow.

role: Workflow coordinator

audience: Campus guide team

situation: The team must publish and maintain accurate AI-assisted guide entries.

开放任务的产物与成功标准

一份简明的分阶段责任计划，其中包含有理据支持的交接安排。

  • 区分所述六个阶段中的验证与责任。
  • 列出用于检查所检索事实和所起草条目的具体证据。
  • 将批准置于发布之前，并安排发布后的持续维护责任。
  • 展示两个实际的输出到输入的交接点，且不对不相关的行动强加顺序。
  • 在整个过程中始终由人员承担负责和问责。

Open-task product and success criteria

A concise stage-by-stage responsibility plan with justified handoffs.

  • Distinguishes validation and responsibility across the six stated stages.
  • Names concrete evidence for checking retrieved facts and drafted entries.
  • Places approval before publication and assigns continuing maintenance after publication.
  • Shows two actual output-to-input handoffs without imposing an order on unrelated actions.
  • Keeps ownership and accountability with people throughout.

步骤5：响应设计 / Stage 5: response design

题型理由

匹配题最能保留可观察的工作流情境与不同人员责任之间的既定关系。四个题行和三至五个选项可以评估验证、批准和维护，而无需规定职位或程序。

未保留的表现

  • 独立制定六阶段责任计划。
  • 识别并解释两个输出到输入的交接点。
  • 独立解释为什么一次最终批准还不够。
  • 推荐特定人员分配并为其提供理据。

Format rationale

Matching best preserves the retained relationship between observable workflow situations and distinct human responsibilities. Four rows and three to five options can assess validation, approval, and maintenance without prescribing job titles or procedures.

Performance not retained

  • Independent production of a six-stage responsibility plan.
  • Identification and explanation of two output-to-input handoffs.
  • Independent explanation of why one final approval is insufficient.
  • Recommendation and justification of particular personnel assignments.
AI06匹配 / Matching

审阅背景，不属于学生题面 / Reviewer context, not part of the student question

工作流的信息依赖与先后关系

步骤的输出会成为后续步骤的输入。应依据资料、草稿和验证结果之间的真实依赖安排顺序，允许没有依赖的活动并行。

本题保留考察：通过将每项后续活动与其所需的先前输出或验证结果进行匹配，重建案例的依赖结构，同时识别出独立的封面设计没有必需的内容工作流前置项。

Information dependencies and workflow sequencing

Outputs feed later inputs. Sequence work using the actual dependencies among evidence, drafts and validation results; do not invent an order between independent actions.

Retained judgment: Reconstruct the case's dependency structure by matching each later activity to the earlier output or validation result it needs, while identifying independent cover design as having no required content-workflow predecessor.

来源 / Source: Video 5.1 ·

一个团队正在更新校园指南。Lina 从官方网站中提取事实。一个 AI 工具根据这些事实起草条目。Omar 对照网页检查草稿。Priya 收到 Omar 的检查结果，并决定是否批准经检查的条目。Mei 独立于所有内容活动设计封面。验证是指对照真实证据检查结果。 将每项活动与它实际需要的先前工作流输出或验证结果进行匹配。每个选项使用一次。

备选项

C1Lina 提取的事实
C2AI 生成的条目草稿
C3Omar 的检查结果
C4没有必需的内容工作流前置项

待匹配内容

R1AI 起草
R2Omar 的检查
R3Priya 的批准
R4Mei 的封面设计

整题完全匹配计分，不计部分分。 每个选项最多使用一次。

A team updates a campus guide. Lina extracts facts from official webpages. An AI tool drafts entries from those facts. Omar checks the drafts against the webpages. Priya receives Omar’s checking result and decides whether to approve the checked entries. Mei designs the cover independently of all content activities. Validation means checking a result against real evidence. Match each activity to the earlier workflow output or validation result it actually requires. Use each option once.

Choices

C1Lina’s extracted facts
C2The AI-produced draft entries
C3Omar’s checking result
C4No required content-workflow predecessor

Rows to match

R1AI drafting
R2Omar’s checking
R3Priya’s approval
R4Mei’s cover design

Whole-item exact match; no partial credit. Use each option at most once.

参考答案与解释 / Reference answer and explanation

R1 → C1 (Lina 提取的事实); R2 → C2 (AI 生成的条目草稿); R3 → C3 (Omar 的检查结果); R4 → C4 (没有必需的内容工作流前置项)

起草需要 Lina 提取的事实；检查需要草稿；而 Priya 的人工批准需要 Omar 的验证结果。Mei 的工作被明确说明为独立进行，因此，为其强加一个内容工作流前置项会增加一项没有依据的约束。

R1 → C1 (Lina’s extracted facts); R2 → C2 (The AI-produced draft entries); R3 → C3 (Omar’s checking result); R4 → C4 (No required content-workflow predecessor)

Drafting requires Lina’s facts; checking requires the drafts; and Priya’s human approval requires Omar’s validation result. Mei’s work is explicitly independent, so imposing a content-workflow predecessor would add an unsupported constraint.

五阶段设计记录 / Five-stage design record

步骤1：表现目标

根据后续活动实际需要的输出或验证结果，排列相互关联的工作流程活动。

依据明确的信息依赖关系说明相对顺序，同时不限制彼此独立的行动。核心证据是依赖结构，而不是选择整套现成方案。

Stage 1: performance claim and observable evidence

Arrange linked workflow activities according to which output or validation result a later activity actually needs.

Relative ordering justified by explicit information dependencies, with independent actions kept unconstrained. The central evidence is the dependency structure, not selecting an entire ready-made plan.

步骤2：情境与开放任务

一个大学团队正在更新校园指南。Lina 从官方网站中提取事实；一个 AI 工具根据这些事实起草条目；Omar 对照网页检查草稿；Mei 独立设计指南封面；Priya 批准经检查的条目以供发布。

创建一个紧凑的工作流计划，说明哪些活动必须先于其他活动，哪些活动可以按任意顺序进行。对于每项必需的顺序关系，指出后续活动所需的输出或验证结果。建议应将发布批准置于何处，并说明理由。验证是指对照真实证据检查结果。

Stage 2: scenario and open task

A university team is updating a campus guide. Lina extracts facts from official webpages; an AI tool drafts entries from those facts; Omar checks drafts against the webpages; Mei designs the guide cover independently; and Priya approves checked entries for publication.

Create a compact workflow plan showing which activities must precede others and which may occur in any order. For every required ordering, name the output or validation result needed by the later activity. Recommend where publication approval belongs and justify it. Validation means checking a result against real evidence.

GRASPS

目标: 根据实际的信息依赖关系，制定一个站得住脚的顺序。

角色: 工作流规划者

受众: 校园指南团队

情境: 团队需要一个高效且责任明确的更新流程。

GRASPS

goal: Produce a defensible sequence based on actual information dependencies.

role: Workflow planner

audience: Campus guide team

situation: The team needs an efficient, accountable update process.

开放任务的产物与成功标准

附有简要理由的依赖关系图或有序计划

  • 通过明确的交接，将事实提取、起草、检查和批准联系起来。
  • 除非有合理依据证明存在明确的依赖关系，否则不对封面设计施加约束。
  • 将人工批准安排在做出该决定所需的证据已经存在之后。

Open-task product and success criteria

A dependency diagram or ordered plan with brief justifications

  • Links fact extraction, drafting, checking, and approval through explicit handoffs.
  • Leaves cover design unconstrained unless a stated dependency is justified.
  • Places human approval after the evidence needed for that decision exists.

步骤5：响应设计 / Stage 5: response design

题型理由

匹配形式保留了可观察的依赖关系重建，而不是将任务简化为选择一个现成计划。它涵盖了若干不同的交接，并允许为独立工作明确选择“无前置项”选项。

未保留的表现

  • 独立识别所有活动的输入和输出。
  • 制作完整的依赖关系图或偏序计划。
  • 为每次交接撰写解释。
  • 撰写关于持续存在的人工责任的理由。

Format rationale

Matching preserves the observable dependency reconstruction rather than reducing it to selection of a ready-made plan. It captures several distinct handoffs and permits an explicit no-predecessor option for independent work.

Performance not retained

  • Independently identifying all activity inputs and outputs.
  • Producing a complete dependency diagram or partially ordered plan.
  • Writing explanations for every handoff.
  • Writing a justification of continuing human responsibility.
AI07匹配 / Matching

审阅背景，不属于学生题面 / Reviewer context, not part of the student question

子任务分解与六要素工作流

为子任务分别明确任务、输入、AI能力、输出、验证方式和人工责任，再检查不同环节如何交接和共同完成目标。

本题保留考察：诊断每个服务台子任务描述中缺失或错误分配的组件，包括所述 AI 能力是否适合所需处理。

Task decomposition and the six-component workflow template

For each subtask specify Task, Input, AI Capability, Output, Validation and Human Responsibility, then check how handoffs jointly achieve the goal.

Retained judgment: Diagnose which component is missing or misassigned in each help-desk subtask description, including whether the stated AI capability fits the required processing.

来源 / Source: Video 5.1 ·

某大学服务台使用预定义的主题类别，并且可以查阅官方课表事实。请将每个缺陷与缺失或不合适的组件匹配。验证是指检查结果；人员责任是指承担责任的人员角色。每个选项可使用一次。

备选项

c1AI 能力
c2输出
c3验证
c4人员责任

待匹配内容

r1主题标注被指定为生成，而不是类别分配。
r2回复起草列出了所使用的事实，但未列出由此产生的草稿。
r3回复起草未包含任何与官方事实的比较。
r4在发送环节，工作人员只录入数据；没有人批准发布。

整题完全匹配计分，不计部分分。 每个选项最多使用一次。

A university help desk uses predefined topic categories and has official timetable facts available. Match each defect to the component that is missing or unsuitable. Validation means checking a result; human responsibility means the accountable person’s role. Options may each be used once.

Choices

c1AI capability
c2Output
c3Validation
c4Human responsibility

Rows to match

r1Topic labelling is assigned generation rather than category assignment.
r2Reply drafting names facts used but not the resulting draft.
r3Reply drafting includes no comparison with official facts.
r4For sending, staff only enter data; nobody approves release.

Whole-item exact match; no partial credit. Use each option at most once.

参考答案与解释 / Reference answer and explanation

r1 → c1 (AI 能力); r2 → c2 (输出); r3 → c3 (验证); r4 → c4 (人员责任)

主题标注是将问题分配到预定义类别，因此生成是一种不合适的 AI 能力。起草步骤未列出其生成的结果，即回复草稿，并且还单独遗漏了依据官方事实进行检查。数据录入并未明确批准或发布回复的问责责任。

r1 → c1 (AI capability); r2 → c2 (Output); r3 → c3 (Validation); r4 → c4 (Human responsibility)

Topic labelling assigns a question to a predefined category, so generation is an unsuitable AI capability. The drafting step fails to name its produced result, the draft reply, and separately omits a check against official facts. Data entry does not assign accountability for approving or releasing the response.

五阶段设计记录 / Five-stage design record

步骤1：表现目标

使用六要素工作流程模板，诊断多个子任务描述中缺失或错误归属的组成部分。

给出多个“子任务缺陷—组成部分”对应关系，要求理解输入、能力、输出、验证或人工责任。

Stage 1: performance claim and observable evidence

Diagnose missing or misassigned components in several subtask descriptions using the six-component workflow template.

Several subtask-defect-to-component correspondences that require understanding input, capability, output, validation or human responsibility.

步骤2：情境与开放任务

某大学服务台希望使用 AI 辅助回答日常课表问题。其工作流程草案规定：(1) 收集学生的问题并标注其主题，但将生成列为所用能力；(2) 根据检索到的官方课表事实起草回复，但未列出输出或验证；(3) 发送回复，而工作人员只负责录入数据。没有描述任何交接。

作为工作流程顾问，请为服务台经理写一份简明的修订方案。诊断每个子任务中缺失或错误分配的组件，用合理的选择替换它们，并说明信息如何在子任务之间传递。为每个修订后的子任务列出全部六个组件，并说明草稿在发送前由谁检查。

Stage 2: scenario and open task

A university help desk wants AI support for answering routine timetable questions. Its draft workflow says: (1) collect a student's question and label its topic, but lists generation as the capability; (2) draft a reply from retrieved official timetable facts, but names no output or validation; (3) send the reply, with staff responsible only for entering data. No handoffs are described.

As a workflow adviser, write a concise revision for the help-desk manager. Diagnose the missing or misassigned components in each subtask, replace them with defensible choices, and show how information passes between subtasks. Include all six components for every revised subtask and explain who checks the draft before it is sent.

GRASPS

目标: 生成一个连贯、合理且由 AI 支持的工作流程。

角色: 工作流程顾问

受众: 服务台经理

情境: 拟议工作流程存在能力不匹配、组件遗漏和交接不明确的问题。

GRASPS

goal: Produce a coherent, defensible AI-supported workflow.

role: Workflow adviser

audience: Help-desk manager

situation: The proposed workflow has mismatched capabilities, omitted components, and unclear handoffs.

开放任务的产物与成功标准

一份附有简要理由的修订后工作流程

  • 每个子任务都明确其任务、输入、能力、输出、验证和人员责任。
  • 能力选择适合所需处理。
  • 适当时，输出会成为明确的输入。
  • 在发送前，验证和责任是具体明确的。

Open-task product and success criteria

A revised workflow with a brief justification

  • Each subtask identifies its task, input, capability, output, validation, and human responsibility.
  • Capability choices fit the required processing.
  • Outputs become explicit inputs where appropriate.
  • Validation and responsibility are concrete before sending.

步骤5：响应设计 / Stage 5: response design

题型理由

匹配题最能体现核心对应关系，同时保持主要的逐子任务框架有效。它可以要求作出多个简明判断，而不要求采用单一的工作流程设计。

未保留的表现

  • 独立生成完整的修订后工作流程
  • 构建并评估信息交接
  • 以书面形式说明修复理由
  • 在替代工作流程结构中进行选择

Format rationale

Matching best represents the focal correspondence while keeping the primary per-subtask framework active. It permits several concise judgments without demanding a single workflow design.

Performance not retained

  • Independent production of a complete revised workflow
  • Construction and evaluation of information handoffs
  • Written justification of repairs
  • Selection among alternative workflow structures
AI08匹配 / Matching

审阅背景，不属于学生题面 / Reviewer context, not part of the student question

AI风险识别与相应保障

幻觉、偏见、敏感信息泄露和过度依赖是不同风险。要根据已知证据区分风险，并选择针对性的核查、信息保护或人工判断。

本题保留考察：针对每项观察到的特征，确定以课程内容为依据的风险解释和有针对性的保障措施，包括对不确定成因作出审慎限定的处理。

AI risks and appropriate safeguards

Hallucination, bias, sensitive-data disclosure and over-reliance are distinct risks. Use available evidence to distinguish them and choose appropriate verification, protection or human judgment.

Retained judgment: For each observed feature, determine the course-grounded risk interpretation and targeted safeguard, including qualified treatment of an uncertain cause.

来源 / Source: Video 6.1 ·

一个学生团队计划向大学受众提交一份由AI起草的校园食物获取报告。请将每项特征与其最直接支持的风险及保障措施判断相匹配。敏感信息是指需要保护的个人信息。每个选项使用一次。

备选项

C1准确性/信息来源风险：对照证据进行核实，并替换或删除缺乏支持的说法。
C2潜在偏见：审查并修改这一刻板印象，但不要声称其未被观察到的成因已得到证实。
C3保密风险：删除或保护敏感细节，并审查其披露情况。
C4过度依赖：开展相关的人工检查，而不是相信行文风格。

待匹配内容

R1草稿中包含一项捏造的调查结果，且没有支持性信息来源。
R2草稿称某个街区“缺乏积极性”；为何会出现这种措辞尚不清楚。
R3一名参与者的医疗状况被复制到AI提示词中。
R4团队未经检查便认为润色精良的文字是可靠的。

整题完全匹配计分，不计部分分。 每个选项最多使用一次。

A student team plans to submit an AI-drafted campus food-access report to a university audience. Match each feature to the most directly supported risk-and-safeguard judgment. Sensitive information means personal information that requires protection. Use each option once.

Choices

C1Accuracy/source risk: verify against evidence and replace or remove the unsupported claim.
C2Potential bias: review and revise the stereotype without claiming its unseen cause is proven.
C3Confidentiality risk: remove or protect the sensitive detail and review its disclosure.
C4Over-reliance: conduct relevant human checks rather than trusting the writing style.

Rows to match

R1The draft includes an invented survey result with no supporting source.
R2It calls one neighbourhood “unmotivated”; why this wording arose is unknown.
R3A participant’s medical condition was copied into the AI prompt.
R4The team assumes the polished prose is reliable without checking it.

Whole-item exact match; no partial credit. Use each option at most once.

参考答案与解释 / Reference answer and explanation

R1 → C1 (准确性/信息来源风险：对照证据进行核实，并替换或删除缺乏支持的说法。); R2 → C2 (潜在偏见：审查并修改这一刻板印象，但不要声称其未被观察到的成因已得到证实。); R3 → C3 (保密风险：删除或保护敏感细节，并审查其披露情况。); R4 → C4 (过度依赖：开展相关的人工检查，而不是相信行文风格。)

捏造的结果需要进行证据和信息来源核查。刻板印象需要接受偏见审查，但其成因仍不确定。使用医疗信息编写提示词会引发保密问题。将润色精良的文字视为可靠体现了过度依赖，因此在使用前需要人工评估。

R1 → C1 (Accuracy/source risk: verify against evidence and replace or remove the unsupported claim.); R2 → C2 (Potential bias: review and revise the stereotype without claiming its unseen cause is proven.); R3 → C3 (Confidentiality risk: remove or protect the sensitive detail and review its disclosure.); R4 → C4 (Over-reliance: conduct relevant human checks rather than trusting the writing style.)

The invented result warrants evidence and source checking. The stereotype warrants bias review, but its cause remains uncertain. Prompting with medical information creates a confidentiality concern. Treating polished prose as reliable shows over-reliance and requires human evaluation before use.

五阶段设计记录 / Five-stage design record

步骤1：表现目标

区分人工智能使用案例中多个已观察特征分别支持哪种风险解释和防护措施。

给出多个“特征—风险或防护措施”对应关系；明确保留因果诊断中的不确定性。不得只评价一个整体良好实践方案。

Stage 1: performance claim and observable evidence

Distinguish which risk interpretation and safeguard are warranted by several observed features of an AI-use case.

Several feature-to-risk/safeguard correspondences; uncertainty in a causal diagnosis remains explicit. Do not evaluate only one overall good-practice plan.

步骤2：情境与开放任务

一个学生团队使用AI起草了一份校园食物获取报告。草稿捏造了一项调查结果，将某个街区描述为“缺乏积极性”，包含一名参与者被复制到提示词中的医疗状况，而团队因为认为润色精良的文字是可靠的，已准备提交该报告。

作为团队的审阅者，请为团队撰写一份简短的修改备忘录。分别处理每项观察到的特征：指出其支持的风险，说明其成因方面的任何不确定性，并建议在提交前采取适度的保障措施。最后，提出有理据的提交、修改或删除建议。

Stage 2: scenario and open task

A student team used AI to draft a campus food-access report. The draft invents a survey result, describes one neighbourhood as “unmotivated,” includes a participant’s medical condition copied into the prompt, and is ready for submission because the team assumes polished prose is reliable.

As the team’s reviewer, write a brief revision memo for the team. Address each observed feature separately: identify the risk it supports, note any uncertainty about its cause, and recommend a proportionate safeguard before submission. Conclude with a justified submit, revise, or remove recommendation.

GRASPS

目标: 针对草稿作出可辩护的风险及保障措施判断。

角色: 团队审阅者

受众: 学生报告团队

情境: 一份AI辅助完成的报告包含四个不同的警示迹象。

GRASPS

goal: Produce a defensible risk-and-safeguard judgment for the draft.

role: Team reviewer

audience: Student report team

situation: An AI-assisted report contains four distinct warning signs.

开放任务的产物与成功标准

一份简明的修改备忘录

  • 分别处理案例中的全部四项特征
  • 将每项特征与有依据的风险解释和保障措施联系起来
  • 对不确定的因果说法作出审慎限定
  • 在建议采取行动之前检查证据、保密性、偏见和预期用途

Open-task product and success criteria

A concise revision memo

  • Addresses all four case features separately
  • Links each feature to a supported risk interpretation and safeguard
  • Keeps uncertain causal claims qualified
  • Checks evidence, confidentiality, bias, and intended use before recommending action

The overall submission decision is removed to focus the introductory item on feature-level judgments.
O5
dependencies: O1, O2, O3, O4
source: open_task		cognitive_operation
用明确的案例事实解释每项风险及保障措施判断，并在证据不完整时保留不确定性。
Explain each risk-and-safeguard judgment with an explicit case fact and preserve uncertainty where evidence is incomplete.
排除 / exclude
移除独立撰写的解释；匹配选项为判断提供范围受限的证据。
Independent written explanations are removed; the matching choices provide bounded evidence of judgment.
P1
dependencies: O1, O2, O3, O4, O5
source: open_task		procedure
逐项审查报告特征，说明有依据的问题和不确定性，安排有针对性的行动，然后得出使用建议。
Review the report feature by feature, state the supported concern and uncertainty, assign a targeted action, then conclude with a use recommendation.
排除 / exclude
学习者不再需要撰写或组织修改备忘录。
Learners no longer produce or organize a revision memo.
C1
dependencies: F1, F2, F3, F4, P1
source: open_task		constraint
必须处理全部四个警示迹象；只要每一项都有理据，合理的组织方式和最终建议可以有所不同。
All four warning signs must be addressed; legitimate organization and final recommendations may vary if each is justified.
保留 / retain
全部四个警示迹象必须保持分开，以免用笼统的保障措施替代所要求的对应关系。
All four warning signs must remain separate so a generic safeguard cannot replace the required correspondences.

步骤5：响应设计 / Stage 5: response design

题型理由

匹配形式最能保留从核心特征到风险及保障措施的关系，并防止作出一个笼统的良好实践回应。四行和四个选项可以包括一项对刻板印象措辞作出审慎限定的判断，同时不会预先透露对应关系。

未保留的表现

  • 独立撰写和组织修改备忘录。
  • 独立拟定保障措施。
  • 为每项判断提供明确的书面论证。
  • 提出最终的提交、修改或删除建议。

Format rationale

Matching best preserves the focal feature-to-risk-and-safeguard relation and prevents one generic good-practice response. Four rows and four options can include a qualified judgment about the stereotyping language without disclosing the mappings in advance.

Performance not retained

  • Independent production and organization of a revision memo.
  • Independent formulation of safeguards.
  • Explicit written justification for every judgment.
  • A final submit, revise, or remove recommendation.
AI09单选 / Single choice

审阅背景，不属于学生题面 / Reviewer context, not part of the student question

智能体的计划调整、工具调用与审批

智能体根据目标、执行结果和反馈调整计划；检索工具获取信息，行动工具执行操作，人设置边界、核实依据并批准需要确认的动作。

本题保留考察：选择一个完整的工作流程，以可辩护的方式将信息收集、响应反馈的准备工作、人工验证、批准以及经授权的日历执行联系起来，同时不阻碍无害的准备工作。

Agent planning, tool use and approval boundaries

An agent adjusts plans using goals, results and feedback. Retrieval tools obtain information; action tools execute operations. People set boundaries, check evidence and approve required actions.

Retained judgment: Select the complete workflow that defensibly links information gathering, feedback-responsive preparation, human validation, approval, and authorized calendar execution without blocking harmless preparation.

来源 / Source: Video 5.3 ·

一个学生支持代理可以无需批准就搜索大学目录，并起草或修改会议提议。外部执行是指更改日历。协调员必须检查目录证据和最终细节，并批准创建日历事件。哪个工作流程在尊重这一边界的同时，最妥善地利用了结果和学生反馈？

A搜索并起草；如果证据缺失，仍请协调员批准；根据意见修改，然后创建会议。
B搜索、起草并创建一个暂定会议；之后，补充获取缺失的证据或应要求修改，并寻求协调员批准。
C搜索并起草；如果证据不完整，则补充获取信息，并根据意见修改；然后在创建之前，提交证据和最终细节以供检查和批准。
D在搜索前获得批准；然后搜索、起草，并在需要时补充获取信息或修改，且无需再次审查就创建会议。

整题完全匹配计分，不计部分分。

A student-support agent may search a university directory and draft or revise a meeting proposal without approval. External execution means changing the calendar. A coordinator must check the directory evidence and final details and approve calendar creation. Which workflow best uses results and student feedback while respecting that boundary?

ASearch and draft; if evidence is missing, ask the coordinator to approve anyway; revise after comments, then create the meeting.
BSearch, draft, and create a tentative meeting; afterward, retrieve missing evidence or revise on request and seek coordinator approval.
CSearch and draft; retrieve more if evidence is incomplete and revise for comments; then submit evidence and final details for checking and approval before creation.
DObtain approval before searching; then search, draft, retrieve more or revise when needed, and create the meeting without another review.

Whole-item exact match; no partial credit.

参考答案与解释 / Reference answer and explanation

C (搜索并起草；如果证据不完整，则补充获取信息，并根据意见修改；然后在创建之前，提交证据和最终细节以供检查和批准。)

C 将检索结果用作起草的输入，针对缺失的证据和反馈作出调整，并在执行日历操作之前，提交由此得到的证据和最终细节，供人工检查和批准。A 允许在证据缺失的情况下批准。B 在检查和批准之前执行。D 不必要地阻碍了检索，并且不要求审查最终提议。

C (Search and draft; retrieve more if evidence is incomplete and revise for comments; then submit evidence and final details for checking and approval before creation.)

C uses retrieval results as drafting inputs, adapts to missing evidence and feedback, and submits the resulting evidence and final details for human checking and approval before calendar execution. A permits approval despite missing evidence. B executes before checking and approval. D unnecessarily blocks retrieval and does not require review of the final proposal.

五阶段设计记录 / Five-stage design record

步骤1：表现目标

重建智能体的信息收集、计划更新、人工验证和经授权的外部工具操作之间的前置依赖关系。

在明确的操作边界下呈现真实的依赖结构；区分检索、起草和执行，且不得虚构在无害准备工作之前需要审批。

Stage 1: performance claim and observable evidence

Reconstruct the prerequisites among an agent’s information gathering, plan updates, human validation and an authorized external tool action.

A genuine dependency structure under explicit action boundaries; retrieval, drafting and execution are distinguished without inventing approval before harmless preparation.

步骤2：情境与开放任务

一个学生支持代理必须安排一次学习会议。它可以搜索大学目录、起草一个拟议时间，并在学生提出意见后修改该提议。它的日历工具可以创建会议，但前提是协调员检查目录证据并批准最终细节。搜索和起草无需批准。

作为工作流程设计者，为协调员制作一个简洁的依赖关系图或编号计划。区分信息检索、提议起草或修改、人工验证和日历执行。说明每个步骤需要先前工作提供什么，标明批准边界，并解释当证据不完整或学生要求更改时的一种有效应对方式。

Stage 2: scenario and open task

A student-support agent must arrange a study meeting. It may search the university directory, draft a proposed time, and revise that proposal after the student comments. Its calendar tool can create the meeting, but only after a coordinator checks the directory evidence and approves the final details. Searching and drafting need no approval.

As the workflow designer, produce a concise dependency diagram or numbered plan for the coordinator. Distinguish information retrieval, proposal drafting or revision, human validation, and calendar execution. Show what each step needs from earlier work, identify the approval boundary, and explain one valid response if the evidence is incomplete or the student requests a change.

GRASPS

目标: 为安排会议设计一个可辩护的工作流程。

角色: 工作流程设计者

受众: 学生支持协调员

情境: 代理协助准备会议，而日历执行受到限制。

GRASPS

goal: Design a defensible workflow for arranging the meeting.

role: Workflow designer

audience: Student-support coordinator

situation: An agent assists with meeting preparation, while calendar execution is restricted.

开放任务的产物与成功标准

包含简要理由的依赖关系图或编号计划。

  • 明确区分检索、准备、验证和执行。
  • 依赖关系反映所述证据和反馈。
  • 日历执行在协调员批准后进行。
  • 不对无害的搜索和起草设置不必要的阻碍。
  • 计划处理证据不完整或被要求更改的情况。

Open-task product and success criteria

A dependency diagram or numbered plan with a brief justification.

  • Retrieval, preparation, validation, and execution are clearly distinguished.
  • Dependencies reflect the stated evidence and feedback.
  • Calendar execution follows coordinator approval.
  • Harmless searching and drafting are not unnecessarily blocked.
  • The plan addresses incomplete evidence or requested changes.

步骤5：响应设计 / Stage 5: response design

题型理由

单项选择形式保留了核心的综合判断，同时允许候选项在依赖关系结构、反馈处理和关卡设置方面有所不同。它避免暗示只有一种僵化的线性顺序是合理的。

未保留的表现

  • 独立制作依赖关系图或编号计划。
  • 由学习者自行撰写判断说明。
  • 开放呈现多种合理的工作流程结构。

Format rationale

Single choice preserves the focal integrated judgment while allowing candidates to differ in dependency structure, feedback handling, and gate placement. It avoids implying that only one rigid linear sequence is legitimate.

Performance not retained

  • Independent production of a dependency diagram or numbered plan.
  • A learner-authored explanation of the judgment.
  • Open representation of multiple legitimate workflow structures.
AI10单选 / Single choice

审阅背景，不属于学生题面 / Reviewer context, not part of the student question

数据偏差与AI推荐结果

AI推荐可能反映训练资料中的偏差。群体差异可以提示需要调查，但仅凭结果分布还不能确定偏差来自哪一个环节。

本题保留考察：识别最站得住脚的综合建议：将群体差异视为开展调查的依据，避免声称未经证实的原因，采用两项相关检查，并在验证完成前保留人工判断。

Data bias and AI recommendations

Recommendations may reflect bias in training information. Group differences can warrant investigation; an observed pattern alone does not establish which mechanism caused it.

Retained judgment: Identify the most defensible integrated recommendation: one that treats the group difference as grounds for investigation, avoids claiming an unproven cause, uses two relevant checks, and retains human judgment pending verification.

来源 / Source: Video 6.1 ·

某就业指导办公室的 AI 在一个月内推荐了 A 组 68% 的申请者和 B 组 42% 的申请者。它使用了学位专业、年级、自报技能和以往出勤情况；群体标签仅用于审查结果。工作人员既未检查训练信息，也未检查推荐准确性——即推荐结果与经独立审查的研讨会相关性标准的一致程度。哪项建议最站得住脚？

A认定该工具存在歧视；在工作人员开展任何审查之前，移除以往出勤情况并重新训练该工具。
B将这一差异视为一个信号；检查训练信息，并根据相关性标准比较各群体的错误情况，同时暂由工作人员确认推荐结果。
C由于群体标签不是输入，因此将这一差异视为随机现象；继续使用推荐结果，并在明年审查推荐率。
D假定学位专业导致了这一差异；审计该项输入，并在工作人员检查训练信息期间让该工具作出决定。

整题完全匹配计分，不计部分分。

A careers office’s AI recommended 68% of Group A and 42% of Group B applicants in one month. It used degree subject, year, self-reported skills and previous attendance; group labels were only used to review outcomes. Staff have examined neither training information nor recommendation accuracy—agreement with independently reviewed workshop-relevance criteria. Which recommendation is most defensible?

AConclude the tool discriminates; remove previous attendance and retrain it before any staff review.
BTreat the difference as a signal; inspect training information and compare group errors against the criteria, with staff confirming recommendations meanwhile.
CTreat the difference as random because group labels were not inputs; continue using recommendations and review rates next year.
DAssume degree subject caused the difference; audit that input and let the tool decide while staff inspect training information.

Whole-item exact match; no partial credit.

参考答案与解释 / Reference answer and explanation

B (将这一差异视为一个信号；检查训练信息，并根据相关性标准比较各群体的错误情况，同时暂由工作人员确认推荐结果。)

接受 B，因为它将观察到的差异视为开展调查的依据，而没有断言其原因；它提出了两项相关检查，并在验证期间保留工作人员的判断。A 和 D 声称了缺乏依据的原因；A 还在调查前就要求作出更改，而 D 则过度依赖该工具。C 对尚未解决的模式不予理会，并在没有及时验证的情况下继续使用该工具。

B (Treat the difference as a signal; inspect training information and compare group errors against the criteria, with staff confirming recommendations meanwhile.)

B is accepted because it treats the observed difference as grounds for investigation without asserting a cause, proposes two relevant checks, and retains staff judgment during verification. A and D claim unsupported causes; A also prescribes a change before investigation, while D over-relies on the tool. C dismisses the unresolved pattern and continues use without timely verification.

五阶段设计记录 / Five-stage design record

步骤1：表现目标

评价一项对看似有偏见的推荐模式所作的连贯调查，同时不作超出证据的因果推断。

作出一个范围明确的综合判断，将观察到的群体差异、所用信息和有依据的后续行动联系起来，而不是诉诸道德或人口特征刻板印象。

Stage 1: performance claim and observable evidence

Evaluate a coherent investigation of a biased-looking recommendation pattern without inferring a cause beyond the evidence.

One bounded integrated judgment linking observed group differences, the information used and justified follow-up, rather than a moral or demographic stereotype.

步骤2：情境与开放任务

某大学就业指导办公室试用一款 AI 工具，用于推荐学生参加面试研讨会。在一个月内，A 组申请者中有 68% 获得推荐，B 组申请者中有 42% 获得推荐。该工具使用了学位专业、年级、自报技能以及以往参加研讨会的出勤情况。工作人员尚未检查训练信息，也未核查推荐准确性。群体标签仅用于审查结果。

向就业指导经理撰写一份简短建议，说明这一模式支持什么、不足以证明什么，以及在继续使用前应开展的两项具体后续检查。

Stage 2: scenario and open task

A university careers office pilots an AI tool that recommends students for interview workshops. In one month, 68% of applicants from Group A and 42% from Group B were recommended. The tool used degree subject, year, self-reported skills, and previous workshop attendance. Staff have not examined the training information or checked recommendation accuracy. Group labels were used only to review outcomes.

Write a brief recommendation to the careers manager explaining what the pattern supports, what it does not establish, and two concrete follow-up checks before continued use.

GRASPS

目标: 判断一个看似存在偏见的推荐模式，而不声称缺乏依据的原因。

角色: 评估助理

受众: 就业指导经理

情境: 经理必须决定如何调查试用结果。

GRASPS

goal: Judge a biased-looking recommendation pattern without claiming an unsupported cause.

role: Evaluation assistant

audience: Careers manager

situation: The manager must decide how to investigate the pilot results.

开放任务的产物与成功标准

一份简洁且有理有据的建议

  • 准确使用观察到的群体差异
  • 将模式证据与对其原因的结论区分开来
  • 识别需要检查的相关信息
  • 提出两项可行的检查并保留人工判断

Open-task product and success criteria

A concise, justified recommendation

  • Uses the observed group difference accurately
  • Separates evidence of a pattern from conclusions about its cause
  • Identifies relevant information to inspect
  • Proposes two feasible checks and retains human judgment

步骤5：响应设计 / Stage 5: response design

题型理由

单项选择题保留了核心的综合判断，同时允许最强建议中包含不同的合理检查。干扰项可以体现过度断言原因、进行无关检查或过度依赖，而不会使任务变成规则回忆。

未保留的表现

  • 独立提出并表述建议
  • 从零开始独立设计两项检查
  • 在原创的扩展回答中解释判断

Format rationale

Single choice preserves the focal integrated judgment while allowing different legitimate checks within the strongest recommendation. Distractors can reflect overclaiming a cause, irrelevant checking, or over-reliance without turning the task into rule recall.

Performance not retained

  • Independently producing and wording a recommendation
  • Independently generating two checks from scratch
  • Explaining the judgment in an original extended response

MIT软件工程 / MIT Software Construction 10题

SE01SE02SE03SE04SE05SE06SE07SE08SE09SE10
SE01排序 / Ordering

审阅背景，不属于学生题面 / Reviewer context, not part of the student question

测试优先开发的步骤与依赖

先明确函数的输入、输出及约束，再根据规格设计测试，随后编写实现。先写测试有助于及早发现规格中的遗漏和歧义，而不是等代码完成后才开始检查。

本题保留考察：按照依赖关系排列具体情境中的行动，认识到早期测试可以促使规约得到澄清，而有效边界测试失败可以促使开展纠正实现的工作。

Test-first programming and the specification–test–implementation sequence

Specify behaviour, design tests, then implement. Early tests can reveal omissions or ambiguities in the specification.

Retained judgment: Order case-specific actions by dependency, recognizing that an early test can prompt specification clarification and that a failed valid boundary test can prompt corrective implementation work.

来源 / Source: Reading 3: Testing · Test-first Programming

某团队正在开发 `shipping(weight)`。有效重量不超过 2 kg 时费用为 $5；超过 2 kg 时费用为 $8。在任何代码尚未编写之前，提出一个 −1 kg 的测试，暴露出负数输入尚未规定。团队选择要求重量为非负数——这是调用者必须满足的条件——并将使测试与这一澄清保持一致。之后，实现对于恰好 2 kg 返回 $8，导致测试失败。按照开发依赖关系，将每项行动从先到后排序。

C实现 `shipping`，使其满足澄清后的规约和已准备的测试。
A在提出的 −1 kg 测试暴露出遗漏后，规定重量必须为非负数。
D在 2 kg 测试失败后，修正实现中的边界比较并重新运行测试。
B准备反映该澄清的测试，包括 2 kg 预期为 $5，3 kg 预期为 $8。

整题完全匹配计分，不计部分分。 所有明确列出的合法顺序均接受。

A team is developing `shipping(weight)`. Valid weights at or below 2 kg cost $5; weights above 2 kg cost $8. Before any code exists, proposing a −1 kg test reveals that negative inputs are unspecified. The team chooses to require nonnegative weight—a condition callers must meet—and will align its tests with that clarification. Later, the implementation returns $8 for exactly 2 kg, failing a test. Order every action from first to last by development dependency.

CImplement `shipping` to satisfy the clarified specification and the prepared tests.
AAfter the −1 kg proposal reveals the omission, specify that weight must be nonnegative.
DAfter the 2 kg test fails, correct the implementation’s boundary comparison and rerun the tests.
BPrepare tests reflecting the clarification, including 2 kg expecting $5 and 3 kg expecting $8.

Whole-item exact match; no partial credit. All listed legal orders are accepted.

参考答案与解释 / Reference answer and explanation

A → B → C → D

A 解决了提出 −1 kg 测试时暴露出的遗漏。随后，B 反映澄清后的规约并检查所述边界。C 在依据规约的测试准备好之后进行。D 依赖于后来失败的测试；由于 2 kg 是有效值，并且规定其费用为 $5，因此应修正实现中的比较并重新运行测试。

A → B → C → D

A resolves the omission exposed while proposing the −1 kg test. B then reflects that clarified specification and checks the stated boundary. C follows preparation of the specification-based tests. D depends on the later failed test; because 2 kg is valid and specified to cost $5, the implementation’s comparison should be corrected and the tests rerun.

五阶段设计记录 / Five-stage design record

步骤1：表现目标

按照测试优先编程，对说明要求、编写测试、实现以及测试失败后的响应等开发活动排序。

给出以课程流程为依据的依赖顺序。说明案例特定的产物，但不要把课程中的排序规则直接写成提示。不得把目标简化为选择完整项目方案。

Stage 1: performance claim and observable evidence

Order the development activities of specifying, testing, implementing and responding to a failed test according to test-first programming.

A dependency sequence grounded in the course process. State case-specific artifacts without printing the course’s sequencing rule as a hint. Do not reduce the target to choosing a whole project plan.

步骤2：情境与开放任务

一个学生团队必须创建 `shipping(weight)`，该函数在重量不超过 2 kg 时返回 $5，在重量超过 2 kg 时返回 $8。书面规约没有说明是否允许负数重量。在任何代码尚未编写之前，一个使用 −1 kg 的拟议测试暴露出这一遗漏。之后，实现对于恰好 2 kg 返回 $8，导致一个已有测试失败。

作为团队的开发者，为队友写一则简短的工作日志。将案例材料按照合理的开发顺序排列，说明团队应如何处理 −1 kg 这一遗漏，并建议在 2 kg 测试失败后采取的下一步行动。运用课程流程说明每项依赖关系的理由。你可以修改规约或添加明确的输入约束，但测试必须反映该决定。

Stage 2: scenario and open task

A student team must create `shipping(weight)`, which returns $5 when weight is at most 2 kg and $8 when it is above 2 kg. The written specification does not say whether negative weights are allowed. Before any code exists, a proposed test using −1 kg exposes this omission. Later, the implementation returns $8 for exactly 2 kg, causing an existing test to fail.

As the team’s developer, write a brief work-log entry for your teammates. Place the case artifacts in a defensible development sequence, state what the team should do about the −1 kg omission, and recommend the next action after the 2 kg test fails. Justify each dependency using the course process. You may revise the specification or add an explicit input constraint, provided tests reflect that decision.

GRASPS

目标: 规划函数的开发顺序并说明理由。

角色: 开发者

受众: 学生队友

情境: 测试揭示了一个规约遗漏和一个实现失败。

GRASPS

goal: Plan and justify the function’s development sequence.

role: Developer

audience: Student teammates

situation: Testing reveals one specification omission and one implementation failure.

开放任务的产物与成功标准

一则简短的、按顺序排列并附有回应理由的工作日志。

  • 按照依赖关系排列所述材料。
  • 在依赖实现行为之前解决负数重量的歧义。
  • 以符合所述行为的方式响应边界测试失败。
  • 说明为何测试先于并指导实现工作。

Open-task product and success criteria

A brief ordered work-log entry with justified responses.

  • Orders the named artifacts by their dependencies.
  • Resolves the negative-weight ambiguity before relying on implementation behavior.
  • Responds to the boundary-test failure consistently with the stated behavior.
  • Explains why tests precede and guide implementation work.

步骤5：响应设计 / Stage 5: response design

题型理由

排序最能保留真正的依赖关系，并使主要的测试优先知识保持活跃，而不会扩展成整个项目计划。

未保留的表现

  • 独立撰写工作日志条目
  • 独立制定负数重量政策
  • 用文字说明每项依赖关系的理由
  • 分析违反前置条件后的行为

Format rationale

Ordering best preserves the genuine dependency relation and keeps the primary test-first knowledge active without expanding into a whole-project plan.

Performance not retained

  • Independent production of a work-log entry
  • Independent formulation of a negative-weight policy
  • Prose justification of every dependency
  • Analysis of behavior after a violated precondition
SE02匹配 / Matching

审阅背景，不属于学生题面 / Reviewer context, not part of the student question

输入空间划分与代表性测试用例

根据预期行为把输入划分为不同子域，再从各子域选取代表性用例。测试集应覆盖这些类别；大量重复同一类别的输入，不能代替对遗漏类别的检查。

本题保留考察：将所有当前测试映射到所提供的基于行为的子域，然后判断哪个子域被重复覆盖，以及是否遗漏了任何子域。

Input-space partitioning and representative test selection

Partition input space into categories with similar expected behaviour and choose representative tests. Repeated examples from one category do not cover an omitted category.

Retained judgment: Map all current tests to the supplied behaviour-based subdomains, then judge which subdomain is repeatedly represented and whether any subdomain is missing.

来源 / Source: Reading 3: Testing · Choosing Test Cases by Partitioning

一个虚构的程序库方法 `loanFee(days)` 接受从 0 到 30（含）的整数。0 天时返回 $0，1–14 天时返回 $2，15–30 天时返回 $5。子域是指预期表现相似的输入。当前测试为 0、3、8、14 和 20 天。将每一行与其子域及相应的覆盖情况匹配。选项可以重复使用。利用所有映射来判断是否有类别被重复覆盖或遗漏。

备选项

c10 天（费用 $0）；覆盖一次
c21–14 天（费用 $2）；覆盖三次
c315–30 天（费用 $5）；覆盖一次
c4不属于任何有效子域；因此未被覆盖

待匹配内容

r1测试：0 天
r2测试：3 天和 8 天
r3测试：14 天
r4测试：20 天

整题完全匹配计分，不计部分分。 同一选项可重复使用。

A fictional library method `loanFee(days)` accepts whole numbers from 0 through 30. It returns $0 for 0 days, $2 for 1–14 days, and $5 for 15–30 days. A subdomain means inputs expected to behave similarly. Current tests are 0, 3, 8, 14, and 20 days. Match each row to its subdomain and resulting coverage. Choices may be reused. Use all mappings to determine whether a category is repeated or missing.

Choices

c10 days ($0 fee); represented once
c21–14 days ($2 fee); represented three times
c315–30 days ($5 fee); represented once
c4No valid subdomain; therefore missing from coverage

Rows to match

r1Test: 0 days
r2Tests: 3 and 8 days
r3Test: 14 days
r4Test: 20 days

Whole-item exact match; no partial credit. Options may be reused.

参考答案与解释 / Reference answer and explanation

r1 → c1 (0 天（费用 $0）；覆盖一次); r2 → c2 (1–14 天（费用 $2）；覆盖三次); r3 → c2 (1–14 天（费用 $2）；覆盖三次); r4 → c3 (15–30 天（费用 $5）；覆盖一次)

0 天测试属于费用为 $0 的子域。测试 3、8 和 14 属于费用为 $2 的子域，因此该子域被重复覆盖。测试 20 属于费用为 $5 的子域。因此，三个有效输入子域均已被覆盖；费用为 $2 的子域中的重复示例不会形成另一个类别。第四个选项被排除，因为列出的每个测试都有效，且没有遗漏任何子域。

r1 → c1 (0 days ($0 fee); represented once); r2 → c2 (1–14 days ($2 fee); represented three times); r3 → c2 (1–14 days ($2 fee); represented three times); r4 → c3 (15–30 days ($5 fee); represented once)

The 0-day test belongs to the $0 subdomain. Tests 3, 8, and 14 belong to the $2 subdomain, so it is repeatedly represented. Test 20 belongs to the $5 subdomain. Thus all three valid-input subdomains are represented; repeated examples in the $2 subdomain do not create another category. The fourth option is rejected because every listed test is valid and no subdomain is missing.

五阶段设计记录 / Five-stage design record

步骤1：表现目标

确定具体测试如何对应不同的输入子域，并识别重复或缺失的类别。

给出多个“测试—子域”对应关系或明确的覆盖关系，而不是选择一个总体上有吸引力的测试方案。保持划分标准定义清楚。

Stage 1: performance claim and observable evidence

Determine how concrete tests map to distinct input subdomains and identify redundant or missing categories.

Several test-to-subdomain correspondences or an explicit coverage relation, not an overall attractive test-plan choice. Keep the partition criterion well defined.

步骤2：情境与开放任务

一个程序库应用测试 `loanFee(days)`。有效输入是从 0 到 30（含）的整数。0 天时结果为 $0，1–14 天时为 $2，15–30 天时为 $5。当前测试使用 0、3、8、14 和 20 天。

作为测试人员，为开发人员撰写一份简短的覆盖情况备忘录。定义基于行为的输入子域，将每个当前测试映射到一个子域，并指出重复和遗漏的子域。然后推荐一个不超过五个输入的修订测试套件，以覆盖每个子域。说明选择任何边界输入的理由。子域是预期表现相似的一组输入。

Stage 2: scenario and open task

A library app tests `loanFee(days)`. Valid inputs are whole numbers from 0 to 30. The result is $0 for 0 days, $2 for 1–14 days, and $5 for 15–30 days. The current tests use 0, 3, 8, 14, and 20 days.

As the tester, write a brief coverage memo for the developer. Define behaviour-based input subdomains, map every current test to one, and identify repeated and missing subdomains. Then recommend a revised suite of no more than five inputs that represents every subdomain. Justify any boundary inputs you select. A subdomain is a set of inputs expected to behave similarly.

GRASPS

目标: 评估并改进有效输入空间的代表性覆盖。

角色: 软件测试人员

受众: 应用开发人员

情境: 发布前必须检查一个现有的小型测试套件。

GRASPS

goal: Evaluate and improve representative coverage of the valid input space.

role: Software tester

audience: App developer

situation: A small existing suite must be checked before release.

开放任务的产物与成功标准

一份简明的覆盖情况备忘录和修订后的测试套件

  • 定义覆盖所有有效输入的子域
  • 明确映射每个当前测试
  • 识别冗余和遗漏
  • 推荐覆盖每个已定义子域的代表值
  • 解释边界选择

Open-task product and success criteria

A concise coverage memo and revised test suite

  • Defines subdomains that cover all valid inputs
  • Maps each current test explicitly
  • Identifies redundancy and omissions
  • Recommends representatives covering every defined subdomain
  • Explains boundary selections

步骤5：响应设计 / Stage 5: response design

题型理由

匹配形式最能保留核心对应关系，同时使入门任务保持简洁。将 3 和 8 归入同一行既符合行数限制，又不会掩盖它们的共同分类。附加一项简短选择可以体现由此得出的冗余和遗漏判断。

未保留的表现

  • 独立生成一种划分
  • 独立生成修订后的代表性测试套件
  • 选择侧重边界的测试并说明理由

Format rationale

Matching best preserves the focal correspondence while keeping the introductory task concise. Grouping 3 and 8 in one row fits the row limit without hiding their shared classification. A brief accompanying selection can capture the resulting redundancy and omission judgment.

Performance not retained

  • Independently producing a partition
  • Independently producing a revised representative suite
  • Selecting and justifying boundary-focused tests
SE03单选 / Single choice

审阅背景，不属于学生题面 / Reviewer context, not part of the student question

边界条件与特殊输入的测试

程序容易在类别交界或特殊位置出错，例如零值、空集合、首尾元素和数值上限。设计测试时，应把这些边界作为明确的子域，而不只选择普通的中间值。

本题保留考察：选择一项精简修订：它能够有理有据地用相关边界子域扩展当前测试套件，为有效输入给出规范支持的结果，并且不对空数组作出无依据的承诺。

Boundary cases as explicit testing subdomains

Include boundary subdomains such as empty collections, zero, numeric extremes and first/last positions, where behaviour may change abruptly.

Retained judgment: Choose the compact revision that defensibly expands the current suite with relevant boundary subdomains, gives specification-supported results for valid inputs, and makes no unsupported promise for an empty array.

来源 / Source: Reading 3: Testing · Include Boundaries in the Partition

子域是指预期表现相似的一类输入。方法 latest(int[] years) 要求数组非空，并返回其中的最大值。当前测试为 [1990,2020]→2020、[2020,1990]→2020 和 [2000,2000]→2000。元素可以包括 0、Integer.MIN_VALUE 和 Integer.MAX_VALUE。以下哪项精简修订最恰当地补充了不同的遗漏边界覆盖，并给出了有依据的预期？

A添加 [1980, 2020]→2020 和 [2020, 1980]→2020；对于 []，预期为 0。
B添加 [0]→0 和 [Integer.MIN_VALUE, Integer.MAX_VALUE]→Integer.MAX_VALUE；[] 不符合非空要求，因此不承诺任何结果。
C添加 [0]→0 和 [Integer.MIN_VALUE, Integer.MAX_VALUE]→Integer.MIN_VALUE；[] 不符合非空要求，因此不承诺任何结果。
D添加 [0]→0、[Integer.MAX_VALUE]→Integer.MAX_VALUE 和 []→抛出异常。

整题完全匹配计分，不计部分分。

A subdomain is a category of inputs expected to behave similarly. Method latest(int[] years) requires a nonempty array and returns its greatest value. Current tests are [1990,2020]→2020, [2020,1990]→2020, and [2000,2000]→2000. Elements may include 0, Integer.MIN_VALUE, and Integer.MAX_VALUE. Which compact revision best adds distinct omitted boundary coverage with supported expectations?

AAdd [1980, 2020]→2020 and [2020, 1980]→2020; for [] expect 0.
BAdd [0]→0 and [Integer.MIN_VALUE, Integer.MAX_VALUE]→Integer.MAX_VALUE; [] is outside the nonempty requirement, so no result is promised.
CAdd [0]→0 and [Integer.MIN_VALUE, Integer.MAX_VALUE]→Integer.MIN_VALUE; [] is outside the nonempty requirement, so no result is promised.
DAdd [0]→0, [Integer.MAX_VALUE]→Integer.MAX_VALUE, and []→an exception.

Whole-item exact match; no partial credit.

参考答案与解释 / Reference answer and explanation

B (添加 [0]→0 和 [Integer.MIN_VALUE, Integer.MAX_VALUE]→Integer.MAX_VALUE；[] 不符合非空要求，因此不承诺任何结果。)

B 添加了有效测试，覆盖零和两个整数极值，并给出了正确的最大值预期。它还避免对无效的空输入承诺行为。A 重复了普通的顺序覆盖，并杜撰了空输入的结果。C 对最大值的预期不正确。D 杜撰了空数组会抛出未规定的异常，并遗漏了最小整数边界。

B (Add [0]→0 and [Integer.MIN_VALUE, Integer.MAX_VALUE]→Integer.MAX_VALUE; [] is outside the nonempty requirement, so no result is promised.)

B adds valid tests covering zero and both integer extremes, with correct greatest-value expectations. It also avoids promising behavior for the invalid empty input. A repeats ordinary ordering coverage and invents an empty-input result. C has an incorrect expected greatest value. D invents an unspecified exception for the empty array and omits the minimum integer boundary.

五阶段设计记录 / Five-stage design record

步骤1：表现目标

评价一项简洁的修订，该修订补充了相关但遗漏的边界情况，并给出正确的预期结果。

对覆盖范围和预期结果作出一个综合比较；提供普通案例事实，但边界测试的支配规则仍应属于已学知识。

Stage 1: performance claim and observable evidence

Evaluate a compact revision that adds relevant omitted boundary cases with correct expected results.

One integrated comparison of coverage and expectations; ordinary case facts are supplied, but the governing boundary-testing rule remains learned knowledge.

步骤2：情境与开放任务

库方法 latest(int[] years) 要求数组非空，并返回其中的最大值。当前测试为 [1990, 2020]→2020、[2020, 1990]→2020 和 [2000, 2000]→2000。数组可以包含 0、Integer.MIN_VALUE 或 Integer.MAX_VALUE。子域是指预期表现相似的一类输入。

作为测试评审人员，请为开发人员编写一份精简修订。将当前测试套件与相关边界子域进行比较，然后添加不超过三个测试。对于每项新增测试，请给出输入、预期结果和所覆盖的边界。说明你的修订为何改善了覆盖。明确处理空数组，但不要假定规范未承诺的行为。

Stage 2: scenario and open task

A library method latest(int[] years) requires a nonempty array and returns its greatest value. The current tests are [1990, 2020]→2020, [2020, 1990]→2020, and [2000, 2000]→2000. Arrays may contain 0, Integer.MIN_VALUE, or Integer.MAX_VALUE. A subdomain is a category of inputs expected to behave similarly.

As a test reviewer, write a compact revision for the developer. Compare the current suite with relevant boundary subdomains, then add no more than three tests. For each addition, give the input, expected result, and boundary covered. Explain why your revision improves coverage. Address the empty array explicitly without assuming behavior not promised by the specification.

GRASPS

目标: 修订一个精简测试套件，以覆盖遗漏的相关边界，并给出有依据的预期。

角色: 测试评审人员

受众: 开发人员

情境: 现有测试套件检查了顺序和相等情况，但可能遗漏重要的边界子域。

GRASPS

goal: Revise a compact test suite to cover omitted relevant boundaries with justified expectations.

role: Test reviewer

audience: Developer

situation: The existing suite checks order and equality but may omit important boundary subdomains.

开放任务的产物与成功标准

一份比较以及添加不超过三个测试的修订套件

  • 识别相关的遗漏边界子域。
  • 为新增的有效输入提供正确的预期结果。
  • 将每项新增测试与一个明确说明的边界相关联。
  • 以符合所述要求的方式处理空数组。
  • 说明该修订如何添加不同的覆盖，而不是重复一个普通类别。

Open-task product and success criteria

A comparison and revised suite with up to three added tests

  • Identifies relevant omitted boundary subdomains.
  • Provides correct expected results for valid added inputs.
  • Links every addition to a stated boundary.
  • Treats the empty array consistently with the stated requirement.
  • Explains how the revision adds distinct coverage rather than repeating an ordinary category.

O2
dependencies: K2, K3, F1, F3, C1, O1
source: open_task		cognitive_operation
给出最多三个有效的新增测试，每个都包含输入、最大值预期和已识别的边界。
Produce up to three valid test additions, each with an input, greatest-value expectation, and identified boundary.
排除 / exclude
已提供完整修订，因此学习者不再需要独立编写测试。
Complete revisions are supplied, so learners no longer produce tests independently.
O3
dependencies: K3, K4, F1, O2
source: open_task		cognitive_operation
评估每个提议的预期是否源于方法规范，以及新增测试是否提供不同的边界覆盖。
Evaluate whether each proposed expectation follows from the method specification and whether additions provide distinct boundary coverage.
保留 / retain
学习者评估每项给定修订中的预期结果和不同的边界覆盖。
Learners evaluate expected results and distinct boundary coverage in each supplied revision.

dependencies: K6, F1
source: open_task		cognitive_operation
将空数组归类为不符合所述输入条件，而不为其指定承诺的结果。
Classify the empty array as outside the stated input condition without assigning it a promised outcome.

排除 / exclude
