A separate learner-written justification is removed for concision.

步骤5：响应设计 / Stage 5: response design

题型理由

单项选择适合保留的有界决策。恰好有一个候选项能够满足所有标准，同时原则上仍允许组合边界或单独测试边界等合法测试设计方法。保留的评估操作 O3 使从编写到评估的转换具有合理依据。

未保留的表现

  • 独立编写测试输入、预期结果和边界标签。
  • 由学习者撰写对覆盖改进的说明。
  • 直接展示多个合法的编写方案。

Format rationale

Single choice fits the retained bounded decision. Exactly one candidate can satisfy all criteria while allowing legitimate test-design approaches, such as combining or isolating boundaries, to remain valid in principle. The retained evaluation operation O3 makes the production-to-evaluation conversion defensible.

Performance not retained

  • Independent production of test inputs, expected results, and boundary labels.
  • A learner-authored explanation of the coverage improvement.
  • Direct demonstration of multiple legitimate production solutions.
SE04匹配 / Matching

审阅背景，不属于学生题面 / Reviewer context, not part of the student question

黑盒与白盒测试的用例选择依据

黑盒测试根据规格选择用例；白盒测试利用实现结构补充用例，例如覆盖尚未执行的分支。两者对预期结果的判断都必须遵守规格，不能把某个实现的偶然行为当成要求。

本题保留考察：对于给出的每项测试决策，判断其选择是仅使用规格说明，还是也使用了实现结构，并判断其预期检查是否仍然符合契约。

Blackbox and whitebox test selection

Blackbox test selection uses the specification; whitebox selection also uses implementation structure. Expected behaviour must still be justified by the specification.

Retained judgment: For each supplied testing decision, determine whether selection uses only the specification or also implementation structure, and whether its expected check remains contract-conforming.

来源 / Source: Reading 3: Testing · Blackbox and Whitebox Testing

find(arr, val) 的契约要求 val 在 arr 中，并保证存在一个索引 i，使得 arr[i] = val。该实现对长度超过 20 的数组设有一个特殊分支，并会缓存重复调用（在内部复用结果）。将每项提议与其选择依据及检查质量相匹配。选项可重复使用。

备选项

C1仅依据规格说明进行选择；符合契约的检查
C2仅依据规格说明进行选择；不符合契约的检查
C3依据实现信息进行选择；符合契约的检查
C4依据实现信息进行选择；不符合契约的检查

待匹配内容

R1测试 find([4,9],9)；检查 arr[i]=9。
R2为执行长数组分支，使用包含 21 个元素且其中有两个 9 的数组；检查 arr[i]=9。
R3为执行缓存，重复调用 find([9,9],9)；要求第二次调用返回第一次调用的索引。
R4仅根据契约，测试 find([9,9],9)；要求索引为 0。

整题完全匹配计分，不计部分分。 同一选项可重复使用。

The contract for find(arr, val) requires val in arr and guarantees an index i with arr[i] = val. The implementation has a special branch for arrays longer than 20 and caches repeated calls (internally reuses a result). Match each proposal to its selection basis and check quality. Choices may be reused.

Choices

C1Specification-only selection; contract-conforming check
C2Specification-only selection; nonconforming check
C3Implementation-informed selection; contract-conforming check
C4Implementation-informed selection; nonconforming check

Rows to match

R1Test find([4,9],9); check arr[i]=9.
R2To exercise the long-array branch, use 21 elements with 9 twice; check arr[i]=9.
R3To exercise the cache, repeat find([9,9],9); require the second call to return the first call’s index.
R4From the contract alone, test find([9,9],9); require index 0.

Whole-item exact match; no partial credit. Options may be reused.

参考答案与解释 / Reference answer and explanation

R1 → C1 (仅依据规格说明进行选择；符合契约的检查); R2 → C3 (依据实现信息进行选择；符合契约的检查); R3 → C4 (依据实现信息进行选择；不符合契约的检查); R4 → C2 (仅依据规格说明进行选择；不符合契约的检查)

R1 仅使用契约信息，并检查所保证的关系。R2 针对一个实现分支，但接受任一匹配的索引。R3 针对缓存，但错误地要求返回第一次调用的结果，因为两个重复值所在的任一索引都是允许的。R4 未使用任何实现事实，但契约并不保证其预期索引。

R1 → C1 (Specification-only selection; contract-conforming check); R2 → C3 (Implementation-informed selection; contract-conforming check); R3 → C4 (Implementation-informed selection; nonconforming check); R4 → C2 (Specification-only selection; nonconforming check)

R1 uses only contract information and checks the guaranteed relationship. R2 targets an implementation branch but accepts either matching index. R3 targets the cache but incorrectly requires the first result when either duplicate index is permitted. R4 uses no implementation fact, but its expected index is not guaranteed.

五阶段设计记录 / Five-stage design record

步骤1：表现目标

区分哪些拟议测试决定基于规格说明，哪些基于实现结构，并判断预期结果是否仍符合契约。

给出多个“策略—测试依据”对应关系。认识到即使测试是根据实现知识选择的，其预期结果仍须有规格说明依据。

Stage 1: performance claim and observable evidence

Distinguish which proposed testing decisions are based on specifications versus implementation structure, and whether expectations remain contract-conforming.

Several strategy-to-testing-basis correspondences. Recognize that a test chosen from implementation knowledge must still use specification-justified expectations.

步骤2：情境与开放任务

某团队测试 find(arr, val)。其契约要求 val 出现在 arr 中，并保证返回一个索引 i，使得 arr[i] 等于 val。代码对长度超过 20 的数组使用一个特殊分支，并缓存重复调用。

作为测试评审员，请为开发人员写一份简短建议。提出三个合法测试：一个仅依据契约得出，一个执行长数组分支，一个执行缓存。对每个测试，说明输入、预期检查，以及其选择属于黑盒还是白盒。解释当 val 出现多次时，为什么每项检查仍然符合契约。

Stage 2: scenario and open task

A team tests find(arr, val). Its contract requires val to occur in arr and guarantees an index i where arr[i] equals val. The code uses a special branch for arrays longer than 20 and caches repeated calls.

As the test reviewer, write a brief recommendation to the developers. Propose three legal tests: one derived only from the contract, one exercising the long-array branch, and one exercising the cache. For each, state the input, expected check, and whether its selection is blackbox or whitebox. Explain why every check remains contract-conforming when val occurs more than once.

GRASPS

目标: 通过区分基于规格说明的选择与依据实现信息的选择来设计测试并论证其合理性。

角色: 测试评审员

受众: 开发团队

情境: 团队需要既覆盖规定行为和实现结构、又不要求未规定结果的测试。

GRASPS

goal: Design and justify tests by separating specification-based selection from implementation-informed selection.

role: Test reviewer

audience: Development team

situation: The team needs tests that cover stated behavior and implementation structures without demanding an unspecified result.

开放任务的产物与成功标准

一份包含三个测试的简明建议

  • 所有调用都使用包含 val 的数组。
  • 每个测试都说明其选择依据。
  • 白盒测试执行所述分支或缓存。
  • 每项检查都接受其元素等于 val 的任一返回索引。

Open-task product and success criteria

A concise three-test recommendation

  • All calls use arrays containing val.
  • Each test identifies its selection basis.
  • The whitebox tests exercise the stated branch or cache.
  • Each check accepts any returned index whose element equals val.

步骤5：响应设计 / Stage 5: response design

题型理由

匹配题直接评估 SE04 的核心区别，同时将 SE08 保留为对预期检查的约束。四行可以映射到四个可重复使用的组合类别，而无需规定唯一的测试设计或披露目标映射。

未保留的表现

  • 独立设计测试输入和断言
  • 为全部三种目的独立构造测试
  • 对每项预期检查进行书面论证

Format rationale

Matching directly assesses the focal SE04 distinction while keeping SE08 as the constraint on expected checks. Four rows can map to four reusable combined categories without requiring a single prescribed test design or disclosing the target mapping.

Performance not retained

  • Independent production of test inputs and assertions
  • Independent construction of tests for all three purposes
  • Written defense of each expected check
SE05匹配 / Matching

审阅背景，不属于学生题面 / Reviewer context, not part of the student question

单元测试与集成测试的职责和隔离

单元测试尽量隔离单个模块，检查其自身契约；集成测试检查模块组合时能否正确协作。只检查组合结果可能遗漏模块的其他合法输入，也可能让故障来源难以定位。

本题保留考察：将所提供的合法测试设计和故障定位解释匹配到独立的 parseTitle 测试、importRecord–parseTitle 协作或两者都不是。

Unit testing, integration testing and isolation of dependencies

Unit tests isolate a module and its contract; integration tests check cooperation. Testing only through a combined system can miss module inputs and obscure fault location.

Retained judgment: Match supplied legal test designs and fault-localisation interpretations to isolated parseTitle testing, importRecord–parseTitle cooperation, or neither.

来源 / Source: Reading 3: Testing · Unit Testing and Stubs

将每一行与一个选项匹配；选项可重复使用。parseTitle(text) 要求文本中有冒号，并返回第一个冒号之前的精确子字符串。importRecord(path) 要求路径指向一个可读的文本文件，并返回对该文件完整内容应用 parseTitle 的结果。一个完整内容为“Cooking: Autumn”的文件被错误地导入为“Gardening”；两个模块都没有单独的测试。

备选项

iso独立的 parseTitle 测试或其定位含义
coop协作测试或其定位含义
neither两者都不是

待匹配内容

r1调用 parseTitle("Cooking: Autumn")；预期为“Cooking”。失败主要表明问题与 parseTitle 有关。
r2导入一个可读文件，其完整内容恰好为“Cooking: Autumn”；预期为“Cooking”。
r3导入失败可能源于读取、解析或二者之间的连接。
r4调用 parseTitle("Cooking: Autumn")；要求输出为小写。

整题完全匹配计分，不计部分分。 同一选项可重复使用。

Match each row to one option; options may be reused. parseTitle(text) requires a colon and returns the exact substring before the first colon. importRecord(path) requires a readable text file and returns parseTitle applied to its complete contents. A file whose complete contents are “Cooking: Autumn” incorrectly imports as “Gardening”; neither module has separate tests.

Choices

isoIsolated parseTitle test or its localisation implication
coopCooperation test or its localisation implication
neitherNeither

Rows to match

r1Call parseTitle("Cooking: Autumn"); expect “Cooking”. Failure chiefly implicates parseTitle.
r2Import a readable file whose complete contents are exactly “Cooking: Autumn”; expect “Cooking”.
r3A failed import may arise in reading, parsing, or their connection.
r4Call parseTitle("Cooking: Autumn"); require lowercase output.

Whole-item exact match; no partial credit. Options may be reused.

参考答案与解释 / Reference answer and explanation

r1 → iso (独立的 parseTitle 测试或其定位含义); r2 → coop (协作测试或其定位含义); r3 → coop (协作测试或其定位含义); r4 → neither (两者都不是)

第 1 行直接提供合法的固定文本，从而隔离 parseTitle；失败主要表明问题与该模块有关。第 2 行使用一个完整内容准确已知的可读文件，同时执行导入和解析。第 3 行正确描述了该协作测试在诊断上的不确定性。第 4 行要求输出为小写，而契约并未保证这一点。

r1 → iso (Isolated parseTitle test or its localisation implication); r2 → coop (Cooperation test or its localisation implication); r3 → coop (Cooperation test or its localisation implication); r4 → neither (Neither)

Row 1 directly supplies legal fixed text, isolating parseTitle; failure chiefly implicates that module. Row 2 uses a readable file with exact known contents and exercises importing and parsing together. Row 3 correctly describes the diagnostic ambiguity of that cooperation test. Row 4 requires lowercase output, which the contract does not guarantee.

五阶段设计记录 / Five-stage design record

步骤1：表现目标

将多个测试设计或故障定位目的分别与单元隔离测试或模块集成测试匹配。

给出多个“测试目的—测试类型”对应关系，包括解释为何仅通过集成系统测试可能掩盖模块缺陷；不得改为选择一个首选的完整测试方案。

Stage 1: performance claim and observable evidence

Match several test designs or fault-localisation purposes to unit isolation versus module integration.

Several test-purpose correspondences, including why testing only through an integrated system can obscure a module defect; not one preferred complete testing plan.

步骤2：情境与开放任务

一个图书馆应用规定 parseTitle(text)：文本必须包含冒号；它返回第一个冒号之前的精确子字符串。importRecord(path)：路径必须指向一个可读的文本文件；它返回对该文件完整内容应用 parseTitle 的结果。导入包含 "Gardening: Spring" 的文件时，按预期返回 "Gardening"，但导入 "Cooking: Autumn" 时却错误地返回 "Gardening"。两个模块都没有单独的测试。

作为开发人员，为团队编写一份简明的测试建议。给出一个独立的 parseTitle 测试、一个协作测试，以及一个有理据的缺陷定位第一步。对于每项测试，请说明输入、承诺的结果、目的，以及失败会揭示什么或留下哪些不确定性。

Stage 2: scenario and open task

A library app specifies parseTitle(text): text must contain a colon; it returns the exact substring before the first colon. importRecord(path): path must name a readable text file; it returns parseTitle applied to that file’s complete contents. The test importing a file containing "Gardening: Spring" returns "Gardening" as expected, but importing "Cooking: Autumn" incorrectly returns "Gardening". Neither module has separate tests.

As a developer, write a concise test recommendation for the team. Give one isolated parseTitle test, one cooperation test, and a justified first step for locating the defect. For each test, state the input, promised result, purpose, and what a failure would reveal or leave uncertain.

GRASPS

目标: 区分独立模块测试与模块协作测试，并说明它们在故障定位方面的价值。

角色: 开发人员

受众: 开发团队

情境: 一个组合测试失败，而参与其中的模块尚未分别接受测试。

GRASPS

goal: Distinguish isolated module testing from testing module cooperation and justify their fault-localisation value.

role: Developer

audience: Development team

situation: A combined test fails, and the participating modules have not been tested separately.

开放任务的产物与成功标准

一份简明且有理据的测试建议

  • 使用包含冒号的固定字面量直接测试 parseTitle。
  • 使用包含已知文本的可读文件测试 importRecord。
  • 仅检查所述契约承诺的精确结果。
  • 解释为什么独立测试失败会缩小故障位置范围，而组合测试失败仍存在不确定性。
  • 说明一个实用的第一步及其理由，但不声称它是唯一有效的顺序。

Open-task product and success criteria

A concise, justified test recommendation

  • Tests parseTitle directly with a fixed literal containing a colon.
  • Tests importRecord with a readable file containing known text.
  • Checks only the exact results promised by the stated contracts.
  • Explains why an isolated failure narrows fault location while a combined failure remains ambiguous.
  • Justifies a practical first step without claiming it is the only valid sequence.

步骤5：响应设计 / Stage 5: response design

题型理由

匹配形式保留了核心关系，同时不会强加唯一的测试方案。四行可以涵盖直接隔离、协作、诊断不确定性，以及一个不符合契约或过度断言的情况。

未保留的表现

  • 独立设计一个 parseTitle 隔离测试。
  • 独立设计一个协作测试。
  • 选择并论证故障定位的第一步。
  • 构建一份完整的测试建议。

Format rationale

Matching preserves the focal relationship without imposing a unique testing plan. Four rows can sample direct isolation, cooperation, diagnostic ambiguity, and a contract-invalid or overclaiming case.

Performance not retained

  • Independent production of an isolated parseTitle test.
  • Independent production of a cooperation test.
  • Selection and justification of a first localisation step.
  • Construction of a complete testing recommendation.
SE06判断题组 / Judgment matrix

审阅背景，不属于学生题面 / Reviewer context, not part of the student question

前置条件、后置条件与双方责任

前置条件由调用方在调用前满足；条件成立时，实现方必须兑现后置条件。若输入违反前置条件，仅凭这份规格便不能要求某个特定返回值或异常行为。

本题保留考察：对于调用 A 和 C，分别判断调用方是否满足了所述调用条件，以及观察到的行为是否违反了契约下适用的承诺。

Preconditions, postconditions and client–implementer obligations

The client must satisfy preconditions. When they hold, the implementation must satisfy postconditions. A violated precondition gives no promised particular result unless specified separately.

Retained judgment: For calls A and C, separately judge whether the caller satisfied the stated invocation condition and whether the observed behavior breaches a promise applicable under the contract.

来源 / Source: Reading 6, Part 1: Specifications · Specification structure

将每个命题标记为真或假。契约：`locate(shelf,title)` 要求 `title` 出现在 `shelf` 中；效果：返回一个索引 `i`，其中 `shelf[i]` 等于 `title`。索引从零开始，采用精确相等，并且不承诺任何其他行为。“调用方”是指进行调用的代码；“违约”是指未能给出适用承诺所要求的结果。A：`locate(["Dune","Dune"],"Dune")` 返回 1。B：同一调用返回 0。C：`locate(["Dune"],"Emma")` 抛出异常。

P1在 A 中，调用方满足了所述要求。
P2在 A 中，返回 1 违反了适用的承诺。
P3在 C 中，调用方满足了所述要求。
P4在 C 中，抛出异常违反了适用的承诺。

整题完全匹配计分，不计部分分。

Mark each proposition true or false. Contract: `locate(shelf,title)` requires `title` to occur in `shelf`; effects: returns an index `i` where `shelf[i]` equals `title`. Indices are zero-based, equality is exact, and no other behavior is promised. “Caller” means code making the call; “breach” means failure to deliver an applicable promised result. A: `locate(["Dune","Dune"],"Dune")` returns 1. B: the same call returns 0. C: `locate(["Dune"],"Emma")` throws an exception.

P1In A, the caller satisfied the stated requirement.
P2In A, returning 1 breaches an applicable promise.
P3In C, the caller satisfied the stated requirement.
P4In C, throwing the exception breaches an applicable promise.

Whole-item exact match; no partial credit.

参考答案与解释 / Reference answer and explanation

P1: 成立; P2: 不成立; P3: 不成立; P4: 不成立

在 A 中，Dune 确实出现，并且索引 1 处包含 Dune，因此满足了要求，而且该返回值没有违反适用的承诺。在 C 中，Emma 没有出现，因此未满足要求；所以，所述结果承诺不适用，该异常并未违反任何适用的承诺。

P1: Supported; P2: Not supported; P3: Not supported; P4: Not supported

In A, Dune occurs and index 1 contains Dune, so the requirement is satisfied and the return does not breach the applicable promise. In C, Emma does not occur, so the requirement is not satisfied; therefore, the stated result promise is not applicable and the exception breaches no applicable promise.

五阶段设计记录 / Five-stage design record

步骤1：表现目标

判断在给定契约下，多个具体调用分别产生哪些客户端义务和实现义务。

分别对合法输入和承诺结果作出范围明确的判断。不要在题干中给出课程中的“前置条件蕴含后置条件”规则。

Stage 1: performance claim and observable evidence

Judge what client and implementation obligations follow in several concrete calls under a stated contract.

Parallel bounded judgments about legal inputs and promised outcomes. Keep the precondition-implies-postcondition course rule out of the stem.

步骤2：情境与开放任务

某程序库使用 locate(String[] shelf, String title)。其契约规定：要求 title 出现在 shelf 中；效果是返回一个索引 i，使得 shelf[i] 等于 title。前置条件是调用方必须满足的条件；后置条件描述所承诺的完成结果。调用如下：A：locate(["Dune","Dune"],"Dune") 返回 1；B：locate(["Dune","Dune"],"Dune") 返回 0；C：locate(["Dune"],"Emma") 抛出异常。

作为代码审查员，请向测试团队写一份简短建议。对于每个调用，说明调用方是否履行了其义务，以及观察到的行为是否违反了任何承诺。然后提出一个符合契约的重复标题测试，包括其输入和断言。根据所述契约论证每项判断。

Stage 2: scenario and open task

A library uses locate(String[] shelf, String title). Its contract says: requires title occurs in shelf; effects returns an index i such that shelf[i] equals title. A precondition is a condition the caller must establish; a postcondition describes the promised completed result. Calls are: A: locate(["Dune","Dune"],"Dune") returns 1; B: locate(["Dune","Dune"],"Dune") returns 0; C: locate(["Dune"],"Emma") throws an exception.

As a code reviewer, write a brief recommendation to the testing team. For each call, state whether the caller met its obligation and whether the observed behavior violates any promise. Then propose one contract-conforming test for duplicate titles, including its input and assertion. Justify each judgment from the stated contract.

GRASPS

目标: 确定具体调用中调用方和实现的义务，并设计一个符合契约的测试。

角色: 代码审查员

受众: 测试团队

情境: 团队正在确定契约支持对哪些观察到的调用和测试断言作出判断。

GRASPS

goal: Determine caller and implementation obligations for concrete calls and design a conforming test.

role: Code reviewer

audience: Testing team

situation: The team is deciding which observed calls and test assertions the contract supports.

开放任务的产物与成功标准

一份简短审查，包括三个并列判断和一个建议的测试。

  • 分别说明 A、B 和 C 中调用方的义务和所承诺的结果。
  • 仅使用契约中明确陈述的事实。
  • 接受契约允许的每一个重复标题索引。
  • 不对超出所述要求范围的输入声称某个特定结果。

Open-task product and success criteria

A brief review with three parallel judgments and one proposed test.

  • Addresses caller obligation and promised outcome separately for A, B, and C.
  • Uses only explicit contract facts.
  • Accepts every duplicate-title index permitted by the contract.
  • Does not claim a particular result for an input outside the stated requirement.

dependencies: O1, O2, O3
source: open_task		cognitive_operation
仅使用所述要求和承诺结果来解释每项判断及测试断言。
Explain each judgment and the test assertion using only the stated requirement and promised result.
排除 / exclude
移除了独立书面论证；仍可观察到相互分离且范围明确的判断。
Independent written justification is removed; separate bounded judgments remain observable.

步骤5：响应设计 / Stage 5: response design

题型理由

四命题真/假矩阵符合绝对数量限制，同时保留了对一个满足条件的调用和一个不满足条件的调用分别作出判断。调用 B 仍作为存在不止一个重复索引的情境证据，但不会被独立评估。

未保留的表现

  • 独立制作符合规约的重复标题测试
  • 应用 SE08 测试知识
  • 独立书面论证判断
  • 独立判断调用 B 中调用方的义务和是否违约

Format rationale

A four-proposition true/false matrix fits the absolute limit while preserving separate judgments for one satisfied-condition call and one unsatisfied-condition call. Call B remains contextual evidence that more than one duplicate index exists, but it is not independently assessed.

Performance not retained

  • Independent production of a specification-conforming duplicate-title test
  • Application of SE08 testing knowledge
  • Independent written justification of judgments
  • Independent caller-obligation and breach judgments for call B
SE07单选 / Single choice

审阅背景，不属于学生题面 / Reviewer context, not part of the student question

规格约束下的行为等价

两个实现即使代码或部分实际行为不同，也可能满足同一规格。判断能否替换，应看合法输入下的行为是否符合调用方依赖的契约，而不是要求所有输入上的行为都完全相同。

本题保留考察：评估所提供的哪项建议正确判断了版本 B 是否保留仪表板可以依赖的行为，同时将结论限定于所述遵守契约的客户端。

Behavioral equivalence relative to a specification

Implementations may differ yet be substitutable for clients respecting the same contract. Do not equate shared contract compliance with identical behaviour outside that reliance.

Retained judgment: Evaluate which supplied recommendation correctly determines whether Version B preserves the behavior the dashboard may rely on, while limiting the conclusion to the stated contract-respecting client.

来源 / Source: Reading 6, Part 1: Specifications · Behavioral equivalence

locate(codes, key) 的契约要求 key 恰好出现一次，并承诺返回其索引。当 key 不存在时，版本 A 返回 codes.length；版本 B 返回 -1。当满足要求时，两者都返回唯一的索引。某个仪表板——即客户端，也就是调用程序——会在每次调用前验证 key 恰好出现一次。哪项建议是可辩护的？

A批准此仪表板使用 B：经过验证的调用满足要求，而且两个版本都返回所承诺的索引。对于其他客户端，不存在 key 时的差异可能仍然很重要。
B拒绝 B，因为不存在 key 时的不同结果使两个版本不可替换，尽管仪表板从不进行不存在 key 的调用，而且两者都满足对它所承诺的结果。
C批准所有客户端使用 B，因为两个版本都满足此契约，这使它们即使对于不满足要求的输入也实际上完全相同。
D推迟批准，因为契约未承诺不存在 key 时的结果，所以即使对于仪表板经过验证的调用，也无法判断是否可替换。

整题完全匹配计分，不计部分分。

The contract for locate(codes, key) requires key to occur exactly once and promises its index. Version A returns codes.length when key is absent; Version B returns -1. Both return the unique index when the requirement holds. A dashboard—the client, meaning the calling program—verifies exactly one occurrence before every call. Which recommendation is defensible?

AApprove B for this dashboard: verified calls meet the requirement, and both versions return the promised index. The absent-key difference may still matter to other clients.
BReject B because differing absent-key results make the versions non-substitutable, although the dashboard never makes absent-key calls and both satisfy its promised result.
CApprove B for all clients because both versions satisfy this contract, making them effectively identical even for inputs that do not meet the requirement.
DDefer approval because no absent-key result is promised, so substitution cannot be judged even for the dashboard’s verified calls.

Whole-item exact match; no partial credit.

参考答案与解释 / Reference answer and explanation

A (批准此仪表板使用 B：经过验证的调用满足要求，而且两个版本都返回所承诺的索引。对于其他客户端，不存在 key 时的差异可能仍然很重要。)

A 是正确的，因为仪表板满足要求，而且两个版本都在其调用中返回所承诺的结果。不存在 key 时的差异不在这些调用的范围内，但对其他客户端可能很重要。B 错误地将任何差异都视为不合格。C 过度推广到了所有客户端和输入。D 忽视了仪表板经过验证的调用所提供的证据。

A (Approve B for this dashboard: verified calls meet the requirement, and both versions return the promised index. The absent-key difference may still matter to other clients.)

A is correct because the dashboard satisfies the requirement and both versions return the promised result on its calls. The absent-key difference is outside those calls and may matter to other clients. B incorrectly treats any difference as disqualifying. C overgeneralizes to all clients and inputs. D ignores the evidence for the dashboard’s verified calls.

五阶段设计记录 / Five-stage design record

步骤1：表现目标

确定实现变更是否保留了客户端有权依赖的行为。

根据明确的虚构契约作出一个综合的可替代性判断；不得声称所有满足宽泛契约的实现都具有相同行为。

Stage 1: performance claim and observable evidence

Determine whether an implementation change preserves the behavior a client is entitled to rely on.

One integrated substitutability judgment under an explicit fictional contract; do not claim every implementation satisfying a broad contract has identical behavior.

步骤2：情境与开放任务

库方法 locate(codes, key) 要求 key 在 codes 中恰好出现一次，并承诺返回其索引。版本 A 从开头搜索，并在 key 不存在时返回 codes.length。版本 B 从末尾搜索，并在 key 不存在时返回 -1。当 key 恰好出现一次时，两者都返回所要求的索引。仪表板仅在验证 key 恰好出现一次后才调用 locate。

作为库审查者，请向仪表板团队简要建议版本 B 是否可以替换版本 A。请引用契约和仪表板的行为，说明所观察到的不存在 key 时的差异，并陈述你的结论所适用的范围。

Stage 2: scenario and open task

A library method locate(codes, key) requires that key occurs exactly once in codes and promises to return its index. Version A searches from the start and returns codes.length when key is absent. Version B searches from the end and returns -1 when absent. Both return the required index when key occurs exactly once. A dashboard calls locate only after verifying exactly one occurrence.

As the library reviewer, write a brief recommendation to the dashboard team on whether Version B may replace Version A. Cite the contract and dashboard behavior, address the observed absent-key difference, and state the limit of your conclusion.

GRASPS

目标: 判断该变更是否保留仪表板可以依赖的行为。

角色: 库审查者

受众: 仪表板团队

情境: 两个实现在契约排除的某种输入上存在差异。

GRASPS

goal: Decide whether the change preserves behavior the dashboard may rely on.

role: Library reviewer

audience: Dashboard team

situation: Two implementations differ for an input excluded by the contract.

开放任务的产物与成功标准

一项简洁且论证充分的替换建议

  • 陈述明确的建议。
  • 使用明确的要求、所承诺的结果和客户端行为。
  • 解释不存在 key 时的差异是否影响此客户端。
  • 避免声称对于所有输入或客户端，其行为都完全相同。

Open-task product and success criteria

A concise, justified substitution recommendation

  • States a clear recommendation.
  • Uses the explicit requirement, promised result, and client behavior.
  • Explains whether the absent-key difference affects this client.
  • Avoids claiming identical behavior for all inputs or clients.

步骤5：响应设计 / Stage 5: response design

题型理由

单项选择保留了有界限的决策，并允许根据各项完整建议对契约、客户端行为、所观察到的差异以及适当范围的运用来进行评估。

未保留的表现

  • 独立提出简洁建议。
  • 自行表述限制条件，或在不同的合理建议措辞之间作出选择。

Format rationale

Single choice preserves the bounded decision and allows complete recommendations to be evaluated for their use of the contract, client behavior, observed difference, and appropriate scope.

Performance not retained

  • Independent production of a concise recommendation.
  • Formulating an original caveat or choosing among different legitimate recommendation phrasings.
SE08判断题组 / Judgment matrix

审阅背景，不属于学生题面 / Reviewer context, not part of the student question

测试输入与预期结果必须遵守规格

测试应使用契约允许的输入，并只检查规格承诺的结果。即使当前实现总返回某个特定结果，也不能把规格未保证的行为写成所有正确实现都必须通过的断言。

本题保留考察：对于每个提议的测试，分别确定其调用和断言是否构成一个基于契约的有效测试。

Specification-conforming test inputs and expected results

Tests must use legal inputs and assert only guaranteed outcomes. Do not reject a valid implementation for choosing a permitted result different from the current one.

Retained judgment: For each proposed test, determine independently whether its call and assertion form a valid contract-based test.

来源 / Source: Reading 6, Part 1: Specifications · Testing and specifications

locate(a,x) 的契约：要求 x 出现在 a 中；效果是返回一个索引 i，使得 a[i]=x。数组从零开始编号，即第一个元素的索引为 0。对于每个提议的测试，如果其调用和断言都得到该契约的支持，则标记为有效；否则标记为无效。分别独立判断每一项。

p1a=[4,9,9], x=9；断言 result==1。
p2a=[4,9,9], x=9；断言 result 是有效索引且 a[result]==9。
p3a=[4], x=9；断言 locate 抛出异常。
p4a=[4,9], x=9；断言 result==1。

整题完全匹配计分，不计部分分。

Contract for locate(a,x): requires x occurs in a; effects returns an index i such that a[i]=x. Arrays are zero-based, meaning the first element has index 0. The current implementation returns the first occurrence. Mark each proposed test Valid if both its call and assertion are supported by this contract; otherwise mark it Invalid. Judge each independently.

p1a=[4,9,9], x=9; assert result==1.
p2a=[4,9,9], x=9; assert result is a valid index and a[result]==9.
p3a=[4], x=9; assert locate throws an exception.
p4a=[4,9], x=9; assert result==1.

Whole-item exact match; no partial credit.

参考答案与解释 / Reference answer and explanation

p1: 不成立; p2: 成立; p3: 不成立; p4: 成立

P1 无效，因为索引 1 和 2 都满足效果，所以要求结果为 1 会拒绝一个允许的结果。P2 有效，因为该调用满足前置条件，且断言检查了所承诺的性质。P3 无效，因为 x 不存在，违反了前置条件；因此，契约并未承诺会抛出异常。P4 有效，因为 x 只出现在索引 1 处，所以 1 是唯一允许的结果。

p1: Not supported; p2: Supported; p3: Not supported; p4: Supported

P1 is invalid because both indices 1 and 2 satisfy the effect, so requiring 1 rejects a permitted result. P2 is valid because the call meets the precondition and the assertion checks the promised property. P3 is invalid because x is absent, violating the precondition; the contract therefore does not promise an exception. P4 is valid because x occurs only at index 1, making 1 the only permitted result.

五阶段设计记录 / Five-stage design record

步骤1：表现目标

依据允许的输入域和所有许可的输出，评价多个拟议的测试断言。

分别作出有效性判断：每项测试都必须能在给定契约下独立成立。某些预期结果可能不当地照搬当前实现。

Stage 1: performance claim and observable evidence

Evaluate several proposed test assertions against the allowed input domain and all permitted outputs.

Parallel validity judgments: each test must stand on its own under the given contract. Some expectations may illegitimately copy the current implementation.

步骤2：情境与开放任务

某个库对 locate(a, x) 作出如下规定：要求 x 出现在 a 中；效果是返回一个索引 i，使得 a[i] = x。当前实现返回第一次出现的位置，但另一个符合契约的实现可以返回任意一次出现的位置。前置条件是对调用者的要求；后置条件描述了该要求得到满足时所承诺的结果。

作为测试审查人员，请就以下提议向开发团队写一份简短建议：(1) a=[4,9,9], x=9，断言 result==1；(2) a=[4,9,9], x=9，断言 a[result]==9；(3) a=[4], x=9，断言抛出异常；(4) a=[4,9], x=9，断言 result==1。对于每项提议，请说明应保留、修订还是移除，并依据契约论证你的判断。为每一项修订提供更正后的断言。最后说明返回第一次出现位置的实现和返回最后一次出现位置的实现是否都能通过你保留的测试。

Stage 2: scenario and open task

A library specifies locate(a, x): requires x occurs in a; effects returns an index i such that a[i] = x. The current implementation returns the first occurrence, but another conforming implementation may return any occurrence. A precondition is a caller requirement; a postcondition describes the promised result when that requirement holds.

As a test reviewer, write a short recommendation to the development team about these proposals: (1) a=[4,9,9], x=9, assert result==1; (2) a=[4,9,9], x=9, assert a[result]==9; (3) a=[4], x=9, assert an exception; (4) a=[4,9], x=9, assert result==1. For each, state keep, revise, or remove and justify your judgment from the contract. Provide corrected assertions for every revision. Conclude whether a first-occurrence and a last-occurrence implementation could both pass your retained tests.

GRASPS

目标: 对提议的测试输入和断言作出以契约为依据的判断。

角色: 测试审查人员

受众: 开发团队

情境: 如果当前实现被另一个满足相同契约的实现替换，测试必须仍然有效。

GRASPS

goal: Produce contract-grounded judgments about proposed test inputs and assertions.

role: Test reviewer

audience: Development team

situation: Tests must remain valid if the current implementation is replaced by another implementation satisfying the same contract.

开放任务的产物与成功标准

一份简明的审查，包括四项有依据的决定、必要的修订以及关于可替换性的结论。

  • 分别独立判断每项提议。
  • 确定每个输入是否满足所述要求。
  • 保留或创建能够接受契约所允许的每个结果的断言。
  • 不要根据当前实现推断缺失输入时的行为或返回第一次出现位置的行为。
  • 使用保留的测试支持最终结论。

Open-task product and success criteria

A concise review with four justified decisions, revisions where needed, and a substitutability conclusion.

  • Judge each proposal independently.
  • Identify whether each input satisfies the stated requirement.
  • Retain or create assertions that accept every result permitted by the contract.
  • Do not infer missing-input behavior or first-occurrence behavior from the current implementation.
  • Support the final conclusion using the retained tests.

步骤3–4：分解与取舍 / Decomposition and simplification

节点 / Node类型 / Type任务要求 / Requirement处理 / Decision理由 / Reason
K1
dependencies: none
source: mit-specs-testing-and-specifications		taught_knowledge
测试，包括了解实现的测试，必须使用规范允许的调用，并且不得依赖规范之外的实现行为。
Tests, including implementation-aware tests, must use calls permitted by the specification and must not rely on implementation behavior beyond it.
保留 / retain
它提供测试合规性的统领性原则。
It supplies the governing test-conformance principle.
K2

source: mit-specs-testing-and-specifications		taught_knowledge
当多个输出都满足后置条件时，测试必须接受每个允许的输出，而不能要求当前实现所选择的输出。
When several outputs satisfy a postcondition, a test must accept each permitted output rather than require the current implementation's choice.
保留 / retain
它是根据所有允许输出评估断言所需的主要知识。

A precondition is the caller's obligation, while the implementation owes the postcondition only when the precondition holds.

步骤5：响应设计 / Stage 5: response design

题型理由

是非矩阵最能保留并列判断关系，并且无需完成已移除的产出任务，就能直接引出对每个提议测试的决定。

未保留的表现

  • 指定保留、修订或移除，并写出以契约为依据的论证。
  • 独立给出更正后的断言。
  • 说明在保留的测试下，返回第一次出现位置和返回最后一次出现位置的实现是否可相互替换。

Format rationale

A true/false matrix best preserves the parallel-judgment relation and directly elicits a decision for each proposed test without requiring removed production work.

Performance not retained

  • Assigning keep, revise, or remove and writing a contract-grounded justification.
  • Independently producing corrected assertions.
  • Explaining whether first- and last-occurrence implementations are substitutable under the retained tests.
SE09匹配 / Matching

审阅背景，不属于学生题面 / Reviewer context, not part of the student question

声明式规格与操作式规格

声明式规格描述最终结果及其与输入的关系；操作式规格描述计算步骤。课程优先采用清楚的声明式表达，以避免无意间固定算法或暴露调用方不应依赖的实现细节。

本题保留考察：对于每份草案，判断它是规定内部步骤还是陈述外部结果，然后判断两种计划中的扫描方向是否都能满足它。

Declarative versus operational specifications

Declarative specifications describe outcome properties and input-output relationships; operational specifications prescribe steps and can accidentally constrain implementation choices.

Retained judgment: For each draft, classify whether it prescribes internal steps or states an external result, then judge whether both planned scan directions can satisfy it.

来源 / Source: Reading 7: Designing Specifications · Declarative vs. operational specs

虚构方法 locate(names, target) 接收一个包含 target 的非空列表。索引从 0 开始。一种计划中的实现从开头扫描；另一种从末尾扫描。只要相应条目等于 target，任何索引都是有效的；并未刻意要求第一次或最后一次出现的位置。请将每份草案与最恰当的描述匹配。每个选项至多使用一次。

备选项

C1主要规定内部操作；只有从开头开始的实现遵循这些指令。
C2只陈述所要求的外部结果；两种计划中的实现都可以满足它。
C3主要规定内部操作，但两种计划中的实现都遵循这些指令。
C4只陈述所要求的外部结果，而且该结果要求采用从开头开始的实现。

待匹配内容

R1草案 A：“从索引 0 开始向上检查条目，并在找到 target 时返回。”
R2草案 B：“返回其条目等于 target 的任何有效索引。”

整题完全匹配计分，不计部分分。 每个选项最多使用一次。

The fictional method locate(names, target) receives a nonempty list containing target. Indexing starts at 0. One planned implementation scans from the beginning; another scans from the end. Any index whose entry equals target is valid; neither the first nor last occurrence is intentionally required. Match each draft to its best description. Use each option at most once.

Choices

C1Primarily prescribes internal actions; only the beginning-first implementation follows those instructions.
C2Only states the required external result; either planned implementation can satisfy it.
C3Primarily prescribes internal actions, but both planned implementations follow those instructions.
C4Only states the required external result, and that result requires the beginning-first implementation.

Rows to match

R1Draft A: “Check entries from index 0 upward and return when target is found.”
R2Draft B: “Return any valid index whose entry equals target.”

Whole-item exact match; no partial credit. Use each option at most once.

参考答案与解释 / Reference answer and explanation

R1 → C1 (主要规定内部操作；只有从开头开始的实现遵循这些指令。); R2 → C2 (只陈述所要求的外部结果；两种计划中的实现都可以满足它。)

草案 A 指示该方法从索引 0 开始向上检查，因此从末尾开始的实现并未遵循其规定的过程。草案 B 只要求返回一个匹配的索引。由于未要求特定的出现位置，任一计划中的扫描方向都可以满足它。

R1 → C1 (Primarily prescribes internal actions; only the beginning-first implementation follows those instructions.); R2 → C2 (Only states the required external result; either planned implementation can satisfy it.)

Draft A instructs the method to check upward from index 0, so the end-first implementation does not follow its prescribed procedure. Draft B requires only a matching returned index. Since no particular occurrence is required, either planned scan direction can satisfy it.

五阶段设计记录 / Five-stage design record

步骤1：表现目标

根据多个候选规格描述是陈述外部结果属性还是规定内部步骤，对其进行分类。

给出多个“描述—规格风格”对应关系，并理解其对实现自由度的影响。不得改为选择一个总体最佳规格。

Stage 1: performance claim and observable evidence

Classify several candidate specification descriptions according to whether they state external result properties or prescribe internal steps.

Several description-to-specification-style correspondences, with the effect on implementation freedom understood. Do not substitute one overall best specification.

步骤2：情境与开放任务

库方法 locate(names, target) 接收一个包含 target 的非空列表，并返回 target 出现位置的一个索引。计划采用两种实现：一种从开头扫描；另一种从末尾扫描。草案 A 写道：“从索引 0 开始向上检查条目，并在找到 target 时返回。”草案 B 写道：“返回其条目等于 target 的任何有效索引。”

作为 API 评审人员，请为开发团队撰写一份简短建议。对每份草案所使用的规范描述类型进行分类，引用支持每项分类的措辞，并解释每份草案如何影响使用两种计划中实现的自由度。然后，将其中一份草案修改为面向客户端的规范。说明关于有效调用所需的任何假设；前置条件是对调用者的要求。

Stage 2: scenario and open task

A library method locate(names, target) receives a nonempty list containing target and returns an index where target occurs. Two implementations are planned: one scans from the beginning; another scans from the end. Draft A says, “Check entries from index 0 upward and return when target is found.” Draft B says, “Return any valid index whose entry equals target.”

As the API reviewer, write a short recommendation to the development team. Classify each draft by what kind of specification description it uses, cite the wording that supports each classification, and explain how each affects freedom to use the two planned implementations. Then revise one draft into a client-facing specification. State any assumption needed about valid calls; a precondition is a caller requirement.

GRASPS

目标: 区分对外部结果的描述与规定内部步骤的描述。

角色: API 评审人员

受众: 开发团队

情境: 团队希望两种搜索实现都仍可采用。

GRASPS

goal: Distinguish descriptions of external results from descriptions that prescribe internal steps.

role: API reviewer

audience: Development team

situation: The team wants both search implementations to remain possible.

开放任务的产物与成功标准

一份包含分类和论证的建议，以及一份修改后的规范。

  • 根据两份草案中可观察到的措辞对其分类。
  • 解释每份草案允许或限制的实现自由度。
  • 给出一份陈述所要求结果而不规定搜索步骤的修改稿。
  • 明确说明有效调用的假设。

Open-task product and success criteria

A classified, justified recommendation and one revised specification.

  • Classifies both drafts from their observable wording.
  • Explains the implementation freedom permitted or constrained by each draft.
  • Produces a revision stating the required result without prescribing search steps.
  • Makes valid-call assumptions explicit.

步骤5：响应设计 / Stage 5: response design

题型理由

匹配形式保留了多组草案与描述之间的对应关系，并使类型分类与实现自由度保持关联，而不是要求选择一份总体上最好的草案。

未保留的表现

  • 在独立组织的解释中引用或转述证据。
  • 独立编写面向客户端的规范措辞。
  • 陈述前置条件并将其与所要求的结果区分开来。

Format rationale

Matching preserves the several draft-to-description correspondences and keeps style classification linked to implementation freedom rather than asking for one overall best draft.

Performance not retained

  • Citing or paraphrasing evidence in an independently constructed explanation.
  • Independently producing client-facing specification wording.
  • Stating a precondition and distinguishing it from the required result.
SE10单选 / Single choice

审阅背景，不属于学生题面 / Reviewer context, not part of the student question

规格强弱与安全替换

新规格若不增加调用方的前置要求，并在旧前置条件适用的范围内提供至少原有的结果保证，就可安全承接旧契约。放宽输入要求或加强输出保证，都会增加实现方承担的责任。

本题保留考察：选择能够正确评估 A 和 B 是否可替换旧契约的综合比较，包括任何等价并列关系。

Specification strength and safe replacement

A replacement contract must not strengthen preconditions and must preserve or strengthen guarantees on the old legal domain. This supports substitutability for old clients.

Retained judgment: Choose the integrated comparison that correctly evaluates A and B as replacements for the old contract, including any equivalence tie.

来源 / Source: Reading 7: Designing Specifications · Stronger vs. weaker specs

旧版 locate(a,v) 要求 v 在 a 中恰好出现一次，并保证返回其索引。A 要求出现一次或多次，并保证返回任意匹配索引 i，其中 a[i]=v。B 重复了旧版的要求和保证。每个契约仅由这些条款构成。客户端义务是调用者必须满足的要求；实现者保证是在合法调用上的承诺结果。比较在旧版下合法的调用所获得的保证时，哪项比较正确判断了对旧版的安全替换？

A只有 B 是安全的：A 允许重复出现，因此其保证必须在这些调用上与旧版保证相匹配；B 与旧版并列。
B两者都是安全的，并且两者都与旧版并列：每当旧版的要求成立时，它们都返回一个匹配索引。
C两者都是安全的：A 对客户端提出的义务更弱，并且在旧版合法调用上具有相同的保证；B 与旧版并列。
D只有 A 是安全的：接受更多调用使 A 更强，而 B 无法替换旧版，因为它没有提供改进的保证。

整题完全匹配计分，不计部分分。

Old locate(a,v) requires v to occur exactly once in a and guarantees returning its index. A requires one or more occurrences and guarantees any matching index i, where a[i]=v. B repeats Old’s requirement and guarantee. Only these clauses form each contract. A client obligation is a caller requirement; an implementer guarantee is a promised result on legal calls. Which comparison correctly judges safe replacement of Old, comparing guarantees on calls legal under Old?

AOnly B is safe: A admits repeated occurrences, so its guarantee must match the old guarantee on those calls; B is tied with Old.
BBoth are safe and both tie with Old: each returns a matching index whenever the old requirement holds.
CBoth are safe: A places a weaker client obligation and has the same guarantee on old-legal calls; B is tied with Old.
DOnly A is safe: accepting more calls makes A stronger, while B cannot replace Old because it offers no improved guarantee.

Whole-item exact match; no partial credit.

参考答案与解释 / Reference answer and explanation

C (两者都是安全的：A 对客户端提出的义务更弱，并且在旧版合法调用上具有相同的保证；B 与旧版并列。)

接受 C。A 允许所有旧版合法调用，外加重复出现的调用；在旧版合法调用上，它的匹配索引就是旧版承诺的唯一索引。B 与旧版具有相同的要求和保证，因此二者等价。A 错误地要求 A 的保证在重复出现的调用上与旧版相匹配。B 忽略了 A 更弱的客户端义务，因此 A 并不与旧版并列。D 错误地将安全替换视为要求改进的保证，并拒绝了一个等价契约。

C (Both are safe: A places a weaker client obligation and has the same guarantee on old-legal calls; B is tied with Old.)

C is accepted. A permits all old-legal calls plus repeated-occurrence calls, and on an old-legal call its matching index is the unique index promised by Old. B has the same requirement and guarantee as Old, so they are equivalent. A wrongly requires A’s guarantee to match Old on repeated-occurrence calls. B overlooks A’s weaker client obligation, so A is not tied with Old. D wrongly treats safe replacement as requiring an improved guarantee and rejects an equivalent contract.

五阶段设计记录 / Five-stage design record

步骤1：表现目标

利用义务和保证，比较一小组彼此可比较的契约。

有依据地比较契约的相对强弱，并将等价契约保留为并列。仅使用真正可比较的案例，并说明所有输入域事实。不得以模糊的总体质量对不可比较的规格排序。

Stage 1: performance claim and observable evidence

Compare a small family of mutually comparable contracts using obligations and guarantees.

A supported comparison of relative contract strength, preserving equivalent contracts as ties. Restrict the family to genuinely comparable cases and state all domain facts. Do not rank incomparable specifications by vague overall quality.

步骤2：情境与开放任务

某库的旧版 locate(a,v) 契约要求 v 恰好出现一次，并保证返回其索引。提案 A 要求出现一次或多次，并返回任意匹配索引。提案 B 具有旧版的要求，并返回该匹配索引。

请就 A 和 B 是否可以安全替换旧契约向库维护者提出建议。写一份简短比较，指出客户端义务和实现者保证，将等价契约视为并列，并将保证比较限定在旧契约下合法的调用。解释其他调用上的行为为什么会或不会影响每项判断。

Stage 2: scenario and open task

A library’s old locate(a,v) contract requires v to occur exactly once and guarantees returning its index. Proposed A requires one or more occurrences and returns any matching index. Proposed B has the old requirement and returns the matching index.

Advise the library maintainer whether A and B may safely replace the old contract. Write a brief comparison that identifies client obligations and implementer guarantees, treats equivalent contracts as tied, and limits guarantee comparisons to calls legal under the old contract. Explain why behavior on other calls does or does not affect each judgment.

GRASPS

目标: 提出一项可辩护的安全替换建议。

角色: 初级软件规格审查员

受众: 库维护者

情境: 现有客户端依赖旧版 locate 契约。

GRASPS

goal: Produce a defensible safe-replacement recommendation.

role: Junior software specification reviewer

audience: Library maintainer

situation: Existing clients rely on the old locate contract.

开放任务的产物与成功标准

简洁的比较与建议

  • 分别比较所述要求和保证。
  • 在旧契约的合法调用上判断每个提案。
  • 当义务和相关保证等价时保留并列关系。
  • 使用契约事实支持每项替换结论。

Open-task product and success criteria

A concise comparison and recommendation

  • Compare the stated requirements and guarantees separately.
  • Judge each proposal on the old contract’s legal calls.
  • Preserve a tie when obligations and relevant guarantees are equivalent.
  • Support each replacement conclusion with contract facts.

步骤3–4：分解与取舍 / Decomposition and simplification

dependencies: none
source: mit-design-stronger-vs-weaker-specs		taught_knowledge

A replacement contract is at least as strong as the old contract when its precondition is no stronger and its postcondition is no weaker on states satisfying the old precondition.

步骤5：响应设计 / Stage 5: response design

题型理由

单项选择保留了一个范围受限的决定，并要求义务、保证、替换和等价关系的比较保持一致。

未保留的表现

  • 提出独立建议。
  • 解释适用前置条件之外的行为。
  • 展示完整的比较过程。

Format rationale

Single choice preserves one bounded decision and requires consistency across obligation, guarantee, replacement, and equivalence comparisons.

Performance not retained

  • Producing an independent recommendation.
  • Explaining behavior outside applicable preconditions.
  • Displaying the complete comparison procedure.

包容性教学 / Inclusive Teaching and Learning 10题

INC01INC02INC03INC04INC05INC06INC07INC08INC09INC10
INC01单选 / Single choice

审阅背景，不属于学生题面 / Reviewer context, not part of the student question

主动参与与学习证据的区别

分组、热闹或发言本身不能证明学习。需要结合任务是否适合目标，以及学生的解释、作品或应用表现来判断。

本题保留考察：综合考虑可见的参与情况、任务的适切性以及对理解的展示，选择关于本节课是否表明发生了有意义学习的最站得住脚的结论。

Active participation versus evidence of learning

Grouping or visible activity alone does not establish learning. Interpret participation together with task suitability and evidence in explanations, products or application.

Retained judgment: Select the most defensible conclusion about whether this lesson demonstrates meaningful learning by jointly considering visible participation, task suitability, and demonstrations of understanding.

来源 / Source: Week 3: Everyone can do something · 3. Observing active teaching

在一节科学课上，各小组兴致勃勃地把熟悉、显而易见的图片分为“有生命”和“无生命”两类，并很快完成了任务。两名学习者既没有动手拿卡片，也没有发言。全班都说每个人已经理解了，但没有人解释自己的选择，也没有人把这一区分应用于新的例子。这里，“应用”是指运用这一区分来判断一个不熟悉的例子。哪一项判断最站得住脚？

A这表明发生了有意义的学习，因为积极参与并迅速完成小组任务说明学习者理解了这一区分。
B证据有限：任务使用了显而易见的例子，参与并不均衡，而且没有学习者展示推理或应用，尽管小组学习本身仍可能有助于学习。
C这表明发生了有意义的学习，因为全班确认已经理解，尽管没有收集个人解释。
D证据有限，主要是因为小组学习使教师无法确定个别学习者是否理解了这一区分。

整题完全匹配计分，不计部分分。

In a science lesson, groups enthusiastically sort familiar, obvious pictures as “living” or “non-living” and finish quickly. Two learners neither handle cards nor speak. The class says everyone understands, but nobody explains a choice or applies the distinction to a new example. Here, application means using the distinction with an unfamiliar example. Which judgment is most defensible?

AMeaningful learning is demonstrated because enthusiastic participation and quick group completion show that learners understood the distinction.
BEvidence is limited: the task used obvious examples, participation was uneven, and no learner demonstrated reasoning or application, although group work itself may still support learning.
CMeaningful learning is demonstrated because the class confirmed understanding, even though individual explanations were not collected.
DEvidence is limited mainly because group work prevents teachers from determining whether individual learners understand the distinction.

Whole-item exact match; no partial credit.

参考答案与解释 / Reference answer and explanation

B (证据有限：任务使用了显而易见的例子，参与并不均衡，而且没有学习者展示推理或应用，尽管小组学习本身仍可能有助于学习。)

B综合考虑了任务要求、参与不均衡以及缺少解释或应用的情况，同时没有否定小组学习。A和C把活动参与或集体保证视为充分证据。D得出了谨慎的结论，但错误地将问题归因于小组学习本身，而非这项任务及其证据。

B (Evidence is limited: the task used obvious examples, participation was uneven, and no learner demonstrated reasoning or application, although group work itself may still support learning.)

B integrates task demand, uneven participation, and the absence of explanations or application without rejecting group work. A and C treat activity or collective assurance as sufficient evidence. D reaches a cautious conclusion but incorrectly attributes the problem to group work itself rather than this task and its evidence.

五阶段设计记录 / Five-stage design record

步骤1：表现目标

判断哪些可观察的课堂特征足以支持关于已发生有意义学习的结论，而不只是表面活动。

并列作出证据判断，将参与情况、任务要求和理解表现联系起来；避免否定所有小组活动，也不要把明显的热情视为充分证据。

Stage 1: performance claim and observable evidence

Judge which observable classroom features warrant a conclusion about meaningful learning rather than mere activity.

Parallel evidence judgments linking participation, task demands and demonstrations of understanding; avoid condemning all group work or treating visible enthusiasm as sufficient.

步骤2：情境与开放任务

在一节采用小组形式的科学课上，学习者兴致勃勃地把图片卡片分为“有生命”和“无生命”两组。卡片上只有熟悉、显而易见的例子。每个小组都很快完成了任务，但两名学习者既没有动手拿卡片，也没有发言。当被问及是否每个人都理解时，全班回答说是。没有学习者解释自己的选择，也没有人把这一区分应用于新的例子。

作为向同事提供建议的教师，简要写出一项有证据支持的判断，说明本节课是否表明发生了有意义的学习。引用具体观察，解释任务要求如何影响你的结论，并提出一项能够从所有学习者那里获得更有力证据的可行后续措施。

Stage 2: scenario and open task

In a group science lesson, learners enthusiastically sort picture cards into “living” and “non-living” sets. The cards show only familiar, obvious examples. Every group finishes quickly, but two learners handle no cards and say nothing. When asked whether everyone understands, the class says yes. No learner explains a choice or applies the distinction to a new example.

As a teacher advising a colleague, write a brief evidence-based judgment on whether this lesson demonstrates meaningful learning. Cite specific observations, explain how the task’s demands affect your conclusion, and propose one practical follow-up that would produce stronger evidence from all learners.

GRASPS

目标: 就学习证据得出一项站得住脚的判断，并说明理由。

角色: 教师顾问

受众: 授课教师

情境: 可见的热情和已完成的小组任务可能无法表明个体是否理解。

GRASPS

goal: Reach and justify a defensible judgment about the evidence of learning.

role: Teacher adviser

audience: The lesson teacher

situation: Visible enthusiasm and completed group work may not show individual understanding.

开放任务的产物与成功标准

一份简要判断和后续计划

  • 将可见的参与同已展示的理解区分开来。
  • 把简单的任务和不均衡的参与同证据的局限联系起来。
  • 使用可观察的事实，同时不否定小组学习本身。
  • 提出一种可行的方式，使每名学习者都能解释、产出或应用自己的理解。

Open-task product and success criteria

A brief judgment and follow-up plan

  • Separates visible participation from demonstrated understanding.
  • Connects the easy task and uneven participation to limits in the evidence.
  • Uses observable facts without dismissing group work itself.
  • Proposes a feasible way for every learner to explain, produce, or apply understanding.

提供 / provide
提供缺少解释以及运用于新例子的情况。
Supplies the absence of explanations and use with a new example.
C1
dependencies: K1, K2, K3, F1, F2, F3, F4, F5
source: open_task		cognitive_operation
评价观察到的课堂是否为有关有意义学习的结论提供了充分证据。
Evaluate whether the observed lesson provides sufficient evidence for a conclusion about meaningful learning.
保留 / retain
保留对这些观察是否充分表明发生了有意义学习这一核心评价。
Preserves the focal evaluation of whether the observations sufficiently demonstrate meaningful learning.
C2
dependencies: K1, K2, K3, F1, F2, F3, F5, C1
source: open_task		cognitive_operation
通过把参与情况、任务要求以及对理解的展示或缺失联系起来，对判断作出解释。
Explain the judgment by linking participation, task demand, and demonstrations or absences of understanding.
保留 / retain
要求检查所提出的判断是否将参与情况、任务要求和理解证据联系起来。
Requires checking whether a proposed judgment links participation, task demand, and evidence of understanding.
C3
dependencies: K3, K4, F3, F4, F5, C1
source: open_task		cognitive_operation
提出一项可行的后续措施，从每名学习者那里引出理解证据。
Produce one feasible follow-up that elicits evidence of understanding from every learner.
排除 / exclude
删除了独立提出后续措施的要求。
Independent production of a follow-up is removed.
C4
dependencies: K4, C3
source: open_task		cognitive_operation
明确学习者将解释、创作或应用什么，以及教师将查看或询问哪些证据。
Specify what learners will explain, create, or apply and what evidence the teacher will inspect or ask about.
排除 / exclude
明确后续措施中要检查的证据不属于简化后的判断范围。
Specifying evidence to inspect in a follow-up is outside the simplified judgment.

dependencies: C3
source: open_task		procedure
进行一次简短检查，其中涉及一个不太明显或新的例子、由学习者产出的回答，或者在小组学习中进行个人解释。
Use a short check involving a less obvious or new example, a learner-produced response, or an individual explanation within group work.
排除 / exclude
无需选择或提出后续程序。
No follow-up procedure must be selected or produced.

dependencies: K1, K2
source: inclusive-week3-3-observing-active-teaching, open_task		constraint

Do not reject group work as such; judge this lesson from the task quality and available evidence.
保留 / retain
防止在缺乏依据的情况下否定小组学习本身。
Prevents an unsupported rejection of group work itself.

source: open_task		constraint
回答应是教师顾问向授课教师提出的一项简短且有证据支持的判断。
The response is a brief evidence-based judgment addressed by a teacher adviser to the lesson teacher.
排除 / exclude
顾问角色和建构式书面成果对于选择题评价并非必要。
The adviser role and constructed written product are unnecessary for selected-response evaluation.

步骤5：响应设计 / Stage 5: response design

题型理由

单项选择题保留了综合判断，并允许把多种合理的教学后续措施排除在选项之外，而不暗示必须采用某一种特定程序。

未保留的表现

  • 失去了独立写出有证据支持的判断这一表现要求。
  • 失去了为每名学习者设计可行后续措施这一表现要求。
  • 失去了明确教师将检查什么证据以及这些证据将如何为教学提供依据这一表现要求。

Format rationale

Single choice preserves the integrated judgment and permits multiple legitimate teaching follow-ups to remain outside the options rather than implying one required procedure.

Performance not retained

  • Independent production of an evidence-based written judgment is lost.
  • Designing a feasible follow-up for every learner is lost.
  • Specifying what evidence the teacher would inspect and how it would inform teaching is lost.
INC02匹配 / Matching

审阅背景，不属于学生题面 / Reviewer context, not part of the student question

观察学习者与响应性调整

教师留意学习者的困难、进步和反应，据此调整支持；对行为先收集相关信息，不把沉默或迟到直接视为能力或态度问题。

本题保留考察：确定所提供的哪项教学回应最符合每项不同的学习者观察或所报告的情况，同时避免无依据的推断，并维持学习者以受尊重的方式参与预期学习。

Observing learners and responsive adaptation

Notice learners’ difficulties, progress and reactions, then adapt support. Seek relevant information rather than inferring ability or attitude directly from silence or lateness.

Retained judgment: Determine which supplied teaching response best follows from each distinct learner observation or reported circumstance while avoiding unsupported inference and maintaining respectful access to the intended learning.

来源 / Source: Week 3: Everyone can do something · 4. Being observant, flexible and focusing on self-esteem

将每项观察与最能以尊重的方式应对障碍、避免对能力或态度作出无依据的结论并保留预期学习内容的回应相匹配。障碍是指使参与或学习变得更困难的事物。每项回应最多使用一次；有一项回应不使用。

备选项

A先安排两人结对演练，然后邀请学习者自愿就相同观点向全班作答。
B私下商定一种切实可行的方式来补上错过的学习内容，同时保留本课的学习目标。
C示范并用视觉方式区分这两个标签，然后让学习者再次尝试完成图示。
D处理嘲笑行为，采用支持性的分组方式，并提供一种风险较低的参与方式。
E提供简化的学习任务，因为有限的参与或反复出错表明能力较低。

待匹配内容

R1全班提问时，阿玛拉保持沉默，但在结对交谈中会解释观点。
R2本迟到了两次，并私下说明早晨要承担照护责任。
R3陈很快开始任务，但在图示中反复混淆两个标签。
R4同学们嘲笑迪娜的回答后，她不再参与。

整题完全匹配计分，不计部分分。 每个选项最多使用一次。

Match each observation to the response that best addresses the barrier respectfully, avoids unsupported conclusions about ability or attitude, and keeps the intended learning. A barrier is something making participation or learning harder. Use each response no more than once; one response is unused.

Choices

AUse paired rehearsal before inviting an optional whole-class response on the same ideas.
BPrivately agree on a practical way to recover missed learning while retaining the lesson goal.
CModel and visually distinguish the two labels, then let the learner retry the diagram.
DAddress the laughter, use supportive grouping, and offer a lower-risk way to contribute.
EGive simplified work because limited participation or repeated errors indicate lower ability.

Rows to match

R1Amara is silent in whole-class questions but explains ideas in paired talk.
R2Ben arrives late twice and privately reports morning caregiving.
R3Chen starts promptly but repeatedly confuses two diagram labels.
R4Dina stops contributing after classmates laugh at her answer.

Whole-item exact match; no partial credit. Use each option at most once.

参考答案与解释 / Reference answer and explanation

R1 → A (先安排两人结对演练，然后邀请学习者自愿就相同观点向全班作答。); R2 → B (私下商定一种切实可行的方式来补上错过的学习内容，同时保留本课的学习目标。); R3 → C (示范并用视觉方式区分这两个标签，然后让学习者再次尝试完成图示。); R4 → D (处理嘲笑行为，采用支持性的分组方式，并提供一种风险较低的参与方式。)

A利用了阿玛拉在结对交谈中表现出的成功，而没有把沉默视为无能。B以尊重的方式回应本所说明的照护责任，并保留了他错过的学习内容。C针对陈在标签方面的具体混淆。D处理与迪娜不再参与有关的同伴反应，并支持她重新参与。E对能力作出了无依据的推断，并以简化的学习任务取代预期学习内容。

R1 → A (Use paired rehearsal before inviting an optional whole-class response on the same ideas.); R2 → B (Privately agree on a practical way to recover missed learning while retaining the lesson goal.); R3 → C (Model and visually distinguish the two labels, then let the learner retry the diagram.); R4 → D (Address the laughter, use supportive grouping, and offer a lower-risk way to contribute.)

A uses Amara’s demonstrated success in paired talk without treating silence as inability. B responds respectfully to Ben’s reported caregiving and preserves missed learning. C targets Chen’s specific label confusion. D addresses the peer reaction linked to Dina’s withdrawal and supports renewed participation. E makes an unsupported ability inference and replaces the intended learning with simplified work.

五阶段设计记录 / Five-stage design record

步骤1：表现目标

根据对学习者需求的多个不同观察，分别确定相应的教学回应。

给出多个“观察—回应”对应关系，说明实际障碍，并且不根据沉默诊断能力或残障。

Stage 1: performance claim and observable evidence

Determine which teaching response follows from each of several distinct observations of learner needs.

Several observation-to-response correspondences, with actual barriers stated and without diagnosing ability or disability from silence.

步骤2：情境与开放任务

在一节课中，全班提问时，阿玛拉保持沉默，但在结对交谈中会解释观点。本迟到了两次，并私下说他早晨分担照护责任。陈很快开始图示任务，但反复混淆两个标签。同学们嘲笑迪娜的回答后，她不再参与。

作为他们的教师，为这四名学习者分别写一份简短的回应计划。针对每个人，说明观察结果能够和不能够证实什么，指出你会寻求哪些信息，并建议一项保留本课预期学习内容的具体调整。解释你将如何观察每项回应是否改善了参与或进步。

Stage 2: scenario and open task

In one lesson, Amara is silent during whole-class questions but explains ideas in paired talk. Ben arrives late twice and privately says he shares morning caregiving. Chen begins a diagram task promptly but repeatedly confuses two labels. Dina stops contributing after classmates laugh at her answer.

As their teacher, write a brief response plan for all four learners. For each, state what the observation does and does not establish, identify any information you would seek, and recommend a specific adaptation that preserves the lesson’s intended learning. Explain how you will observe whether each response improves participation or progress.

GRASPS

目标: 根据不同的观察制定有依据且能回应学习者需要的教学行动。

角色: 班级教师

受众: 一位教学同事

情境: 四名学习者表现出不同的情况，需要在不对能力或态度作出无依据判断的前提下给予支持。

GRASPS

goal: Develop justified, responsive teaching actions from distinct observations.

role: Class teacher

audience: A teaching colleague

situation: Four learners show different patterns requiring support without unsupported judgments about ability or attitude.

开放任务的产物与成功标准

一份包含四部分的回应计划

  • 将每项回应与学习者所述的情况或观察到的困难联系起来。
  • 将直接观察与假设区分开来。
  • 在原因仍不确定时寻求相关信息。
  • 明确提出保留预期学习内容且尊重学习者的调整。
  • 指出参与或进步得到改善的可观察迹象。

Open-task product and success criteria

A four-part response plan

  • Connect each response to the learner’s stated circumstances or observed difficulty.
  • Separate direct observations from assumptions.
  • Seek relevant information where the cause remains uncertain.
  • Specify respectful adaptations that retain the intended learning.
  • Name observable signs of improved participation or progress.

步骤5：响应设计 / Stage 5: response design

题型理由

之所以选择匹配题，是因为每个不同的案例都必须对应最可辩护的回应性行动。四行和五个选项既支持针对具体案例作出判断，也提供一个看似合理的干扰项，同时避免要求独立提出回应。

未保留的表现

  • 独立提出四项调整
  • 独立确定需要寻求的信息
  • 明确可观察的后续指标
  • 为每项判断提供书面理由
  • 执行从观察到复查的完整流程

Format rationale

Matching is selected because each distinct case must correspond to the most defensible responsive action. Four rows and five options support case-specific judgment and one plausible distractor while avoiding independent response production.

Performance not retained

  • Independent production of four adaptations
  • Independent identification of information to seek
  • Specification of observable follow-up indicators
  • Written justification of each judgment
  • Execution of the full observation-to-review procedure
INC03单选 / Single choice

审阅背景，不属于学生题面 / Reviewer context, not part of the student question

灵活安排、自尊与学习目标

灵活改变活动或支持方式，并以尊重和恰当反馈维护自尊，帮助学习者参与学习；调整安排仍需服务学习目标。

本题保留考察：通过评估方案是否保留因果解释、支持尊重地参与、维持恰当的鼓励，并包含有助于调整的有效观察，选择最站得住脚且可行的调整方案。

Flexible arrangements, self-esteem and learning goals

Adapt activities and support while maintaining respect and appropriate encouragement. Flexibility should help learners participate in the intended learning.

Retained judgment: Choose the most defensible feasible adaptation by evaluating whether it preserves causal explanation, enables respectful participation, maintains appropriate encouragement, and includes useful observation for adjustment.

来源 / Source: Week 3: Everyone can do something · 4. Being observant, flexible and focusing on self-esteem

在一节30分钟的历史课上，学习者必须作出因果解释——说明一个本地事件如何促成另一个事件。阿米娜理解示例，但说话较慢，并且在全班回答时遭到嘲笑。她说，绘画或结对交谈有助于她自信地参与。现有纸张可用，但没有额外人员或时间。哪项方案最站得住脚？

A免除阿米娜进行解释的任务；让她在同学口头回答时画出任一事件。表扬她在艺术方面的努力，并记录她是否完成。
B让所有人画出事件顺序，结对排练因果解释，然后通过口述或使用视觉材料进行分享。鼓励阿米娜进行历史推理，并观察准确性、参与情况和同伴反应。
C在同学完成原有的全班活动时，为阿米娜提供额外的私下口语练习。让她最后回答，并观察她说话是否更快。
D将全班的解释任务替换为绘画。展示阿米娜的画作以增强她的信心，并观察同学是否鼓掌。

整题完全匹配计分，不计部分分。

In a 30-minute history lesson, learners must give a causal explanation—show how one local event contributed to another. Amina understands the examples but speaks slowly and is laughed at during whole-class answers. She says drawing or paired talk helps her participate confidently. Paper is available, but no extra staff or time. Which plan is most defensible?

AExcuse Amina from explaining; let her draw either event while classmates answer aloud. Praise her artistic effort and note whether she finishes.
BHave everyone draw an event sequence, rehearse a causal explanation in pairs, then share by speaking or using the visual. Encourage Amina’s historical reasoning and observe accuracy, participation, and peer reactions.
CGive Amina extra private speaking practice while classmates complete the original whole-class activity. Ask her to answer last and observe whether she speaks faster.
DReplace the explanation task with drawing for the whole class. Display Amina’s picture as a confidence boost and observe whether classmates applaud.

Whole-item exact match; no partial credit.

参考答案与解释 / Reference answer and explanation

B (让所有人画出事件顺序，结对排练因果解释，然后通过口述或使用视觉材料进行分享。鼓励阿米娜进行历史推理，并观察准确性、参与情况和同伴反应。)

B保留了因果解释，同时为所有学习者提供了可行的参与选择，以尊重的方式鼓励阿米娜进行推理，并收集有助于调整的有用证据。A和D用绘画取代了因果解释目标。C保留了障碍，将阿米娜与同学分开，并将说话速度而非学习和参与作为主要证据。

B (Have everyone draw an event sequence, rehearse a causal explanation in pairs, then share by speaking or using the visual. Encourage Amina’s historical reasoning and observe accuracy, participation, and peer reactions.)

B preserves causal explanation while offering feasible participation choices to all learners, encouraging Amina’s reasoning respectfully, and gathering useful evidence for adjustment. A and D replace the causal-explanation goal with drawing. C retains the barrier, separates Amina from classmates, and treats speaking speed rather than learning and participation as the main evidence.

五阶段设计记录 / Five-stage design record

步骤1：表现目标

评价一项连贯的调整：在保持学习目标的同时，灵活且尊重地回应已说明的需求。

在资源限制下作出一个综合教学决定，并设置一些看似合理的替代方案；这些方案虽有部分帮助，却遗漏了某个相关条件。

Stage 1: performance claim and observable evidence

Evaluate a coherent adaptation that maintains the learning goal while responding flexibly and respectfully to stated needs.

One integrated teaching decision under resource constraints, with plausible alternatives that partly help but miss a relevant condition.

步骤2：情境与开放任务

在一节30分钟的历史课上，学习者必须解释一个本地历史事件如何导致另一个事件。阿米娜理解示例，但说话较慢，并且在全班回答时遭到嘲笑。她说，通过绘画或结对交谈参与时，她更有信心。教师有纸张，但没有额外人员或课堂时间。

为这节课推荐一项调整方案并说明理由。描述阿米娜和同学将如何参与、如何保持原有的因果解释目标不变，以及教师将如何以尊重的方式鼓励阿米娜。包括一项教师将用于审视或调整这一安排的观察。

Stage 2: scenario and open task

During a 30-minute history lesson, learners must explain how one local event caused another. Amina understands the examples but speaks slowly and is laughed at during whole-class answers. She says she participates more confidently through drawing or paired talk. The teacher has paper but no extra staff or lesson time.

Recommend and justify one adaptation for this lesson. Describe how Amina and classmates will participate, how the original causal-explanation goal remains intact, and how the teacher will encourage Amina respectfully. Include one observation the teacher will use to review or adjust the arrangement.

GRASPS

目标: 设计一项灵活且尊重的调整方案，以保留预期学习内容。

角色: 任课教师

受众: 一位教学同事

情境: 必须在现有时间和资源范围内解决参与障碍。

GRASPS

goal: Design a flexible, respectful adaptation that preserves the intended learning.

role: Classroom teacher

audience: A teaching colleague

situation: Participation barriers must be addressed within existing time and resources.

开放任务的产物与成功标准

一项有理据的课堂调整方案

  • 将因果解释保持为学习目标
  • 提供一条切实可行的参与途径
  • 回应阿米娜所述的需求，同时不降低期望
  • 采用尊重的鼓励方式
  • 确定可供进一步调整使用的可观察证据

Open-task product and success criteria

A justified lesson adaptation

  • Keeps causal explanation as the learning goal
  • Provides a workable participation route
  • Responds to Amina’s stated needs without lowering expectations
  • Uses respectful encouragement
  • Identifies observable evidence for further adjustment

步骤5：响应设计 / Stage 5: response design

题型理由

单项选择题保留了综合判断，同时提供四项看似合理的方案；每项方案都涉及部分条件，但在目标保留、参与、尊重、可行性或回应性观察方面有所不同。

未保留的表现

  • 独立形成原创的调整方案
  • 独立撰写完整的理由说明和观察计划

Format rationale

Single choice preserves the integrated judgment while allowing four plausible plans that each address some conditions but differ in goal preservation, participation, respect, feasibility, or responsive observation.

Performance not retained

  • Independently producing an original adaptation
  • Independently composing a complete justification and observation plan
INC04单选 / Single choice

审阅背景，不属于学生题面 / Reviewer context, not part of the student question

先备经验与有意义的学习任务

包容性教学利用学习者已有知识和生活经验，把新内容与相关情境联系起来，同时给学习者应用和解释的机会。

本题保留考察：判断哪项分析正确解释了所提出的三项预算活动如何将不同的已有经验与具体内容、有意义的应用及解释联系起来，同时不要求学习者披露个人财务情况。

Prior experience and meaningful learning tasks

Build on prior knowledge and relevant life experience, connecting new content to meaningful contexts and opportunities to apply and explain.

Retained judgment: Determine which analysis correctly explains how three proposed budgeting activities connect varied prior experience to specific content, meaningful application, and explanation without requiring personal financial disclosure.

来源 / Source: Week 3: Everyone can do something · 2. Everyone can do something: what does inclusive teaching look like?; 5. Inclusive teaching; 6. Challenges in inclusive teaching and learning

一门预算课程的学习者包括市场摊贩、工薪人员和帮助处理家庭支出的年轻人。课程并非先讲定义、再进行一次计算，而是让学习者：(1) 比较所提供或自愿分享的固定及变动收入与支出示例；(2) 对项目进行分类，并平衡反映不同情况的虚构人物预算；(3) 讨论：“你会修改哪一项预算选择，为什么？”是否披露个人情况由学习者自愿决定。开放性问题允许作出不止于简短固定答案的回答。以下哪项分析最能解释这些活动如何促进理解和应用？

A它具有包容性，主要是因为每个人都讨论熟悉的支出；计算和解释作用不大。
B示例将不同经验与预算类别联系起来；虚构人物预算支持应用；比较能引出推理，同时不要求披露个人情况。
C摊贩的日常支出提供了共同基础，而报告每个虚构人物预算的结余情况则表明学习者已经理解。
D只有第一项活动能利用已有知识；虚构人物预算无法提供有意义的应用。

整题完全匹配计分，不计部分分。

A budgeting class includes market sellers, salaried workers and young adults who help with family spending. Instead of definitions followed by one calculation, learners: (1) compare supplied or voluntary examples of regular and changing income and expenses; (2) classify items and balance fictional profiles reflecting varied circumstances; (3) discuss, “Which budget choice would you revise, and why?” Personal disclosure is optional. An open question allows more than a brief fixed answer. Which analysis best explains how these activities support understanding and application?

AIt is inclusive mainly because everyone discusses familiar spending; calculation and explanation add little.
BExamples connect varied experience to budget categories; profiles support application; comparison elicits reasoning without requiring disclosure.
CSellers’ daily spending provides a shared foundation, and reporting each profile’s balance demonstrates understanding.
DOnly the first activity builds prior knowledge; fictional profiles cannot provide meaningful application.

Whole-item exact match; no partial credit.

参考答案与解释 / Reference answer and explanation

B (示例将不同经验与预算类别联系起来；虚构人物预算支持应用；比较能引出推理，同时不要求披露个人情况。)

B指出了各项活动之间的具体联系：不同经验有助于理解预算类别，虚构人物预算使有意义的应用成为可能，而开放性问题则能引出推理，同时不强制披露个人情况。A低估了应用和解释的作用。C假定学习者拥有共同经验，并将固定答案视为充分。D错误地否定了虚构人物预算，也忽视了后续联系。

B (Examples connect varied experience to budget categories; profiles support application; comparison elicits reasoning without requiring disclosure.)

B identifies specific links across the activities: varied experience informs budget categories, fictional profiles enable meaningful application, and an open question elicits reasoning without compulsory disclosure. A undervalues application and explanation. C assumes a shared experience and treats a fixed answer as sufficient. D wrongly dismisses fictional profiles and overlooks later connections.

五阶段设计记录 / Five-stage design record

步骤1：表现目标

确定多个课堂活动如何利用既有经验来支持应用和理解。

给出多个“活动—教学目的”对应关系，并以相关先备知识和有意义的应用为依据；不得概括成一句笼统的优质课堂口号。

Stage 1: performance claim and observable evidence

Determine how several lesson activities use prior experience to support application and understanding.

Several activity-to-instructional-purpose correspondences grounded in relevant prior knowledge and meaningful application; not an overall good-lesson slogan.

步骤2：情境与开放任务

一名辅导教师正在介绍家庭预算。学习者包括市场摊贩、工薪人员和帮助管理家庭支出的年轻人。课程草案先讲定义，然后使用一个虚构的家庭预算。学习者计算一项支出，并且只报告答案。

作为这名辅导教师的同事，将课程修改为三项相互联系的活动。针对每项活动，说明学习者将利用哪些相关经验、这些经验如何与预算内容相联系，以及学习者将应用或解释什么。向辅导教师说明你的选择理由。至少包含一个开放性问题，并留出学习者对话的时间；开放性问题允许作出不止于简短固定答案的回答。

Stage 2: scenario and open task

A tutor is introducing household budgeting. Learners include market sellers, salaried workers and young adults who help manage family spending. The draft lesson begins with definitions, then uses a fictional household budget. Learners calculate one expense and report only the answer.

As the tutor’s colleague, revise the lesson into three linked activities. For each activity, state which relevant experience learners will draw on, how it connects to budgeting content, and what learners will apply or explain. Justify your choices to the tutor. Include at least one open question and time for learner dialogue; an open question allows more than a brief fixed answer.

GRASPS

目标: 设计将预算内容与相关已有经验及有意义的应用联系起来的活动。

角色: 教学同事

受众: 预算课程的辅导教师

情境: 一堂以定义为主导的课程几乎没有利用学习者各不相同的经验。

GRASPS

goal: Design activities that connect budgeting content with relevant prior experience and meaningful application.

role: Teaching colleague

audience: The budgeting tutor

situation: A definition-led lesson makes little use of learners’ varied experience.

开放任务的产物与成功标准

包含三项活动的课程修改方案及简短理由说明

  • 指出每项活动所利用的相关已有经验
  • 说明每项活动如何将经验与新内容联系起来
  • 包含有意义的应用和解释
  • 运用对话，但不假定所有学习者拥有同一种经验

Open-task product and success criteria

A three-activity lesson revision with a brief justification

  • Identifies relevant prior experience for each activity
  • Shows how each activity connects experience to new content
  • Includes meaningful application and explanation
  • Uses dialogue without assuming all learners share one experience

包含一个开放性问题，即允许作出不止于简短固定答案的回答的问题，并留出对话时间。
Include an open question, meaning one allowing more than a brief fixed answer, and time for dialogue.
保留 / retain
学习者必须识别开放式解释和对话的机会。
Learners must recognize an opportunity for open explanation and dialogue.
O5
dependencies: C1, O1, O2, O3, O4
source: open_task		cognitive_operation
安排三项活动的顺序，使经验、联系、应用和解释相互支持。
Sequence the three activities so that experience, connection, application, and explanation support one another.
排除 / exclude
取消独立安排顺序的要求，以使这道入门题保持明确的范围。
Independent sequencing is removed to keep the introductory item bounded.
O6
dependencies: F1, C2, O1, O2, O5
source: open_task		cognitive_operation
评估每项联系对于所述学习者而言是否合理，同时不假定他们拥有同一种经验。
Evaluate whether each connection is plausible for the stated learners without assuming one shared experience.
保留 / retain
学习者必须在不假定存在同一种共同经验的情况下评估合理性。
Learners must evaluate plausibility without assuming one shared experience.

dependencies: K1, K2, K3, K4, O5, O6
source: open_task		cognitive_operation
说明每项活动如何利用已有经验来促进应用或理解。
Justify how each activity uses prior experience to support application or understanding.
保留 / retain
必须通过各项活动具体的教学目的来说明所选判断的理由。
The selected judgment must be justified through activity-specific instructional purposes.

步骤5：响应设计 / Stage 5: response design

题型理由

单项选择最能保留一个范围明确、以课程知识为依据的判断，同时允许存在若干合理的课程设计。选项应评估所提供的设计，而不应暗示永远只有一种顺序是有效的。

未保留的表现

  • 独立设计三项活动
  • 为每项活动独立确定一种经验或人物预算
  • 独立安排课程修改方案的顺序
  • 为辅导教师撰写完整的理由说明

Format rationale

Single choice best preserves one bounded, course-grounded judgment while allowing several legitimate lesson designs to remain possible. The options should evaluate the supplied design rather than imply that only one sequence can ever be valid.

Performance not retained

  • Independent production of three activities
  • Independent identification of an experience or profile for each activity
  • Independent sequencing of the lesson revision
  • Writing a full justification to the tutor
INC05判断题组 / Judgment matrix

审阅背景，不属于学生题面 / Reviewer context, not part of the student question

开放问题、思考时间与对话

让学习者有时间思考，并通过开放问题和对话说明理由。不能只依赖少数学生对封闭问题作答来判断全班理解。

本题保留考察：分别评价快速的回忆性提问是否给学习者提供了思考、解释和参与的机会，以及在未分享推理时，可见的两人讨论是否提供了充分的学习证据。

Open questions, thinking time and dialogue

Provide thinking time and opportunities to explain through open questions and dialogue. A few answers to closed questions do not establish whole-class understanding.

Retained judgment: Evaluate separately whether rapid recall questioning gives learners opportunities to think, explain and contribute, and whether visible pair discussion supplies adequate evidence of learning when reasoning is not shared.

来源 / Source: Week 3: Everyone can do something · 2. Everyone can do something: what does inclusive teaching look like?; 5. Inclusive teaching; 6. Challenges in inclusive teaching and learning

在一节30分钟的课上，Bello老师提出了六个回忆性问题——即要求简短事实性答案的问题。同样的三名自愿回答者立即作答。随后，她让学生两人一组解决一个相关但有难度的问题。大多数小组都在交谈，但活动在学习者解释其推理之前就结束了。判断每项评价为真或假。

p1同样的三名自愿回答者立即作答，只能提供有限的证据来表明全班有时间思考或能够参与。
p2因为全部六个回忆性问题都得到了回答，所以这种提问充分确定了全班的理解情况。
p3两人讨论表明学生有所参与，但在学习者没有解释其推理的情况下，这还不能确定他们从这项有难度的任务中学到了东西。
p4因为这个问题具有相关性，而且大多数小组都进行了讨论，所以他们的参与足以证明他们理解了这个问题。

整题完全匹配计分，不计部分分。

In a 30-minute lesson, Ms Bello asks six recall questions—questions seeking short factual answers. The same three volunteers answer immediately. She then gives pairs a relevant but demanding problem. Most pairs talk, but the activity ends before learners explain their reasoning. Mark each evaluation true or false.

p1Immediate answers from the same three volunteers provide limited evidence that the class had time to think or could contribute.
p2Because all six recall questions received answers, the questioning adequately established whole-class understanding.
p3Pair talk shows participation, but without learners explaining their reasoning it does not yet establish learning from the demanding task.
p4Because the problem was relevant and most pairs talked, their participation is sufficient evidence that they understood it.

Whole-item exact match; no partial credit.

参考答案与解释 / Reference answer and explanation

p1: 成立; p2: 不成立; p3: 成立; p4: 不成立

P1为真，因为只有三名自愿回答者立即作答，限制了可观察到的思考时间和参与范围。P2为假，因为几个简短的事实性答案并不能确定全班的理解情况。P3为真，因为必须结合任务难度和学习者推理中的证据来解读参与情况。P4为假，因为仅有相关且可见的活动并不足以证明学习已经发生。

p1: Supported; p2: Not supported; p3: Supported; p4: Not supported

P1 is true because immediate responses from only three volunteers restrict observable thinking time and contribution. P2 is false because several short factual answers do not establish whole-class understanding. P3 is true because participation must be interpreted alongside task demand and evidence in learners’ reasoning. P4 is false because relevant, visible activity alone is not sufficient evidence of learning.

五阶段设计记录 / Five-stage design record

步骤1：表现目标

根据提问或讨论方式为学习者提供的思考和解释机会，评价多种相关做法。

分别判断思考时间、开放式提问和对话，并提供谁实际能够参与的证据。不得假定一种普遍适用的理想教学顺序。

Stage 1: performance claim and observable evidence

Evaluate several questioning or discussion practices against the opportunities they give learners to think and explain.

Parallel judgments about thinking time, open questioning and dialogue, with evidence for who can actually contribute. Do not assume one universal ideal teaching sequence.

步骤2：情境与开放任务

在一节30分钟的课上，Bello老师提出六个回忆性问题，立即接受同样三名自愿回答者的答案，然后让学生两人一组解决一个相关但有难度的问题。大多数小组都在交谈，但她在要求学习者解释其推理之前就结束了活动。

作为Bello老师的同伴顾问，请为她写一份简短建议。评价这两种课堂做法是否提供了思考、解释和参与的机会。提出两项可行的改进，说明思考时间、开放性问题或对话会在何处发生，以及她应观察哪些学习者证据。

Stage 2: scenario and open task

In a 30-minute lesson, Ms Bello asks six recall questions, accepts answers from the same three volunteers immediately, and then assigns pairs a relevant but demanding problem. Most pairs talk, but she ends the activity before asking learners to explain their reasoning.

As Ms Bello’s peer adviser, write a brief recommendation for her. Evaluate both lesson practices for opportunities to think, explain and contribute. Propose two feasible changes, describing where thinking time, open questions or dialogue would occur and what learner evidence she should observe.

GRASPS

目标: 增加学习者思考和解释的机会。

角色: 同伴顾问

受众: Bello老师

情境: 一节课中出现了快速的封闭式提问和活跃的两人讨论，但理解证据有限。

GRASPS

goal: Improve opportunities for learners to think and explain.

role: Peer adviser

audience: Ms Bello

situation: A lesson shows quick closed questioning and active pair talk but limited evidence of understanding.

开放任务的产物与成功标准

一份有理据且包含两项可行改进的建议。

  • 使用明确的案例事实评价每种做法。
  • 说明谁可以参与以及如何参与。
  • 指出哪些解释或应用能够表明学习已经发生。

Open-task product and success criteria

A justified recommendation with two feasible changes.

  • Judges each practice using explicit case facts.
  • Specifies who can contribute and how.
  • Identifies explanations or application that would show learning.
