步骤5：响应设计 / Stage 5: response design

题型理由

真/假矩阵最能呈现这些并列判断。两到四个针对具体做法的命题既可以保持关于思考、解释和对话的主要知识处于活跃状态，也可以检验是否错误地将两人讨论视为学习的证明。

未保留的表现

  • 独立提出两项可行的课堂改进。
  • 说明所提改进在课堂中的具体位置。
  • 解释所提改进如何创造思考时间、对话或更广泛参与的机会。
  • 独立明确指出要观察的解释、成果或应用。
  • 撰写同伴顾问建议。

Format rationale

A true/false matrix best represents the parallel judgments. Two to four practice-specific propositions can keep the primary knowledge about thinking, explanation and dialogue active while also testing whether pair talk is mistakenly treated as proof of learning.

Performance not retained

  • Independent production of two feasible lesson changes.
  • Locating proposed changes within the lesson.
  • Explaining how proposed changes create thinking time, dialogue or broader contribution.
  • Independently specifying explanations, products or applications to observe.
  • Writing a peer-adviser recommendation.
INC06匹配 / Matching

审阅背景，不属于学生题面 / Reviewer context, not part of the student question

通过评价认可学习者的多种技能

包容性教学的评价应认可学习者的多种技能。评价活动需要让学生有机会展示目标技能，不能把单一的表达形式当成全部学习表现。

本题保留考察：将每种评估成果与它能够有效展示的历史表现相匹配，同时将共有的历史证据与非目标的作答形式品质区分开来。

Assessment that recognises a range of learner skills

Inclusive teaching uses assessment that gives credit for a range of skills. The response or performance should let learners demonstrate the intended skill rather than treating one narrow mode of expression as the whole of learning.

Retained judgment: Match each assessment product to the historical performance it can validly demonstrate, while separating shared history evidence from non-target response-mode qualities.

来源 / Source: Week 3: Everyone can do something · 2. Everyone can do something: what does inclusive teaching look like?; 5. Inclusive teaching; 6. Challenges in inclusive teaching and learning

一门历史课要求学生解释当地一次抗议活动造成的两项变化，并用课堂证据支持每一项变化。一名学习者写得慢，但在讨论中能清楚地解释；另一名学习者书写准确，但避免发言；第三名学习者能通过带标签的图示有效沟通。写作篇幅、发言自信和艺术质量属于非目标品质——即不在这一历史目标范围内的特征。请将每种成果与它能够如何展示所要求的学习成果相匹配。每个选项至多使用一次；有一个选项不使用。

备选项

c1两项书面解释，每项都与相关的课堂证据相联系。
c2两项口头解释，每项都与相关的课堂证据相联系。
c3用标签和关联来解释两项变化，每项都与相关的课堂证据相联系。
c4所选形式的精致程度：篇幅、表达自信或艺术质量。

待匹配内容

r1书面解释
r2录制的或现场的口头解释
r3带标签的视觉序列

整题完全匹配计分，不计部分分。 每个选项最多使用一次。

A history class must explain two changes caused by a local protest and support each with lesson evidence. One learner writes slowly but explains clearly in discussion; another writes accurately but avoids speaking; a third communicates effectively through labelled diagrams. Writing length, speaking confidence, and artistic quality are non-target qualities—features outside this history goal. Match each product to how it can show the required learning. Use each choice at most once; one choice is unused.

Choices

c1Two written explanations, each linked to relevant lesson evidence.
c2Two spoken explanations, each linked to relevant lesson evidence.
c3Labels and connections explaining two changes, each linked to relevant lesson evidence.
c4Polish in the chosen mode: length, confident delivery, or artistic quality.

Rows to match

r1Written explanation
r2Recorded or live oral explanation
r3Labelled visual sequence

Whole-item exact match; no partial credit. Use each option at most once.

参考答案与解释 / Reference answer and explanation

r1 → c1 (两项书面解释，每项都与相关的课堂证据相联系。); r2 → c2 (两项口头解释，每项都与相关的课堂证据相联系。); r3 → c3 (用标签和关联来解释两项变化，每项都与相关的课堂证据相联系。)

前三个匹配项表明每种成果如何传达两项变化，并将每项变化与课堂证据相联系。C4描述的是非目标品质，因此不能证明已达到所述历史目标。

r1 → c1 (Two written explanations, each linked to relevant lesson evidence.); r2 → c2 (Two spoken explanations, each linked to relevant lesson evidence.); r3 → c3 (Labels and connections explaining two changes, each linked to relevant lesson evidence.)

The first three matches show where each product communicates both changes and links each change to lesson evidence. C4 describes non-target qualities, so it does not demonstrate the stated history goal.

五阶段设计记录 / Five-stage design record

步骤1：表现目标

将评价机会与其能让学习者展示并获得认可的目标技能相匹配。

在明确的课程目标下，给出多个“表现方式—目标技能”对应关系，区分目标技能的证据与单一狭窄作答方式带来的无关要求。

Stage 1: performance claim and observable evidence

Match assessment opportunities to the target skills they allow learners to demonstrate and receive credit for.

Several performance-to-target-skill correspondences under explicit lesson goals, distinguishing evidence of the intended skill from irrelevant demands of one narrow response mode.

步骤2：情境与开放任务

一门历史课要求学生展示当地一次抗议活动如何改变了社区生活。目前的评估是一篇600词的文章。目标是解释两项变化，并用课堂证据支持每一项变化。一名学习者在讨论中能清楚地解释想法，但写得慢；另一名学习者书写准确，但避免发言；第三名学习者能通过带标签的图示有效沟通。

作为教师，请重新设计评估，使这些学习者能够展示目标技能并因这些技能获得认可，而不把某一种作答形式变成学习目标。请制定一份简要的评估计划，说明允许采用的成果形式、共同的证据要求，以及如何给予认可。说明每种成果如何展现预期技能，并指出一项你移除的不相关要求。在作出任何进一步调整之前，说明你会观察什么或询问什么。

Stage 2: scenario and open task

A history class must show how a local protest changed community life. The current assessment is a 600-word essay. The goal is to explain two changes and support each with lesson evidence. One learner explains ideas clearly in discussion but writes slowly; another writes accurately but avoids speaking; a third communicates effectively through labelled diagrams.

As the teacher, redesign the assessment so these learners can demonstrate and receive credit for the target skills without making one response mode the learning goal. Produce a brief assessment plan naming permitted products, common evidence requirements, and how credit will be awarded. Justify how each product reveals the intended skills and identify one irrelevant demand you removed. State what you would observe or ask before making any further adaptation.

GRASPS

目标: 创建一项与所述历史技能相一致的包容性评估。

角色: 历史教师

受众: 教学团队

情境: 单一的文章形式可能会掩盖某些学习者的历史理解。

GRASPS

goal: Create an inclusive assessment aligned with the stated history skills.

role: History teacher

audience: Teaching team

situation: A single essay format may conceal some learners' historical understanding.

开放任务的产物与成功标准

一份有理据支持的评估重新设计方案

  • 提供不止一种可行的作答形式。
  • 要求每种形式都使用课堂证据解释两项变化。
  • 说明每种形式如何为目标技能提供可观察的证据。
  • 将历史理解与特定形式的要求区分开来。
  • 基于观察或相关学习者信息进行进一步调整。

Open-task product and success criteria

A justified assessment redesign

  • Offers more than one feasible response mode.
  • Requires every mode to explain two changes using lesson evidence.
  • Shows how each mode provides observable evidence of the target skills.
  • Separates historical understanding from mode-specific demands.
  • Bases further adaptation on observation or relevant learner information.

步骤5：响应设计 / Stage 5: response design

题型理由

匹配要求进行若干项不同的对应，而不是作出一个总体认可，因此保留了所声明的证据粒度。它还允许不同的有效作答形式保持其正当性，前提是每种形式都能使共同的历史证据可被观察。

未保留的表现

  • 独立制定完整的评估重新设计方案。
  • 独立选择允许采用的成果形式。
  • 逐项对成果进行深入论证。
  • 在进一步调整前选择一项观察事项或针对学习者的问题。

Format rationale

Matching preserves the declared evidence granularity by requiring several distinct correspondences rather than one overall endorsement. It also allows different valid response modes to remain legitimate when each makes the common history evidence observable.

Performance not retained

  • Independent production of a complete assessment redesign.
  • Independent selection of permitted products.
  • Extended product-by-product justification.
  • Selection of an observation or learner question before further adaptation.
INC07多选 / Multiple select

审阅背景，不属于学生题面 / Reviewer context, not part of the student question

合作学习中的实质参与

同伴讨论和共同任务可以支持学习，但要关注每位学习者能否实际贡献，并获得说清和展示理解的机会。

本题保留考察：判断哪些拟议的合作安排能让每名具名学习者都在内容或推理方面作出有意义的贡献，并有机会展示个人理解。

Meaningful contribution in cooperative learning

Peer discussion and shared tasks can support learning, but check whether each learner can contribute meaningfully and demonstrate understanding.

Retained judgment: Judge which proposed cooperative arrangements give every named learner a meaningful content or reasoning contribution and an opportunity to demonstrate personal understanding.

来源 / Source: Week 3: Everyone can do something · 2. Everyone can do something: what does inclusive teaching look like?; 3. Observing active teaching

目前，Ana选择并解释用水实例；Ben只画图，不作解释；Chen记录小组想法；Devi因为角色在她加入前就已分配好而只能旁观。只有Ana展示共同完成的海报。有意义的贡献涉及内容或推理，而不只是看起来很忙。选择所有能让四名学习者都作出有意义的贡献，并为每名学习者的理解情况提供可观察证据的修订安排。

A每名学习者选择一个实例，通过口头表达、书写或绘画来呈现，并在小组合并这些实例前解释该实例如何体现用水。
BAna选择实例，Ben为其配图，Chen撰写说明文字，Devi添加颜色；然后由Ana代表小组解释完成的海报。
C每名学习者准备一个实例和相应解释；小组选择用于制作一张海报的实例，并由每名学习者解释自己的贡献。
D学习者轮流画图、誊写说明文字、计时和展示，但只有最后的展示者选择一个实例并解释它与用水的联系。

整题完全匹配计分，不计部分分。

Currently, Ana selects and explains water-use examples; Ben draws without explaining; Chen records group ideas; Devi watches because roles were assigned before she joined. Only Ana presents the shared poster. A meaningful contribution involves content or reasoning, not simply being busy. Select every revised arrangement that gives all four learners a meaningful contribution and observable evidence of each learner’s understanding.

AEach learner chooses one example, represents it through speech, writing, or drawing, and explains how it shows water use before the group combines the examples.
BAna chooses the examples, Ben illustrates them, Chen writes captions, and Devi adds colour; Ana then explains the finished poster for the group.
CEach learner prepares an example and explanation; the group selects examples for one poster, and every learner explains their own contribution.
DLearners rotate drawing, copying captions, timing, and presenting, but only the final presenter chooses an example and explains its connection to water use.

Whole-item exact match; no partial credit.

A (每名学习者选择一个实例，通过口头表达、书写或绘画来呈现，并在小组合并这些实例前解释该实例如何体现用水。); C (每名学习者准备一个实例和相应解释；小组选择用于制作一张海报的实例，并由每名学习者解释自己的贡献。)

A和C让每名学习者都能在内容或推理方面作出贡献，并提供其个人理解情况的可观察证据。B把内容选择和解释留给Ana。在D中，轮换活动让所有学习者都参与其中，但只有最后的展示者在内容或推理方面作出贡献并展示理解情况。

A (Each learner chooses one example, represents it through speech, writing, or drawing, and explains how it shows water use before the group combines the examples.); C (Each learner prepares an example and explanation; the group selects examples for one poster, and every learner explains their own contribution.)

A and C give every learner a content or reasoning contribution and observable evidence of personal understanding. B reserves content selection and explanation for Ana. In D, rotating activity involves all learners, but only the final presenter contributes content or reasoning and demonstrates understanding.

五阶段设计记录 / Five-stage design record

步骤1：表现目标

判断哪些合作学习安排能让每位学习者真正有机会作出贡献并展示理解。

在明确的参与条件下分别作出有依据的判断；避免仅因身处小组就认定实现了包容。

Stage 1: performance claim and observable evidence

Judge which cooperative-learning arrangements give each learner a genuine opportunity to contribute and show understanding.

Parallel warranted judgments under explicit participation conditions; avoid equating group membership alone with inclusion.

步骤2：情境与开放任务

在一节关于当地用水的课上，四名学习者共同完成一张海报。Ana选择实例并加以解释；Ben画图，但没有被要求解释；Chen记录小组的想法；Devi因为书写角色在她加入前就已分配好而只能旁观。教师只听到Ana进行展示。

为下一节课建议一种修订后的合作安排。解释该安排如何让每名学习者都作出有意义的贡献，并为每名学习者的理解情况提供证据。使用所述事实，并指出教师在任务期间应进行的一项观察。

Stage 2: scenario and open task

In a lesson on local water use, four learners share one poster. Ana selects examples and explains them; Ben draws but is not asked to explain; Chen records the group’s ideas; Devi watches because the writing role was assigned before she joined. The teacher hears only Ana present.

Recommend a revised cooperative arrangement for the next lesson. Explain how it gives every learner a meaningful contribution and produces evidence of each learner’s understanding. Use the stated facts, and identify one observation the teacher should make during the task.

GRASPS

目标: 改进共同任务，使每名学习者都能作出贡献并展示理解。

角色: 规划下一节课的教师

受众: 一名教学同事

情境: 目前的小组成员安排和活动没有提供平等的机会来解释学习所得。

GRASPS

goal: Improve the shared task so every learner can contribute and show understanding.

role: Teacher planning the next lesson

audience: A teaching colleague

situation: Current group membership and activity do not provide equal opportunities to explain learning.

开放任务的产物与成功标准

一份简明且有理据的修订方案

  • 为每名学习者分配或提供实质性贡献的机会
  • 包括每名学习者可观察的解释、成果或应用
  • 检查任务的适切性，而不是把分组本身当作学习证据

Open-task product and success criteria

A concise, justified revision plan

  • Assigns or enables a substantive contribution for each learner
  • Includes an observable explanation, product, or application from each learner
  • Checks task suitability rather than treating grouping as evidence of learning

步骤5：响应设计 / Stage 5: response design

题型理由

多项选择形式保留了平行判断，并允许存在不同的合理合作结构，而不会暗示只有一种强制性方案。

未保留的表现

  • 独立制定修订后的合作安排。
  • 撰写以课程为依据的解释。
  • 独立指出一项教师观察。
  • 明确判断任务难度或适切性。

Format rationale

Multiple-select preserves parallel judgments and allows different legitimate cooperative structures without implying one mandatory solution.

Performance not retained

  • Independently producing a revised cooperative arrangement.
  • Writing a course-grounded explanation.
  • Independently identifying a teacher observation.
  • Explicitly judging task difficulty or suitability.
INC08匹配 / Matching

审阅背景，不属于学生题面 / Reviewer context, not part of the student question

普遍课堂支持与额外支持

教师可以先采取许多所有人受益的课堂措施，并针对具体障碍提供额外支持；不能把所有改进都推迟到获得专门人员以后。

本题保留考察：将每项已陈述的障碍与可行的应对措施相匹配，同时区分一般课堂行动与对学习者的额外支持，并认识到在获得专业人员的支持或意见之前，教师主导的常规行动就可以开始。

General classroom support and additional learner support

Teachers can take classroom actions benefiting many learners and add support for specific barriers, while seeking specialist input where required. Useful classroom action need not all wait for specialists.

Retained judgment: Match each stated barrier with a feasible response while distinguishing general classroom action from additional learner support and recognising that ordinary teacher-led action can begin before specialist input.

来源 / Source: Week 3: Everyone can do something · 7. Tackling specific inclusivity challenges; 7.1. The Wave model in action

在一个大班中，几名学习者难以理解密集的书面说明。阿米娜看不清黑板上的小字。本常常迟到，因为他缺乏可靠的交通方式和学习材料。教师可以重新组织课程并咨询同事，但目前无法获得专业人员的支持或意见。不假定存在任何诊断或强制转介流程。请将每项需要与最站得住脚的应对措施匹配。额外支持是指针对某一特定学习者或障碍提供的帮助。有一个选项不使用；请勿重复使用选项。

备选项

C1将密集的说明改为简短步骤，并检查全班是否理解；立即开始。
C2维持当前安排，直到专业人员的建议明确哪些改变是适当的。
C3提供清晰可读的副本或合适的座位位置，并在有帮助时咨询同事。
C4提供所需材料，并允许采用支持性的迟到入班安排；立即开始。

待匹配内容

R1针对共同的说明障碍采取的一般课堂应对措施
R2针对阿米娜的视觉信息获取障碍提供的额外支持
R3针对本的情境障碍提供的额外支持

整题完全匹配计分，不计部分分。 每个选项最多使用一次。

In a large class, several learners struggle with dense written instructions. Amina cannot clearly see small board text. Ben often arrives late because he lacks reliable transport and materials. The teacher may reorganise lessons and consult colleagues, but specialist input is unavailable for now. No diagnosis or mandatory referral process is assumed. Match each need to the most defensible response. Additional support means help directed to a particular learner or barrier. One option is unused; do not reuse options.

Choices

C1Replace dense directions with short steps and check class understanding; begin now.
C2Keep current arrangements until specialist advice identifies which changes are appropriate.
C3Provide a readable copy or suitable position, while consulting colleagues if useful.
C4Provide needed materials and allow a supportive late-entry routine; begin now.

Rows to match

R1General classroom response to the shared instruction barrier
R2Additional support for Amina’s access barrier
R3Additional support for Ben’s contextual barriers

Whole-item exact match; no partial credit. Use each option at most once.

参考答案与解释 / Reference answer and explanation

R1 → C1 (将密集的说明改为简短步骤，并检查全班是否理解；立即开始。); R2 → C3 (提供清晰可读的副本或合适的座位位置，并在有帮助时咨询同事。); R3 → C4 (提供所需材料，并允许采用支持性的迟到入班安排；立即开始。)

C1是针对共同的密集说明理解困难所采取的一般行动。C3在不假定诊断的情况下，增加了与阿米娜的视觉信息获取障碍相匹配的支持。C4通过提供材料和灵活参与来应对本的资源和迟到障碍。C2被排除，因为它在等待专业人员建议期间，不必要地推迟了可行的课堂行动。

R1 → C1 (Replace dense directions with short steps and check class understanding; begin now.); R2 → C3 (Provide a readable copy or suitable position, while consulting colleagues if useful.); R3 → C4 (Provide needed materials and allow a supportive late-entry routine; begin now.)

C1 is a general action addressing the shared difficulty with dense instructions. C3 adds support matched to Amina’s visual-access barrier without assuming a diagnosis. C4 addresses Ben’s resource and lateness barriers through materials and flexible participation. C2 is rejected because it postpones feasible classroom action unnecessarily while awaiting specialist advice.

五阶段设计记录 / Five-stage design record

步骤1：表现目标

针对多个已明确识别的障碍，区分可行的一般课堂行动与额外的学习者支持。

给出多个“障碍—支持层级”对应关系，不强行虚构临床阈值，也不要求在采取普通课堂行动前等待专家。

Stage 1: performance claim and observable evidence

Distinguish feasible general classroom actions and additional learner support for several explicitly identified barriers.

Several barrier-to-level-of-support correspondences, without forcing invented clinical thresholds or waiting for specialists before ordinary classroom action.

步骤2：情境与开放任务

你在一个大班授课。几名学习者难以理解密集的书面说明；阿米娜看不清黑板上的小字；本常常迟到，因为他缺乏可靠的交通方式和学习材料。你可以重新组织课程并咨询同事，但无法立即获得专业人员的支持或意见。

撰写一份简短的支持计划，建议能惠及许多学习者的课堂行动，以及对阿米娜和本的额外支持。将每项行动与已识别的障碍联系起来，说明哪些行动可以立即开始，指出任何有用的同事意见或专业人员的支持或意见，并说明你会观察哪些情况来判断支持是否有效。

Stage 2: scenario and open task

You teach a large class. Several learners struggle with dense written instructions; Amina cannot clearly see small board text; and Ben often arrives late because he lacks reliable transport and materials. You can reorganise lessons and consult colleagues, but specialist input is not immediately available.

Write a brief support plan recommending classroom actions that benefit many learners and additional support for Amina and Ben. Link each action to an identified barrier, explain what can begin now, identify any useful colleague or specialist input, and state what you would observe to judge whether support is helping.

GRASPS

目标: 制定一份可行且与障碍相匹配的支持计划。

角色: 任课教师

受众: 你的教学团队

情境: 学习者面临读写能力、视觉信息获取、资源和迟到方面的障碍。

GRASPS

goal: Create a feasible, barrier-matched support plan.

role: Classroom teacher

audience: Your teaching team

situation: Learners face literacy, visual-access, resource and lateness barriers.

开放任务的产物与成功标准

一份简洁且有依据的支持计划

  • 将一般课堂行动与额外的个别支持区分开来。
  • 将行动与明确的障碍相匹配。
  • 不作不必要的拖延，开始有用的常规课堂行动。
  • 包括可观察的检查和适当的协作投入。

Open-task product and success criteria

A concise justified support plan

  • Separates general classroom actions from additional individual support.
  • Matches actions to explicit barriers.
  • Begins useful ordinary classroom action without unnecessary delay.
  • Includes observable checks and appropriate collaborative input.

步骤5：响应设计 / Stage 5: response design

题型理由

匹配题最能保留核心对应关系。使用三个障碍行和四个简洁的应对选项。将额外支持简要定义为针对某一特定学习者或障碍提供的帮助。选项应允许作出基于课程的判断，而不规定诊断、转介路径或措辞唯一的干预措施。

未保留的表现

  • 独立制定完整的支持计划。
  • 为每项行动撰写理由。
  • 提出用于复核效果的可观察指标。

Format rationale

Matching best preserves the focal correspondence. Use three barrier rows and four concise response options. Briefly define additional support as help directed to a particular learner or barrier. Options should permit course-grounded judgment without prescribing a diagnosis, referral pathway, or uniquely worded intervention.

Performance not retained

  • Independent production of a complete support plan.
  • Written justification of every action.
  • Generation of observable indicators for reviewing effects.
INC09匹配 / Matching

审阅背景，不属于学生题面 / Reviewer context, not part of the student question

学习障碍与可行支持的对应

低读写水平、视觉困难、资源不足等可能形成不同障碍。根据已知需要调整材料、参与方式或环境，并观察支持是否有效。

本题保留考察：将虚构案例中有证据支持的每一种不同参与障碍，与最能直接满足相应学习机会需求的可用课堂调整相匹配。

Matching participation barriers to feasible support

Different barriers, such as low literacy, visual access or limited resources, call for relevant changes to materials, participation or the environment, followed by observation of their effect.

Retained judgment: Match each distinct participation barrier evidenced in the fictional case to the available classroom change that most directly addresses that access need.

来源 / Source: Week 3: Everyone can do something · 7. Tackling specific inclusivity challenges

Mogas最近流离失所。教师可以提供共享材料、调整分组、私下交谈、表扬参与，并灵活处理迟到。推断每项观察所显示的主要障碍，然后将其与最能直接改善学习机会的可用调整相匹配。每个选项最多使用一次；无需作出诊断。

备选项

A在他所在小组的工作区提供共享材料。
B安排有组织的同伴支持：由同学按计划提供帮助。
C灵活执行迟到规定，并悄然帮助他重新投入任务。
D表扬他在课堂上的建设性参与。

待匹配内容

R1同学们开始书写时，Mogas看着他们，并在空荡荡的课桌上寻找用具。
R2老师当着全班对他说话时，他变得沉默；一名同学走近他后，他开始参与。
R3在小组活动开始后才进入教室时，他停留在门口，没有加入活动。

整题完全匹配计分，不计部分分。 每个选项最多使用一次。

Mogas was recently displaced. The teacher can provide shared materials, adjust grouping, speak privately, praise participation, and handle lateness flexibly. Infer the main barrier shown in each observation, then match it to the available change that most directly improves access. Use each option no more than once; no diagnosis is required.

Choices

AProvide shared materials at his group’s workspace.
BArrange structured peer support: planned help from classmates.
CApply the lateness rule flexibly and quietly reconnect him to the task.
DPraise his constructive participation during the lesson.

Rows to match

R1As classmates begin writing, Mogas watches them and searches the empty desk for equipment.
R2When addressed before the class, he becomes silent; after a classmate approaches him, he contributes.
R3Entering after group work has begun, he remains by the doorway instead of joining the activity.

Whole-item exact match; no partial credit. Use each option at most once.

参考答案与解释 / Reference answer and explanation

R1 → A (在他所在小组的工作区提供共享材料。); R2 → B (安排有组织的同伴支持：由同学按计划提供帮助。); R3 → C (灵活执行迟到规定，并悄然帮助他重新投入任务。)

R1把看着其他人开始活动和寻找用具结合在一起，表明存在资源获取障碍；共享材料最直接地解决这一问题。R2表明，公开受到关注会促使他退缩，而同伴邀请则使他能够参与，因此，有组织的同伴支持最直接地解决互动障碍。R3涉及迟到后难以加入正在进行的学习活动；灵活处理并悄然帮助他重新投入任务，能直接解决进入任务的问题。表扬通常可能有助于增强信心，但对这些观察所反映的问题而言不够直接。

R1 → A (Provide shared materials at his group’s workspace.); R2 → B (Arrange structured peer support: planned help from classmates.); R3 → C (Apply the lateness rule flexibly and quietly reconnect him to the task.)

R1 combines watching others begin with searching for equipment, indicating a resource-access barrier; shared materials address it most directly. R2 shows that public attention prompts withdrawal while peer invitation enables contribution, so structured peer support most directly addresses the interaction barrier. R3 concerns difficulty entering ongoing work after a late arrival; flexible handling and quiet reconnection directly address task entry. Praise may support confidence generally but is less direct for these observations.

五阶段设计记录 / Five-stage design record

步骤1：表现目标

将多个具体的参与障碍分别与材料、活动或环境方面的可行调整匹配。

给出多个“障碍—支持措施”对应关系；明确实际的无障碍需求，不假定需要专业诊断或存在一种通用便利措施。

Stage 1: performance claim and observable evidence

Match several specific participation barriers to feasible changes in material, activity or environment.

Several barrier-to-support correspondences; make the actual access need explicit so no specialist diagnosis or single universal accommodation is assumed.

步骤2：情境与开放任务

Mogas最近流离失所，缺少学习材料，而且经常迟到。他在被公开纠正时会退缩，但在同伴邀请时会加入活动。教师可以调整座位和分组、提供共享材料、在课堂上与他交谈、表扬建设性参与，并灵活执行迟到规定。同事可以帮助审查计划。

为Mogas撰写一份简短的两课时支持计划。确定他不同的参与障碍，针对每种障碍建议可行的课堂调整，并使用案例事实说明匹配的理由。说明你将观察什么，以确定学习机会和参与情况是否改善，以及如果某项障碍仍然存在，你可以如何与同事一起修订计划。

Stage 2: scenario and open task

Mogas has recently been displaced, lacks learning materials and often arrives late. He withdraws when corrected publicly but joins work when a peer invites him. The teacher can adjust seating and grouping, provide shared materials, speak with him during lessons, praise constructive participation and apply the lateness rule flexibly. Colleagues can help review the plan.

Write a brief two-lesson support plan for Mogas. Identify his distinct participation barriers, recommend feasible classroom changes for each, and justify the matches using the case facts. State what you will observe to determine whether access and participation improve, and how you might revise the plan with colleagues if a barrier remains.

GRASPS

目标: 将Mogas的具体参与障碍与可行支持相匹配，并审查其效果。

角色: 任课教师

受众: 教学同事

情境: 一名学习者的流离失所经历、资源有限、迟到和退缩正在限制其参与。

GRASPS

goal: Match Mogas’s specific participation barriers to feasible supports and review their effects.

role: Classroom teacher

audience: Teaching colleagues

situation: A learner’s displacement, limited resources, lateness and withdrawal are restricting participation.

开放任务的产物与成功标准

一份有理据的两课时支持与观察计划

  • 区分所述的资源、参与和迟到障碍，不推断诊断结果。
  • 将所选的每项支持与明确的案例证据联系起来。
  • 仅使用情境中可行的课堂调整。
  • 指出参与情况改善的可观察迹象，以及在支持无效时有理据的修订方案。

Open-task product and success criteria

A justified two-lesson support and observation plan

  • Separates the stated resource, participation and lateness barriers without inferring a diagnosis.
  • Links each selected support to explicit case evidence.
  • Uses only feasible classroom changes from the scenario.
  • Names observable signs of improved participation and a defensible revision if support is ineffective.

source: inclusive-week3-7-1-the-wave-model-in-action		taught_knowledge
有用的课堂行动可以由教师发起，而与同事合作可以支持进一步改进。
Useful classroom action can begin through the teacher while collaboration with colleagues can support further progress.
排除 / exclude
移除协作，以使简化任务聚焦于障碍与支持的匹配。
Collaboration is removed to focus the simplified task on barrier-support matching.

The material-access facts are needed to establish one barrier.

Mogas often arrives late.

The late-arrival fact establishes a distinct entry barrier.

Mogas withdraws when corrected publicly but joins work when invited by a peer.

The interaction facts establish another barrier without supplying the complete matching judgment.
S4
dependencies: none
source: scenario		scenario_fact
教师可以调整座位和分组、提供共享材料、在课堂上私下交谈、表扬建设性参与，并灵活执行迟到规定。
The teacher can adjust seating and grouping, provide shared materials, speak privately during lessons, praise constructive participation and apply the lateness rule flexibly.
提供 / provide

排除 / exclude
同事审查不属于简化判断的范围。
Colleague review is outside the simplified judgment.
C1
dependencies: K1, S1, S2, S3
source: open_task		cognitive_operation
仅使用明确的案例事实，区分资源、互动和迟到后进入活动的障碍。
Separate the resource, interaction and late-entry barriers using only explicit case facts.
保留 / retain
学习者必须将明确的案例事实区分为彼此不同且不涉及诊断的障碍。
Learners must separate explicit case facts into distinct, non-diagnostic barriers.
C2
dependencies: K2, K3, S4, C1
source: open_task		cognitive_operation
为每项障碍选择一项或多项可用的课堂调整。
Select one or more available classroom changes for each barrier.
保留 / retain
学习者必须为每项障碍选择一种可用的调整。
Learners must select an available change for each barrier.
C3
dependencies: S1, S2, S3, C2
source: open_task		cognitive_operation
通过解释每项所选支持如何满足对应案例中的具体学习机会需求，为其提供理由。
Justify each selected support by explaining how it addresses the corresponding case-specific access need.
排除 / exclude
移除独立的书面论证；所选对应关系即提供证据。
Independent written justification is removed; the selected correspondences provide the evidence.
P1
dependencies: C2
source: open_task		procedure
在两个课时中实施所选支持，并观察学习机会和参与情况中可观察到的变化。
Implement the selected supports across two lessons and look for observable changes in access and participation.
排除 / exclude
不评估在两个课时中的实施。
Implementation across two lessons is not assessed.
C4
dependencies: P1, S1, S2, S3
source: open_task		cognitive_operation
明确可观察的指标，例如获得材料、加入同伴活动、回应教师接触，或在迟到后保持参与。
Specify observable indicators such as obtaining materials, joining peer work, responding to teacher contact or remaining engaged after late arrival.
排除 / exclude
这项入门匹配任务不要求确定可观察的效果。
Identifying observable effects is removed from this introductory matching task.
C5
dependencies: K4, S5, C4
source: open_task		cognitive_operation
利用观察结果保留有效调整，或与同事一起修订无效的支持。
Use the observations to retain effective changes or revise an ineffective support with colleagues.
排除 / exclude
不评估依据观察结果修订支持。
Revising support from observations is not assessed.
X1
dependencies: S4, C1, C2
source: open_task, scenario		constraint
不要推断诊断结果，不要假定存在一种普遍适用的便利措施，也不要建议情境中不可用的支持。
Do not infer a diagnosis, assume one universal accommodation or recommend supports unavailable in the scenario.
保留 / retain
判断必须基于明确需求和可用支持，不能推断诊断结果或普遍适用的应对方式。
Judgments must use explicit needs and available supports without inferring a diagnosis or universal response.

步骤5：响应设计 / Stage 5: response design

题型理由

匹配最能体现保留的对应关系，并使主要知识保持有效。三项障碍描述与四到五个有明确范围的支持选项能够形成简洁且有理据的判断，同时通过仔细措辞各行和各选项，为合理的替代方案保留可能性。

未保留的表现

  • 独立制定一份两课时计划并为其提供理由。
  • 在书面论证中解释每项对应关系。
  • 实施支持并明确可观察的效果。
  • 与同事一起修订无效的支持。

Format rationale

Matching best represents the retained correspondence and keeps the primary knowledge active. Three barrier rows and four or five bounded support options permit a concise, defensible judgment while leaving legitimate alternatives possible through careful row and option wording.

Performance not retained

  • Independently producing and justifying a two-lesson plan.
  • Explaining each correspondence in a written rationale.
  • Implementing supports and specifying observable effects.
  • Revising ineffective support with colleagues.
INC10单选 / Single choice

审阅背景，不属于学生题面 / Reviewer context, not part of the student question

教师、同伴与同事的协作支持

教师可以通过观察、鼓励、同伴支持和灵活安排帮助学习者；与同事协作能够扩大支持，而不是将责任简单转交给他人。

本题保留考察：评估哪一种综合安排能最好地协调教师、同伴和同事的贡献，回应观察到的证据，包含审查和调整，并让教师负责决策和后续跟进。

Teacher, peer and colleague collaboration

Teachers can combine observation, encouragement, peer support and flexible arrangements. Working with colleagues can extend support without simply transferring responsibility.

Retained judgment: Evaluate which integrated arrangement best coordinates teacher, peer, and colleague contributions, responds to observed evidence, includes review and adjustment, and leaves decisions and follow-up with the teacher.

来源 / Source: Week 3: Everyone can do something · 7.1. The Wave model in action

Amina变得沉默寡言，并因家庭安排正在发生变化而每周迟到两次。她会漏看书面指示，但在有支持性的同学帮助时参与得很好。表扬和更简短的指示带来的进展并不稳定。一位同事可以观察一节课并讨论调整措施。同伴支持是指同学帮助她参与，而不是接管教学。以下哪种为期两周的安排最站得住脚？

A安排有支持性的同伴结对，同时由这位同事管理Amina的进展，并在两周后汇报。
B安排有支持性的同伴结对并提供易于获取和理解的指示。这位同事观察一次；教师审查Amina获取学习内容的情况和参与情况，讨论观察结果，并调整支持。
C继续采用结对和更简短的指示，不作改变，然后只审查Amina的迟到情况是否有所改善。
D用个人学习取代同伴支持，因为Amina的沉默寡言表明课堂学习内容对她来说太难。

整题完全匹配计分，不计部分分。

Amina has become quiet and is late twice weekly because family arrangements are changing. She misses written instructions but contributes well with supportive classmates. Praise and shorter instructions have produced uneven progress. A colleague can observe one lesson and discuss adjustments. Peer support means classmates helping participation, not taking over teaching. Which two-week arrangement is most defensible?

AUse supportive pairs, while the colleague manages Amina’s progress and reports back after two weeks.
BUse supportive pairs and accessible instructions. The colleague observes once; the teacher reviews Amina’s access and participation, discusses observations, and adjusts support.
CContinue pairing and shorter instructions unchanged, then review only whether Amina’s lateness has improved.
DReplace peer support with individual work because Amina’s quietness indicates that the classwork is too difficult.

Whole-item exact match; no partial credit.

参考答案与解释 / Reference answer and explanation

B (安排有支持性的同伴结对并提供易于获取和理解的指示。这位同事观察一次；教师审查Amina获取学习内容的情况和参与情况，讨论观察结果，并调整支持。)

B协调了有效的同伴支持、同事观察以及由教师主导的审查和调整。A将责任转交给了同事。C既没有灵活应对，也没有审查获取学习内容的情况和参与情况。D取消了一项已经有所帮助的支持，并根据沉默寡言作出了缺乏依据的推断。

B (Use supportive pairs and accessible instructions. The colleague observes once; the teacher reviews Amina’s access and participation, discusses observations, and adjusts support.)

B coordinates effective peer support, colleague observation, and teacher-led review and adjustment. A transfers responsibility to the colleague. C neither responds flexibly nor reviews access and participation. D removes a support that has helped and makes an unsupported inference from quietness.

五阶段设计记录 / Five-stage design record

步骤1：表现目标

评价一项连贯的支持安排，使教师、同伴和同事共同参与，同时保留责任归属。

依据已知案例条件，对协调支持作出一个综合判断；不得自动委派责任，也不得设置不合情理的冷漠干扰选项。

Stage 1: performance claim and observable evidence

Evaluate a coherent support arrangement combining teacher, peer and colleague contributions while retaining responsibility.

One integrated judgment about coordinated support under the known case conditions, not automatic delegation or an implausibly uncaring distractor.

步骤2：情境与开放任务

Amina最近变得沉默寡言，由于家庭安排发生变化，她每周迟到两次，并且会漏看书面指示。与有支持性的同学结对时，她参与得很好。她的教师表扬过她的想法，也尝试过使用更简短的指示，但进展并不稳定。一位教学同事可以协助观察一节课并讨论调整措施。

写一份简短的支持计划，建议教师、同伴和同事在接下来的两周内应如何协作。说明这种安排为何符合所述证据、每个人将做什么，以及教师将如何监测进展并继续承担责任。

Stage 2: scenario and open task

Amina has recently become quiet, arrives late twice a week because of changing family arrangements, and misses written instructions. She contributes well when paired with supportive classmates. Her teacher has praised her ideas and tried shorter instructions, but progress is uneven. A teaching colleague can help observe one lesson and discuss adjustments.

Write a brief support plan recommending how the teacher, peers and colleague should work together for the next two weeks. Explain why the arrangement fits the stated evidence, what each person will do, and how the teacher will monitor progress and retain responsibility.

GRASPS

目标: 设计一种协调一致的支持安排并说明理由。

角色: Amina的教师

受众: 你的教学团队

情境: 支持必须回应观察到的参与情况、迟到情况和获取指示的情况，同时不能臆断其原因。

GRASPS

goal: Design and justify a coordinated support arrangement.

role: Amina’s teacher

audience: Your teaching team

situation: Support must respond to observed participation, lateness and access to instructions without assuming their causes.

开放任务的产物与成功标准

一份包含职责和审查证据的两周支持计划。

  • 协调教师、同伴和同事各自不同的贡献
  • 运用观察、鼓励和灵活安排
  • 明确要审查的可观察变化
  • 让教师负责决策和后续跟进

Open-task product and success criteria

A two-week support plan with responsibilities and review evidence.

  • Coordinates distinct teacher, peer and colleague contributions
  • Uses observation, encouragement and flexible arrangements
  • Identifies observable changes to review
  • Keeps the teacher responsible for decisions and follow-up

步骤5：响应设计 / Stage 5: response design

题型理由

单项选择适合一个边界明确的综合决策。各选项可以在协调、假设、监测和责任方面有所不同，而不必将某一种确切的同伴或同事配置规定为普遍要求。

未保留的表现

  • 独立制定一份两周支持计划。
  • 自行分配并以书面形式说明每项贡献的理由。
  • 自行制定监测指标。
  • 单独评估表扬、基于优势的鼓励和信心培养。

Format rationale

Single choice fits one bounded integrated decision. Options can vary in coordination, assumptions, monitoring, and responsibility without making one exact peer or colleague configuration universally required.

Performance not retained

  • Independent production of a two-week support plan.
  • Original assignment and written justification of each contribution.
  • Generation of original monitoring indicators.
  • Separate evaluation of praise, strengths-based encouragement, and confidence-building.
